# T4 RULES

1. Scope: Template 4 = registry id 5, package mt/templates/t4/, config namespace cfg['t4']. Never change behaviour of ids 1-4. Generic files (render.py, draw.py, captions.py, ask.py, common.py) get only minimal guarded edits (if template_id == 5) or purely additive helpers.
2. Concept: the user's video is the BASE and fills the canvas. Everything else is an overlay layer. No opaque plate over the video centre unless the module is an explicit takeover (split, b-roll).
3. Canvases: reels 1080x1920 and youtube 1920x1080 (short side always 1080). Author layouts in pixels, safe margin 48 px. Every visual module must work and be checked on BOTH canvases.
4. Size: new files <= 500 lines, functions <= 80 lines, one responsibility per file, names t4_*.
5. Robustness: normalisers never raise; missing media -> gray placeholder + one t4_warn line; trigger phrase not found -> warn once and skip.
6. Arabic/RTL: use mt.fonts.shape and is_rtl_text; test every text module with Arabic and English strings.
7. Performance: static design goes to the foreground plate (cfg['_t2_fg']) or one sprite Item; cache plates; no per-frame Python drawing beyond Item animations.
8. Verification per task: (a) tools/mt_quick.py, tools/mt_t3_quick.py, tools/mt_t4_quick.py pass; (b) render --still on both canvases to out/t4/<task>_<canvas>.png, VIEW the images, compare with the cited R-image, fix once, re-check once, record remaining differences in T4_NOTES.md. Full --selftest only in P0, P22, P23.
9. Finish: append <= 12 lines to T4_NOTES.md (done / public functions / cfg keys / known gaps), git commit 'T4-Pn' if a repo exists. Do NOT start the next task. If running out of room, write RESUME HERE + the exact next step in T4_NOTES.md.

CLI: python -m mt.templates.t4.kit out.png
- Sprite convention: RGBA incl. glow margin; sprite.info["origin"] = where local (0,0) sits; kit_paste() aligns it. Masks = mt.draw masks.
- Icons: ICON_NAMES = code laptop phone gear globe database server cloud bulb chip shield. Reused _draw_icon (at 4x): code, phone, chip, shield. Own vectors: laptop gear database server cloud(+upload arrow) bulb globe (old globe clogged at 96 px). Unknown name -> t4_warn + empty icon. No cfg keys.

## P4 - T4 timing (assumed task number)
- DONE: t4/timing.py (pure, no drawing) + 9 unit checks 'T4-3' in tools/mt_t4_quick.py (31/31 PASS; only the new checks were run + the existing T4-1/2 ones).
- Public: t4_time(entry, cues, duration, default_hold) -> (start, end) | None; t4_stagger(n, step) -> [0, step, ...]; t4_ask_when(prompt, default_hold, key=None) -> {"start", "hold"} | {"trigger", "hold"}; t4_warn / t4_warn_reset re-exported from t4/warn.py (already existed since P2 - one message set, so no duplicate prints).
- Rules: start = trigger (find_trigger; cues = one cue list OR a list of streams, first stream with a hit wins) else `start`; end = `end` (must be > start) else start + `hold` else start + default_hold (None -> video end). start >= duration -> None; end clamped to duration; negative start -> 0; numbers or '0:45' / '3s' strings; junk never raises (one t4_warn, None).
- Trigger missing + start given -> start is used (one warning); missing and no start -> None (one warning). No cfg keys.
- GAPS: no offset / pre-roll option; windows shorter than 0.05 s are dropped; t4_ask_when is not wired into any module yet.

## P5 - T4 caption style 'pill' (assumed task number, ref R2; R9 for the reels height)
- DONE: t4/captions.py (~290 lines). t4_caption_style (registry hook, re-exported by t4/__init__) asks: style (1 Pill | 2 Plain = the classic ask_caption_style) -> font -> size -> text colour -> keyword colour (default #FFC21A) -> underline glow colour (#19D3FF) -> keywords -> word-by-word / phrases (+ words per group / max words) -> y% -> animation + duration. t4_build_items now returns t4_caption_items(cfg, duration).
- Stream keys: t4_style "pill", ref "canvas", font, size, color, hl_color, neon, keywords, mode words|phrase, words_per_group / max_words, y_pct (None = default), anim, anim_dur. No cfg["t4"] keys.
- Look: dark glass pill (alpha ~0.8, gradient), thin neon underline on the bottom edge (blue wide + cyan core, faded at both ends, built with kit.connector), white bold text, keyword = hl colour, x1.18 size + soft glow. Regular (non-bold) font -> thin same-colour stroke = faux bold. Pill radius ~0.36 size, <= 2 lines (shrinks to 50 % of the size, then stops).
- Geometry (px at short side 1080, scaled for other canvases): pill width <= 84 % of the canvas width; y_pct = the PILL'S BOTTOM EDGE in % of the canvas height, grows upward; defaults reels 84 (R9), youtube 91.5 (R2); never closer than 48 px to the canvas edge. Size default 56 (40 for 2nd+ streams).
- Reuse: stream_groups (parse_markup + keyword matching + grouping), t4_tokens = parse_markup + apply_keywords, visual_order + shape (word-level bidi, same as make_rich_sprite), kit.connector / kit_paste. make_rich_sprite itself is NOT used (one size per sprite, no glow).
- render.py: ONE guarded line (generic caption loop skips t4_style == "pill", next to t3_style). Other templates: same behaviour (T1 diff 0.0).
- Verified: 'T4-4' in tools/mt_t4_quick.py (12 checks: tokens, 84 % width + bottom edge + safe margin on both canvases for an Arabic and an English SRT, drawn once via build_items, keywords by list and *stars*, wrap / shrink, missing SRT / font / junk values warn once, no canvas, question flow pill and plain) -> 43/43. Stills (--still, Tajawal-Bold, real arabic-reshaper + python-bidi) on 1920x1080 and 1080x1920 for Arabic, English, mixed AR/EN and long 2-line cues, viewed: joined RTL letters, keyword on the correct end, balanced lines, pill inside 84 %.
- Bug caught by the checks: a '%' in the y-position question crashed the prompt (fixed: f-string).
- Regression: mt_quick T1 diff 0.0; T2 diff 0.074-0.114 - identical with the ORIGINAL render.py (A/B), so not from this task (env / earlier state). mt_t3_quick 49/50 (T3-6 = pre-existing, same as P1).
- ENV: pip works here -> real arabic-reshaper + python-bidi installed (no stubs needed for these runs). Test font Tajawal-Bold.ttf from google/fonts, kept outside the project (put Cairo / Tajawal Bold in fonts/ on your machine).
- GAPS: a mixed AR/EN line whose first word is Latin is laid out LTR (is_rtl_text rule, same as every other caption style); a 2nd pill stream needs its own y_pct (no automatic stacking); no word-by-word keyword pop; the underline is a fixed 80 % of the pill width.

Typical shift 12-100 px; no free place -> one warning, stays at the margin. Long text shrinks to a narrower panel when the frame is on.

Also accepted: one group dict or {on, groups}.

- Timing: tiles pop in one after another (t4_stagger), connector fades in after the last tile, everything fades out together at the group's end. Each tile = one cached sprite Item.
- Placement: anchored groups use the P8-frame-aware search of the name tag (ink >= 48 px from the edges, clear of the real frame plate); xy groups are only clamped to the safe area; connector points are clamped so glow + dot stay inside it. Two groups overlapping -> a warning.

- Remaining diffs vs R2: R2's tiles are slightly perspective-tilted with irregular offsets and a soft sheen; ours are a flat axis-aligned grid. R2's connector is an elbow with right angles; points are free-form (a polyline), so an elbow = three points. Test logos are generic shapes (no brand logos in the repo).
- GAPS: no per-tile delay / animation override; no tilt; one group per question run (more groups via the job file); tile text labels not supported; a connector is not frame-aware (it may cross the frame plate).

====================================================================================================
FILE: mt\templates\t4\scenes.py
====================================================================================================
"""mt.templates.t4.scenes - Template 4 module 'scenes' (refs R5 portrait / R6 landscape): infographic scenes built from WIDGETS placed in SLOTS.
cfg['t4']['scenes'] = [scene, ...]   (also accepted: one scene dict, or {on, scenes: [...]}; module default {'on': False} = none)
scene = {
    preset 'web_dev'         names the slot set (SLOTS) + connector lines (CONNECTORS); unknown -> one warning, 'web_dev'
    widgets [{type, slot, box, on, ...widget keys}]   type = a name from scenes_reg.WIDGETS; slot = a slot of the preset (default: the widget's
                             own default slot); box [x, y, w, h] (px at short side 1080) replaces the slot. Unknown type / slot -> one warning, skipped.
    start | trigger, end | hold (default 6 s)   timing via t4_time (spoken phrase must be in the captions)
    anim 'fade' (default; 'pop' / 'none' / any mt.anim name), anim_dur 0.45, stagger 0.2 (s between widgets)
    connectors None | [] | [{points, color, end_dot, width, glow}]   None = the preset's lines, [] = none }
SLOTS[preset]['portrait'|'landscape'] = {slot: (x, y, w, h)}  px at short side 1080 (canvas short side is always 1080); CONNECTORS[preset][aspect] = [line, ...]
with points = [x, y] px | 'slot' | 'slot-top|bottom|left|right' (the visible ink of the widget in that slot). A line is drawn only when every
slot it names holds a widget. Engine: widget fn(spec, box, ctx) -> sprite -> ONE Item per widget (staggered), connectors after the last widget, all fade
out together. Ink stays >= 48 px from the canvas edges (the sprite is moved inward when a widget draws outside the safe area; no frame avoidance yet).
Public: SLOTS, CONNECTORS, T4_SCENE, t4_scenes_cfg(raw) -> [scene], t4_scenes_items(cfg, duration), t4_ask_scenes(); registry: scenes_reg."""
import copy
import math

from mt.anim import ANIMS
from mt.ask import ask_anim
from mt.common import ask_choice, yn
from mt.draw import Item
from mt.templates.t4 import scenes_title  # noqa: F401  (registers the 'title_block' widget)
from mt.templates.t4 import scenes_widgets  # noqa: F401  (registers the 'code_window' widget)\
from mt.templates.t4 import scenes_ring
from mt.templates.t4 import scenes_bullets  # noqa: F401  (registers the 'bullet_list' widget)
from mt.templates.t4 import scenes_card  # noqa: F401  (registers the 'info_card' widget)
from mt.templates.t4 import scenes_charts  # noqa: F401  (registers 'bar_chart', 'progress_list', 'badge')
from mt.templates.t4.scenes_widgets import t4_widget_line_items
from mt.templates.t4.kit import connector
from mt.templates.t4.nameplate import T4_SAFE, _bool, _flt, _ink_box, _window
from mt.templates.t4.scenes_reg import WIDGET_ASK, WIDGET_SLOT, WIDGETS, get_widget
from mt.templates.t4.tiles import _make_item
from mt.templates.t4.timing import t4_ask_when, t4_stagger
from mt.templates.t4.warn import t4_warn

DEFAULT_PRESET = "web_dev"
PRESET_LABEL = {"web_dev": "Web development HUD (R5 / R6)"}
SLOTS = {"web_dev": {"portrait": {"title": (48, 60, 984, 220),        # R5: content centred on y 170; text area = 984 - 175 - 32
                                  "code": (48, 295, 434, 242),
                                  "ring": (637, 296, 240, 240),                                  
                                  "bullets": (48, 640, 984, 440),
                                  "chart": (64, 1310, 952, 300),
                                  "progress": (64, 1630, 952, 220),
                                  "badge": (684, 556, 316, 64),                                  
                                  "card": (48, 1100, 984, 190)},

                     "landscape": {"title": (48, 126, 780, 116),       # R6: content centred on y 184 (P15-P17 add their slots here)
                                   "code": (52, 316, 543, 289),
                                   "ring": (700, 300, 320, 320),
                                   "bullets": (1080, 270, 780, 420),
                                   "chart": (52, 640, 560, 392),
                                   "progress": (672, 650, 380, 380),
                                   "badge": (860, 150, 340, 64),
                                   "card": (1080, 720, 780, 170)}}}

CONNECTORS = {"web_dev": {"portrait": [], "landscape": []}}
T4_SCENE = {"on": True, "preset": DEFAULT_PRESET, "widgets": [], "start": 0.0, "trigger": "", "end": None, "hold": None, "anim": "fade",
            "anim_dur": 0.45, "stagger": 0.2, "connectors": None}
_LINE = {"points": [], "color": "#19d3ff", "end_dot": True, "width": 2.5, "glow": 9.0}
_SIDES = ("top", "bottom", "left", "right", "center")


# ---- config -----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
def _box(v):
    try:
        b = [float(x) for x in v]
        return b if len(b) == 4 and all(math.isfinite(x) for x in b) and b[2] > 0 and b[3] > 0 else None
    except (TypeError, ValueError):
        return None


def _widget(raw):
    """Clean widget entry (extras kept) or None."""
    if not isinstance(raw, dict):
        return None
    t = raw.get("type")
    t = t.strip().lower() if isinstance(t, str) else ""
    if not t:
        return None
    out = copy.deepcopy(raw)
    sl = raw.get("slot")
    out.update({"type": t, "slot": sl.strip().lower() if isinstance(sl, str) else "", "box": _box(raw.get("box")), "on": _bool(raw.get("on"), True)})
    return out


def _pt(p):
    """[x, y] numbers | 'slot' token -> kept; else None."""
    if isinstance(p, str):
        return p.strip().lower() or None
    try:
        x, y = float(p[0]), float(p[1])
        return [x, y] if len(p) == 2 and math.isfinite(x) and math.isfinite(y) else None
    except (TypeError, ValueError, IndexError):
        return None


def _lines(raw):
    """None -> None (= use the preset's); list -> clean lines (>= 2 points each); anything else -> None."""
    if not isinstance(raw, (list, tuple)):
        return None
    out = []
    for r in raw:
        if not isinstance(r, dict) or not isinstance(r.get("points"), (list, tuple)):
            continue
        ln = copy.deepcopy(_LINE)
        ln["points"] = [p for p in (_pt(q) for q in r["points"]) if p is not None][:12]
        c = r.get("color")
        ln["color"] = c if isinstance(c, str) and c.strip() else ln["color"]
        ed = r.get("end_dot")
        ln["end_dot"] = ed if ed in (True, False, "both", "start") else True
        ln["width"], ln["glow"] = _flt(r.get("width"), 0.5, 30, 2.5), _flt(r.get("glow"), 0, 60, 9.0)
        if len(ln["points"]) >= 2:
            out.append(ln)
    return out


def _scene(raw):
    out = copy.deepcopy(T4_SCENE)
    out.update({k: copy.deepcopy(v) for k, v in raw.items() if k not in out})                     # extras survive
    p = raw.get("preset")
    out["preset"] = p.strip().lower() if isinstance(p, str) and p.strip() else DEFAULT_PRESET
    ws = raw.get("widgets")
    ws = [ws] if isinstance(ws, dict) else ws if isinstance(ws, (list, tuple)) else []
    out["widgets"] = [w for w in (_widget(x) for x in ws) if w is not None]
    trig = raw.get("trigger")
    out["trigger"] = trig.strip() if isinstance(trig, str) else ""
    out["start"] = raw["start"] if raw.get("start") is not None else (None if out["trigger"] else 0.0)
    out["end"], out["hold"] = raw.get("end"), raw.get("hold")
    a = raw.get("anim")
    a = ("reveal" if a.strip().lower() == "typewriter" else a.strip().lower()) if isinstance(a, str) else "fade"
    out["anim"] = a if a in ANIMS or a in ("pop", "none") else "fade"
    out["anim_dur"], out["stagger"] = _flt(raw.get("anim_dur"), 0.0, 5.0, 0.45), _flt(raw.get("stagger"), 0.0, 3.0, 0.2)
    out["connectors"] = _lines(raw.get("connectors"))
    out["on"] = _bool(raw.get("on"), True)
    return out


def t4_scenes_cfg(raw):
    """Normalise cfg['t4']['scenes'] -> list of complete scenes. Accepts a list, one scene dict (has 'widgets') or {on, scenes: [...]}
    (the module default {'on': False} = no scenes). Never raises; junk entries are dropped."""
    try:
        if isinstance(raw, dict) and "widgets" in raw:
            raw = [raw]
        elif isinstance(raw, dict):
            raw = raw.get("scenes") if _bool(raw.get("on"), False) else []
        return [_scene(x) for x in raw if isinstance(x, dict)] if isinstance(raw, (list, tuple)) else []
    except Exception:
        return []


# ---- placement ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
def _aspect(W, H):
    return "portrait" if H > W else "landscape"


def _shift(a, b, lo, hi):
    """Move needed so [a, b] lies inside [lo, hi] (0 when it does, or when it is larger than the room)."""
    if b - a > hi - lo:
        return 0
    return int(math.ceil(lo - a)) if a < lo else int(math.floor(hi - b)) if b > hi else 0


def _put(sprite, box, W, H, s):
    """Sprite whose origin (info['origin']) = the box's top-left -> (cx, cy of the Item, ink rect on the canvas, moved px) | None (empty sprite)."""
    ink = sprite.info.get("ink") or _ink_box(sprite)    
    if ink is None:
        return None
    ox, oy = sprite.info.get("origin", (0, 0))
    x0, y0, m = int(round(box[0])) - ox, int(round(box[1])) - oy, T4_SAFE * s
    dx = _shift(x0 + ink[0], x0 + ink[2], m, W - m)
    dy = _shift(y0 + ink[1], y0 + ink[3], m, H - m)
    x0, y0 = x0 + dx, y0 + dy
    return x0 + sprite.width / 2.0, y0 + sprite.height / 2.0, (x0 + ink[0], y0 + ink[1], x0 + ink[2], y0 + ink[3]), abs(dx) + abs(dy)


def _warn_overlaps(placed):
    for i, (a, ra) in enumerate(placed):
        for b, rb in placed[i + 1:]:
            if ra[0] < rb[2] and rb[0] < ra[2] and ra[1] < rb[3] and rb[1] < ra[3]:
                t4_warn("%s and %s overlap - give one of them another slot / box." % (a, b))


def _widget_items(cfg, scene, label, win, ctx, slots, rects, placed):
    """One staggered Item per widget; fills rects[slot or 'wN'] with the placed ink rect. -> (items, start of the last item)."""
    W, H, s = ctx["W"], ctx["H"], ctx["s"]
    ready = []
    for i, w in enumerate(scene["widgets"]):
        if not w["on"]:
            continue
        fn, slot = get_widget(w["type"]), (w["slot"] or WIDGET_SLOT.get(w["type"], ""))
        if fn is None:
            t4_warn("%s: unknown widget type '%s' - skipped (known: %s)." % (label, w["type"], ", ".join(sorted(WIDGETS)) or "none"))
            continue
        box = w["box"] or slots.get(slot)
        if box is None:
            t4_warn("%s: widget '%s' has no slot '%s' in preset '%s' (%s) and no box - skipped." % (label, w["type"], slot, ctx["preset"], ctx["aspect"]))
            continue
        box = tuple(float(v) * s for v in box)
        try:
            sp = fn(dict(w), box, ctx)
            pl = _put(sp, box, W, H, s) if sp is not None else None
        except Exception as e:
            t4_warn("%s: widget '%s' could not be drawn (%s: %s) - skipped." % (label, w["type"], type(e).__name__, e))
            continue
        if pl is None:
            continue
        key = slot if slot and slot not in rects else "w%d" % (i + 1)
        rects[key] = pl[2]
        placed.append(("%s widget '%s'" % (label, key), pl[2]))
        ready.append((sp, pl))
    steps, last = t4_stagger(len(ready), scene["stagger"]), max(win[0], win[1] - 0.1)
    items = [_make_item(sp, min(win[0] + d, last), win[1], pl[0], pl[1], scene) for (sp, pl), d in zip(ready, steps) if not sp.info.get("lines_only")]    
    for (sp, pl), d in zip(ready, steps):                                   # typewriter widgets: one 'reveal' Item per code line
        items += t4_widget_line_items(sp, pl[0], pl[1], min(win[0] + d, last), win[1], 0.0 if sp.info.get("lines_only") else scene["anim_dur"])
    return items, (min(win[0] + steps[-1], last) if steps else win[0])


# ---- connectors --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
def _resolve(p, rects, s):
    """[x, y] px | 'slot' | 'slot-side' -> canvas point, or None when that slot has no widget."""
    if not isinstance(p, str):
        return float(p[0]) * s, float(p[1]) * s
    name, side = p, "center"
    head, _, tail = p.rpartition("-")
    if head and tail in _SIDES:
        name, side = head, tail
    r = rects.get(name)
    if r is None:
        return None
    cx, cy = (r[0] + r[2]) / 2.0, (r[1] + r[3]) / 2.0
    return {"left": (r[0], cy), "right": (r[2], cy), "top": (cx, r[1]), "bottom": (cx, r[3])}.get(side, (cx, cy))


def _connector_items(scene, ctx, rects, t0, end):
    lines = scene["connectors"] if scene["connectors"] is not None else _lines(CONNECTORS.get(ctx["preset"], {}).get(ctx["aspect"], [])) or []
    W, H, s, out = ctx["W"], ctx["H"], ctx["s"], []
    for ln in lines:
        pts = [_resolve(p, rects, s) for p in ln["points"]]
        if any(p is None for p in pts):
            continue                                                       # a slot it needs holds no widget: no line
        m = (T4_SAFE + ln["glow"] * 1.6 + 2) * s
        pts = [(min(max(x, m), W - m), min(max(y, m), H - m)) for x, y in pts]
        sp = connector(pts, ln["color"], ln["end_dot"], ln["width"] * s, ln["glow"] * s)
        ox, oy = sp.info["origin"]
        out.append(Item(sp, min(t0 + scene["anim_dur"], max(t0, end - 0.1)), end, -ox + sp.width / 2.0, -oy + sp.height / 2.0, "fade", scene["anim_dur"], out=True))
    return out


def t4_scenes_items(cfg, duration):
    """Items of every scene: one per widget (staggered), then the preset's connectors; all fade out together. Never raises."""
    t4 = cfg.get("t4") if isinstance(cfg, dict) and isinstance(cfg.get("t4"), dict) else {}
    scenes = [sc for sc in t4_scenes_cfg(t4.get("scenes")) if sc["on"]]
    if not scenes:
        return []
    try:
        W, H = int(cfg["canvas"][0]), int(cfg["canvas"][1])
    except (KeyError, TypeError, ValueError, IndexError):
        t4_warn("t4 scenes: the job has no canvas - skipped.")
        return []
    items, placed = [], []
    for i, sc in enumerate(scenes):
        label = "scene %d" % (i + 1)
        try:
            items += _one_scene(cfg, sc, label, duration, W, H, placed)
        except Exception as e:
            t4_warn("t4 %s: could not be drawn (%s: %s) - skipped." % (label, type(e).__name__, e))
    _warn_overlaps(placed)
    return items


def _one_scene(cfg, sc, label, duration, W, H, placed):
    if not sc["widgets"]:
        t4_warn("%s: no widgets - skipped." % label)
        return []
    win = _window(cfg, sc, label, duration, 6.0)
    if win is None:
        return []
    preset = sc["preset"]
    if preset not in SLOTS:
        t4_warn("%s: unknown preset '%s' - using '%s' (known: %s)." % (label, preset, DEFAULT_PRESET, ", ".join(sorted(SLOTS))))
        preset = DEFAULT_PRESET
    ctx = {"W": W, "H": H, "s": min(W, H) / 1080.0, "aspect": _aspect(W, H), "preset": preset, "cfg": cfg}
    rects = {}
    items, t_last = _widget_items(cfg, sc, label, win, ctx, SLOTS[preset][ctx["aspect"]], rects, placed)
    return items + (_connector_items(sc, ctx, rects, t_last, win[1]) if items else [])


# ---- questions --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
def t4_ask_scenes():
    """Question group 'scenes' (called by t4_ask_config). -> a list with ONE scene, or {'on': False}. More scenes: edit the job file."""
    if not yn("Add an infographic scene (title block + more widgets in later tasks - reference R5 / R6)?", default=False):
        return {"on": False}
    names = sorted(SLOTS)
    p = ask_choice("Scene layout", [PRESET_LABEL.get(n, n) for n in names], default=1, key="t4_scene_preset")
    widgets = []
    for name in WIDGETS:
        if name in WIDGET_ASK and yn("Add the '%s' widget?" % name, default=True):
            widgets.append(dict(WIDGET_ASK[name](), type=name, slot=WIDGET_SLOT.get(name, "")))
    sc = {"preset": names[p - 1], "widgets": widgets}
    sc.update(t4_ask_when("Scene", 6.0, key="t4_scene_when"))
    sc["anim"] = ask_anim("Scene animation (widgets appear one after another)", default="fade", key="t4_scene_anim")
    return [sc]

====================================================================================================
FILE: mt\templates\t4\scenes_reg.py
====================================================================================================
"""mt.templates.t4.scenes_reg - Template 4 'scenes': the widget registry (no drawing, no scene logic).
A widget is a function  fn(spec, box, ctx) -> RGBA sprite | None  where
    spec  the widget's dict from the scene (type, slot, box, + the widget's own keys)
    box   (x, y, w, h) in canvas px = the slot (already scaled: slots are authored at short side 1080)
    ctx   {'W', 'H', 's' (short side / 1080), 'aspect' 'portrait'|'landscape', 'preset', 'cfg'}
The sprite must carry sprite.info['origin'] = (ox, oy): where the BOX's top-left sits inside the sprite (glow margin = origin > 0).
The engine (scenes.py) puts that point on the box's top-left, keeps the visible ink inside the 48 px safe area and builds the Item.
Public: WIDGETS, WIDGET_ASK, WIDGET_SLOT, register_widget(name, fn, ask=None, slot=None), get_widget(name).
  ask  = optional question function () -> dict of widget keys (used by t4_ask_scenes); slot = the widget's default slot name."""
from mt.templates.t4.warn import t4_warn

WIDGETS = {}        # name -> fn
WIDGET_ASK = {}     # name -> ask()
WIDGET_SLOT = {}    # name -> default slot name


def register_widget(name, fn, ask=None, slot=None):
    """Add (or replace) a widget type. -> True, or False (one t4_warn) for an empty name / non-callable. Never raises."""
    key = name.strip().lower() if isinstance(name, str) else ""
    if not key or not callable(fn):
        t4_warn("scenes: register_widget(%r) needs a name and a callable - ignored." % (name,))
        return False
    WIDGETS[key] = fn
    for table, v in ((WIDGET_ASK, ask if callable(ask) else None), (WIDGET_SLOT, slot.strip().lower() if isinstance(slot, str) and slot.strip() else None)):
        if v is None:
            table.pop(key, None)
        else:
            table[key] = v
    return True


def get_widget(name):
    """-> the widget function, or None (unknown type / not a string)."""
    return WIDGETS.get(name.strip().lower()) if isinstance(name, str) else None

====================================================================================================
FILE: mt\templates\t4\cfg.py
====================================================================================================
"""mt.templates.t4.cfg - Template 4 "Overlay Studio": config defaults + normaliser (cfg["t4"]).
The user's video is the BASE and fills the canvas; every module below is an overlay layer (placeholders until their P-task)."""
import copy
import re

CANVAS_REELS = [1080, 1920]
CANVAS_YOUTUBE = [1920, 1080]
T4_MODULES = ("frame", "nameplate", "corner_list", "tags", "tiles", "cards", "scenes", "split", "broll")
T4_DEFAULTS = {
    "canvas_mode": "auto",                                   # 'auto' (by the video aspect) | 'reels' | 'youtube'
    "fit": "fill",                                           # 'fill' (crop to the canvas) | 'fit' (whole video, letterboxed)
    "backdrop": {"type": "color", "value": "#000000", "path": "", "strength": 5},   # type color|image|blur; only visible when fit leaves bars
    **{m: {"on": False} for m in T4_MODULES},
}
_HEX = re.compile(r"^#?([0-9a-fA-F]{6}|[0-9a-fA-F]{3})$")


def t4_canvas(mode, video_info=None):
    """-> [1080, 1920] (reels) or [1920, 1080] (youtube). 'auto' = by the video aspect (wider than tall -> youtube; no info -> reels)."""
    m = str(mode or "auto").strip().lower()
    if m == "youtube":
        return list(CANVAS_YOUTUBE)
    if m == "reels":
        return list(CANVAS_REELS)
    try:
        return list(CANVAS_YOUTUBE) if float(video_info["w"]) > float(video_info["h"]) else list(CANVAS_REELS)
    except (TypeError, KeyError, ValueError):
        return list(CANVAS_REELS)


def _hex(v, default):
    m = _HEX.match(str(v).strip()) if isinstance(v, str) else None
    if not m:
        return default
    h = m.group(1)
    h = "".join(c * 2 for c in h) if len(h) == 3 else h
    return "#" + h.lower()


def _choice(v, allowed, default):
    v = str(v).strip().lower() if isinstance(v, str) else ""
    return v if v in allowed else default


def t4_cfg(raw):
    """Normalise cfg["t4"]: always returns a complete dict, never raises (junk values fall back to the defaults).
    Module dicts keep their extra keys (later P-tasks own them); only `on` is coerced to bool."""
    out = copy.deepcopy(T4_DEFAULTS)
    if not isinstance(raw, dict):
        return out
    try:
        out["canvas_mode"] = _choice(raw.get("canvas_mode"), ("auto", "reels", "youtube"), out["canvas_mode"])
        out["fit"] = _choice(raw.get("fit"), ("fill", "fit"), out["fit"])
        bd = raw.get("backdrop")
        if isinstance(bd, dict):
            b = out["backdrop"]
            b["type"] = _choice(bd.get("type"), ("color", "image", "blur"), b["type"])
            b["value"] = _hex(bd.get("value"), b["value"])
            b["path"] = bd["path"].strip() if isinstance(bd.get("path"), str) else ""
            try:
                b["strength"] = max(1, min(10, int(bd.get("strength", b["strength"]))))
            except (TypeError, ValueError, OverflowError):
                pass
        for m in T4_MODULES:
            src = raw.get(m)
            if isinstance(src, dict):
                d = copy.deepcopy(src)
                d["on"] = bool(src.get("on", False))
                out[m] = d
            elif m in ("tiles", "cards", "scenes") and isinstance(src, list):          # tiles / cards = a LIST (t4/tiles.py, t4/cards.py normalise it)
                out[m] = copy.deepcopy(src)
        for k, v in raw.items():                         # unknown keys survive (forward compatibility with newer jobs)
            if k not in out:
                out[k] = copy.deepcopy(v)
    except Exception:
        return copy.deepcopy(T4_DEFAULTS)
    return out


====================================================================================================
FILE: mt\templates\t4\base.py
====================================================================================================
"""mt.templates.t4.base - Template 4: the BASE video's backdrop (what shows around a 'fit' video): solid colour, image, or a blurred
copy of the video itself. Fill mode needs no backdrop (the video covers the canvas), so image / blur are only built for fit."""
import os

from PIL import Image, ImageFilter

from mt.templates.t4.warn import t4_warn

BLUR_DIV = 8                       # the blur is made at 1/8 size (fast, smooth) and scaled back up
_BLACK = {"type": "color", "value": "#000000"}


def t4_blur_params(strength, W, H):
    """strength 1..10 -> {W, H, sw, sh, lr}: lr = box-blur radius at the 1/8 size (clamped so ffmpeg's chroma limit is never hit)."""
    sw, sh = max(4, int(round(W / float(BLUR_DIV)))), max(4, int(round(H / float(BLUR_DIV))))
    try:
        s = max(1, min(10, int(strength)))
    except (TypeError, ValueError):
        s = 5
    return {"W": int(W), "H": int(H), "sw": sw, "sh": sh, "lr": max(1, min(int(round(s * 1.2)), min(sw, sh) // 4 - 1))}


def t4_blur_filter(p):
    """ffmpeg filter chain turning the source copy [rb] into the blurred backdrop: cover-scale+crop at 1/8 size, boxblur, scale up."""
    return (f"scale={p['sw']}:{p['sh']}:force_original_aspect_ratio=increase,crop={p['sw']}:{p['sh']},"
            f"boxblur={p['lr']}:2,scale={p['W']}:{p['H']}:flags=bicubic,setsar=1,format=yuv420p")


def t4_blur_still(frame, W, H, p):
    """PIL twin of t4_blur_filter for --still: blurred cover copy of `frame` (RGB) at W x H, RGBA."""
    s = max(W / float(frame.width), H / float(frame.height))
    fr = frame.convert("RGB").resize((max(W, int(round(frame.width * s))), max(H, int(round(frame.height * s)))), Image.BILINEAR)
    l, t = (fr.width - W) // 2, (fr.height - H) // 2
    small = fr.crop((l, t, l + W, t + H)).resize((p["sw"], p["sh"]), Image.LANCZOS)
    small = small.filter(ImageFilter.BoxBlur(p["lr"])).filter(ImageFilter.BoxBlur(p["lr"]))
    return small.resize((W, H), Image.BICUBIC).convert("RGBA")


def _image_ok(path):
    try:
        if path and os.path.isfile(str(path)):
            with Image.open(str(path)) as im:
                im.verify()
            return True
    except Exception:
        pass
    return False


def t4_apply_backdrop(cfg, t4):
    """Set cfg['background'] (+ cfg['_t4_blur'] for the blur type) from the normalised t4 dict. Never raises: a missing / broken
    image falls back to the solid colour with one t4_warn line."""
    bd, W, H = t4["backdrop"], cfg["canvas"][0], cfg["canvas"][1]
    cfg.pop("_t4_blur", None)
    cfg["background"] = {"type": "color", "value": bd["value"]}
    if t4["fit"] != "fit":
        return
    if bd["type"] == "image":
        if _image_ok(bd["path"]):
            cfg["background"] = {"type": "image", "path": str(bd["path"])}
        else:
            t4_warn(f"backdrop image '{os.path.basename(str(bd['path'])) or '(none)'}' not found or unreadable - using the solid colour")
    elif bd["type"] == "blur":
        cfg["_t4_blur"] = t4_blur_params(bd["strength"], W, H)


====================================================================================================
FILE: mt\templates\t4\kit.py
====================================================================================================
"""mt.templates.t4.kit - Template 4 neon / glass primitives (look of R1 / R5 / R10).
Masks are mt.draw masks ((L image, (ox, oy))). Every sprite function returns an RGBA image whose glow margin is included;
sprite.info["origin"] = (ox, oy) is where the shape's local (0, 0) sits inside the image -> use kit_paste(base, sprite, x, y).
Public: PALETTE, chamfer_rect, neon_stroke, glass_panel, connector, hex_badge, hazard_stripes, icon, neon_icon, kit_paste, kit_sheet.
All shapes are built at 4x and shrunk (crisp edges); sprites never raise on bad input."""
import math

from PIL import Image, ImageChops, ImageDraw, ImageFilter

from mt.draw import (_t2_alpha_paste, t2_darken, t2_draw_mask, t2_glow, t2_grad, t2_lighten, t2_line_mask, t2_paint,
                     t2_poly_mask, t2_rgb, t2_rrect_mask)
from mt.templates.t4.kit_icons import ICON_NAMES, SS, icon_mask, soft_erode

PALETTE = {"cyan": (25, 211, 255), "blue": (30, 107, 255), "yellow": (255, 194, 26),
           "glass": (6, 16, 40, 199), "white": (255, 255, 255)}      # glass = rgba(6,16,40,0.78)


# ---- small internals ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
def _tag(im, origin):
    im.info["origin"] = (int(origin[0]), int(origin[1]))
    return im


def _origin(sp):
    return sp.info.get("origin", (0, 0))


def _shift(mask, dx, dy):
    return mask[0], (mask[1][0] + int(dx), mask[1][1] + int(dy))


def kit_paste(base, sprite, x=0, y=0):
    """Alpha-composite a kit sprite so that its local (0, 0) lands on canvas (x, y). Clips at the canvas edge."""
    ox, oy = _origin(sprite)
    _t2_alpha_paste(base, sprite, int(round(x)) - ox, int(round(y)) - oy)
    return base


def _pad_for(glow):
    return int(max(0.0, glow) * 2.6) + 4


def _edge_bands(mask, specs, pad):
    """Inner edge bands of a filled mask: specs = [(d0, d1)] (px inside the edge) -> ([L sprite-size], (W, H), origin)."""
    m, (ox, oy) = mask
    W, H = m.width + 2 * pad, m.height + 2 * pad
    cv = Image.new("L", (W, H), 0)
    cv.paste(m, (pad, pad))
    big = cv.resize((W * SS, H * SS), Image.BICUBIC)
    cache = {0: big}

    def E(d):
        if d not in cache:
            cache[d] = soft_erode(big, d * SS)
        return cache[d]
    out = [ImageChops.subtract(E(a), E(b)).resize((W, H), Image.BOX) for a, b in specs]
    return out, (W, H), (pad - ox, pad - oy)


def _halo(band, sigma, peak, width):
    """Gaussian halo of a thin band whose peak alpha is `peak` (gain compensates the band's thinness)."""
    gain = peak * sigma * 2.5066 / max(1.0, width)
    return t2_glow((band, (0, 0)), sigma, gain)


def _neon(size, band, core, color, glow, width):
    """glow (wide + tight) + saturated band + near-white core on a transparent sprite of `size`."""
    sp = Image.new("RGBA", size, (0, 0, 0, 0))
    c = t2_rgb(color)
    if glow > 0:
        t2_paint(sp, _halo(band, glow, 0.42, width), c)
        t2_paint(sp, _halo(band, max(1.0, glow / 3.0), 0.55, width), c)
    t2_paint(sp, (band, (0, 0)), c)
    if core is not None:
        t2_paint(sp, (core, (0, 0)), t2_lighten(c, 0.78))
    return sp


# ---- shapes ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
def chamfer_rect(size, cut):
    """Mask of a w x h rectangle with cut corners. cut = px for all corners or (tl, tr, br, bl). Local origin = top-left."""
    w, h = float(size[0]), float(size[1])
    cs = list(cut) if isinstance(cut, (tuple, list)) and len(cut) == 4 else [cut] * 4
    tl, tr, br, bl = [max(0.0, min(float(v or 0), w / 2.0, h / 2.0)) for v in cs]
    pts = [(tl, 0), (w - tr, 0), (w, tr), (w, h - br), (w - br, h), (bl, h), (0, h - bl), (0, tl)]
    clean = [p for i, p in enumerate(pts) if p != pts[i - 1]]
    return t2_poly_mask(clean)


def neon_stroke(mask, color=None, width=2.0, glow=10):
    """Neon outline of a filled mask: glow + coloured band (inside the edge) + bright core. -> RGBA sprite."""
    color = PALETTE["cyan"] if color is None else color
    bw = max(1.0, float(width))
    bands, size, org = _edge_bands(mask, [(0, bw), (0.3 * bw, 0.7 * bw)], _pad_for(glow))
    return _tag(_neon(size, bands[0], bands[1], color, glow, bw), org)


# ---- panels ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
def _glass_sprite(mask, tint, color, bw, glow):
    """Shared by glass_panel / hex_badge: gradient dark-glass fill + inner edge glow + sheen + neon border."""
    c = t2_rgb(color)
    bands, size, org = _edge_bands(mask, [(0, bw), (0.3 * bw, 0.7 * bw), (0, 11)], _pad_for(glow))
    sp = Image.new("RGBA", size, (0, 0, 0, 0))
    a = tint[3] if len(tint) > 3 else 199
    rgb = t2_rgb(tint)
    sm = _shift(mask, org[0], org[1])
    t2_paint(sp, sm, t2_grad([(0, t2_lighten(rgb, 0.10) + (a,)), (0.55, rgb + (a,)), (1, t2_darken(rgb, 0.25) + (a,))], 90))
    t2_paint(sp, sm, t2_grad([(0, (190, 230, 255, 34)), (0.35, (190, 230, 255, 0))], 90))
    inner = bands[2].filter(ImageFilter.GaussianBlur(3.5)).point(lambda v: min(255, int(v * 1.5)))
    t2_paint(sp, (inner, (0, 0)), c, 0.30)
    stroke = _neon(size, bands[0], bands[1], c, glow, bw)
    sp.alpha_composite(stroke)
    return _tag(sp, org)


def glass_panel(size, radius=18, tint=None, border=None, cut=0, border_w=2.0, glow=10):
    """Dark-glass HUD panel (rgba(6,16,40,0.78) by default) with a neon border. cut > 0 / (tl,tr,br,bl) -> chamfered corners."""
    w, h = max(8, int(size[0])), max(8, int(size[1]))
    mask = chamfer_rect((w, h), cut) if cut else t2_rrect_mask((0, 0, w, h), radius)
    return _glass_sprite(mask, tint or PALETTE["glass"], PALETTE["cyan"] if border is None else border, border_w, glow)


def _hex_pts(cx, cy, R):
    return [(cx + R * math.cos(math.radians(60 * i)), cy + R * math.sin(math.radians(60 * i))) for i in range(6)]


def hex_badge(size, icon_name="code", color=None, glow=10):
    """Hexagon badge (pointy left / right, as in R1 / R10): glass fill, neon border, thin inner ring, glowing icon. size = width."""
    color = PALETTE["cyan"] if color is None else color
    R = max(8.0, float(size)) / 2.0
    cx, cy = R, R * 0.866
    bw = max(2.0, R * 0.07)
    sp = _glass_sprite(t2_poly_mask(_hex_pts(cx, cy, R), round_r=R * 0.07), PALETTE["glass"], color, bw, glow)
    ring = t2_poly_mask(_hex_pts(cx, cy, R * 0.80), round_r=R * 0.05)
    rb, _, ro = _edge_bands(ring, [(0, max(1.0, bw * 0.45))], 2)
    ox, oy = _origin(sp)
    t2_paint(sp, (rb[0], (ox - ro[0], oy - ro[1])), t2_rgb(color), 0.45)
    if icon_name:
        isz = int(R * 1.05)
        ic = neon_icon(icon_name, isz, t2_lighten(t2_rgb(color), 0.35), glow=max(3.0, R * 0.16))
        kit_paste(sp, ic, cx - isz / 2.0 + ox, cy - isz / 2.0 + oy)
    return sp


# ---- connectors / stripes ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
def _disc(cx, cy, r):
    def fn(d, L, s):
        a, b = L(cx - r, cy - r), L(cx + r, cy + r)
        d.ellipse((a[0], a[1], b[0] - 1, b[1] - 1), fill=255)
    return t2_draw_mask((cx - r, cy - r, cx + r, cy + r), fn)


def connector(points, color=None, end_dot=True, width=2.5, glow=9):
    """Thin neon line through `points` (local px) with round joints; end_dot True | 'both' | 'start' | False. Local = given coords."""
    color = PALETTE["cyan"] if color is None else color
    try:
        pts = [(float(x), float(y)) for x, y in points]
    except (TypeError, ValueError):
        pts = []
    if len(pts) < 2:
        return _tag(Image.new("RGBA", (2, 2), (0, 0, 0, 0)), (0, 0))
    c, dr = t2_rgb(color), max(3.0, width * 2.2)
    pad = _pad_for(glow) + int(dr)
    minx, miny = min(p[0] for p in pts), min(p[1] for p in pts)
    ox, oy = int(math.floor(-minx)) + pad, int(math.floor(-miny)) + pad
    size = (int(math.ceil(max(p[0] for p in pts) - minx)) + 2 * pad, int(math.ceil(max(p[1] for p in pts) - miny)) + 2 * pad)
    sp = Image.new("RGBA", size, (0, 0, 0, 0))
    line = _shift(t2_line_mask(pts, width), ox, oy)
    core = _shift(t2_line_mask(pts, max(1.0, width * 0.4)), ox, oy)
    t2_paint(sp, _shift(t2_glow(line, glow, 0.42 * glow * 2.5066 / width), 0, 0), c)
    t2_paint(sp, _shift(t2_glow(line, max(1.0, glow / 3.0), 0.55 * max(1.0, glow / 3.0) * 2.5066 / width), 0, 0), c)
    t2_paint(sp, line, c)
    t2_paint(sp, core, t2_lighten(c, 0.78))
    ends = [pts[-1]] if end_dot is True else [pts[0], pts[-1]] if end_dot == "both" else [pts[0]] if end_dot == "start" else []
    for ex, ey in ends:
        d = _shift(_disc(ex, ey, dr), ox, oy)
        t2_paint(sp, t2_glow(d, dr * 1.2, 1.1), c, 0.7)
        t2_paint(sp, d, c)
        t2_paint(sp, _shift(_disc(ex, ey, dr * 0.45), ox, oy), t2_lighten(c, 0.85))
    return _tag(sp, (ox, oy))


def hazard_stripes(size, angle=30.0, color=None, glow=6, stripe=None, gap=None):
    """Slanted dash bar like the yellow / blue '////' marks in R1 / R10. angle = lean from vertical (+ = '/'). Local origin = top-left."""
    color = PALETTE["yellow"] if color is None else color
    w, h = max(4, int(size[0])), max(4, int(size[1]))
    sw = float(stripe or h * 0.45)
    off = h * math.tan(math.radians(max(-70.0, min(70.0, angle))))
    gp = float(gap if gap is not None else sw * 0.62)
    n = max(1, int((w - abs(off) + gp) // (sw + gp)))
    if n > 1:
        gp = (w - abs(off) - n * sw) / (n - 1)                    # whole dashes only, evenly spread, no clipped ends
    else:
        sw = max(2.0, w - abs(off))

    def fn(d, L, s):
        for k in range(n):
            x = k * (sw + gp) + (0 if off >= 0 else -off)
            q = [(x + off, 0), (x + off + sw, 0), (x + sw, h), (x, h)]
            d.polygon([L(px, py) for px, py in q], fill=255)
    mask = t2_draw_mask((0, 0, w, h), fn, pad=0)
    pad = _pad_for(glow)
    sp = Image.new("RGBA", (w + 2 * pad, h + 2 * pad), (0, 0, 0, 0))
    m = _shift(mask, pad, pad)
    if glow > 0:
        t2_paint(sp, t2_glow(m, glow, 1.0), t2_rgb(color), 0.55)
    t2_paint(sp, m, t2_rgb(color))
    return _tag(sp, (pad, pad))


# ---- icons ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
def icon(name, size, color=None):
    """Flat anti-aliased icon (size x size RGBA) in `color` (default white). Names: kit_icons.ICON_NAMES."""
    color = PALETTE["white"] if color is None else color
    m = icon_mask(name, size)
    sp = Image.new("RGBA", m.size, (0, 0, 0, 0))
    t2_paint(sp, (m, (0, 0)), t2_rgb(color))
    return _tag(sp, (0, 0))


def neon_icon(name, size, color=None, glow=None):
    """Icon with a soft neon halo + near-white core (local (0, 0) = icon's top-left)."""
    color = PALETTE["cyan"] if color is None else color
    size = max(4, int(size))
    glow = size * 0.12 if glow is None else glow
    pad = _pad_for(glow)
    m = icon_mask(name, size)
    sp = Image.new("RGBA", (size + 2 * pad, size + 2 * pad), (0, 0, 0, 0))
    mk = (m, (pad, pad))
    c = t2_rgb(color)
    if glow > 0:
        t2_paint(sp, t2_glow(mk, glow, 1.5), c, 0.75)
    t2_paint(sp, mk, c)
    t2_paint(sp, mk, t2_lighten(c, 0.55), 0.45)
    return _tag(sp, (pad, pad))


# ---- contact sheet ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
def _sheet_bg(W, H):
    bg = Image.new("RGBA", (W, H), (4, 9, 24, 255))
    bg.alpha_composite(t2_grad([(0, (3, 8, 22, 255)), (1, (9, 24, 58, 255))], 90)((W, H), (0, 0)))
    d = ImageDraw.Draw(bg)
    for x in range(0, W, 60):
        d.line([(x, 0), (x, H)], fill=(40, 90, 150, 22))
    for y in range(0, H, 60):
        d.line([(0, y), (W, y)], fill=(40, 90, 150, 22))
    return bg


def _label(base, xy, text, size=20, fill=(150, 195, 230)):
    from mt.fonts import default_font, load_font
    try:
        f = load_font(default_font(), size)
    except Exception:
        from PIL import ImageFont
        f = ImageFont.load_default()
    ImageDraw.Draw(base).text(xy, text, font=f, fill=fill)


def _sec_palette(bg, y):
    _label(bg, (40, y), "PALETTE  (cyan / blue / yellow / glass over a video-like patch / white)")
    patch = t2_grad([(0, (170, 130, 105, 255)), (1, (55, 85, 120, 255))], 20)((330, 90), (0, 0))
    for i, k in enumerate(("cyan", "blue", "yellow", "white")):
        x = 40 + i * 120
        t2_paint(bg, t2_rrect_mask((x, y + 36, x + 100, y + 126), 10), PALETTE[k])
        _label(bg, (x, y + 132), k, 16)
    bg.paste(patch, (540, y + 36))
    kit_paste(bg, glass_panel((200, 90), 14, glow=0, border=PALETTE["blue"]), 600, y + 36)
    _label(bg, (600, y + 132), "glass 0.78 on video", 16)


def _sec_strokes(bg, y):
    _label(bg, (40, y), "neon_stroke  /  chamfer_rect  (cut 14, glow 6 / 10 / 18, widths 1.5 / 2 / 3.5)")
    for i, (g, w, col) in enumerate(((6, 1.5, "cyan"), (10, 2.0, "cyan"), (18, 3.5, "blue"), (10, 2.0, "yellow"))):
        m = chamfer_rect((250, 110), (14, 14, 14, 14) if i != 3 else (30, 0, 30, 0))
        kit_paste(bg, neon_stroke(m, PALETTE[col], w, g), 60 + i * 300, y + 50)
    m = t2_rrect_mask((0, 0, 110, 110), 55)
    kit_paste(bg, neon_stroke(m, PALETTE["cyan"], 2.5, 12), 1290, y + 50)


def _sec_panels(bg, y):
    _label(bg, (40, y), "glass_panel  (rounded r=18 | R1 name card cuts | R10 name tag cuts)  over a video-like patch")
    bg.paste(t2_grad([(0, (190, 150, 120, 255)), (0.5, (110, 130, 150, 255)), (1, (50, 70, 110, 255))], 25)((1540, 190), (0, 0)), (40, y + 34))
    kit_paste(bg, glass_panel((420, 150), 18), 70, y + 54)
    kit_paste(bg, glass_panel((520, 150), 0, cut=(0, 36, 0, 36), border=PALETTE["cyan"], border_w=2.5, glow=12), 560, y + 54)
    kit_paste(bg, glass_panel((420, 90), 0, cut=(0, 22, 0, 22), border_w=2), 1130, y + 54)
    kit_paste(bg, glass_panel((200, 60), 12, border=PALETTE["yellow"], glow=8), 1130, y + 160)


def _sec_conn(bg, y):
    _label(bg, (40, y), "connector  (end_dot True / both / False, elbow + diagonal)")
    kit_paste(bg, connector([(60, y + 80), (260, y + 80), (330, y + 40), (520, y + 40)], PALETTE["cyan"]))
    kit_paste(bg, connector([(600, y + 40), (700, y + 100), (900, y + 100)], PALETTE["cyan"], "both"))
    kit_paste(bg, connector([(980, y + 90), (1300, y + 90)], PALETTE["blue"], False, 3, 12))
    kit_paste(bg, connector([(1380, y + 100), (1480, y + 40), (1560, y + 40)], PALETTE["yellow"], True, 2, 7))


def _sec_badges(bg, y):
    _label(bg, (40, y), "hex_badge  (code / gear / globe)   hazard_stripes  (yellow 30 deg, blue 30 deg, yellow -30 deg, thin)")
    for i, nm in enumerate(("code", "gear", "globe")):
        kit_paste(bg, hex_badge(130, nm), 60 + i * 190, y + 40)
    kit_paste(bg, hazard_stripes((110, 26), 30), 700, y + 70)
    kit_paste(bg, hazard_stripes((110, 26), 30, PALETTE["blue"]), 860, y + 70)
    kit_paste(bg, hazard_stripes((110, 26), -30), 1020, y + 70)
    kit_paste(bg, hazard_stripes((300, 12), 45, PALETTE["cyan"], 4, stripe=14, gap=9), 1180, y + 77)


def _sec_icons(bg, y):
    _label(bg, (40, y), "icon / neon_icon  (96 px, then 48 / 32 / 24 px flat for jaggies)")
    for i, nm in enumerate(ICON_NAMES):
        x = 40 + i * 140
        kit_paste(bg, neon_icon(nm, 96, PALETTE["cyan"]), x, y + 34)
        for j, s in enumerate((48, 32, 24)):
            kit_paste(bg, icon(nm, s, PALETTE["white"]), x + (0, 52, 90)[j], y + 150)
        _label(bg, (x, y + 190), nm, 15)


def kit_sheet(out_png):
    """Contact sheet of every primitive on a dark background -> out_png (returns the path)."""
    W, H = 1700, 1300
    bg = _sheet_bg(W, H)
    for fn, y in ((_sec_palette, 20), (_sec_strokes, 200), (_sec_panels, 400), (_sec_conn, 680), (_sec_badges, 820),
                  (_sec_icons, 1030)):
        fn(bg, y)
    _label(bg, (40, 1265), "kit_sheet - mt/templates/t4/kit.py", 18, (90, 130, 170))
    import os
    os.makedirs(os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(out_png)), exist_ok=True)
    bg.convert("RGB").save(out_png)
    return out_png


if __name__ == "__main__":
    import sys
    print(kit_sheet(sys.argv[1] if len(sys.argv) > 1 else "out/t4/kit_sheet.png"))


====================================================================================================
FILE: mt\templates\t4\kit_icons.py
====================================================================================================
"""mt.templates.t4.kit_icons - Template 4 vector icons + the soft-erode helper shared with kit.py.
Every icon is drawn at 4x on an 'L' canvas and shrunk with LANCZOS (no jagged edges), then tinted by kit.icon().
Reused from mt.draw._draw_icon (drawn at 4x): code, phone, chip, shield. New here: laptop, gear, database, server,
cloud (upload arrow, as in R5), bulb, globe (lighter than the old one: its crowded strokes clogged at 96 px). Unknown names never raise: one t4_warn line + an empty icon."""
import math

from PIL import Image, ImageChops, ImageDraw, ImageFilter

from mt.draw import _draw_icon
from mt.templates.t4.warn import t4_warn

SS = 4
ICON_NAMES = ("code", "laptop", "phone", "gear", "globe", "database", "server", "cloud", "bulb", "chip", "shield")
_REUSED = ("code", "phone", "chip", "shield")


def soft_erode(L, d):
    """Coverage ('L') of the shape in L shrunk by d px - isotropic and anti-aliased (gaussian blur + soft threshold)."""
    if d <= 0:
        return L
    sigma = max(1.2, d / 1.1)
    z = d / sigma
    p = 0.5 * (1.0 + math.erf(z / math.sqrt(2.0)))
    slope = math.exp(-z * z / 2.0) / math.sqrt(2.0 * math.pi) / sigma          # d(blur)/d(px) at the edge
    b = L.filter(ImageFilter.GaussianBlur(sigma))
    return b.point(lambda v: int(255 * min(1.0, max(0.0, 0.5 + (v / 255.0 - p) / slope))))


class _Pen:
    """Unit-square pen on a big 'L' canvas: coordinates 0..1, stroke width w (unit), round caps / joints."""

    def __init__(self, S, w=0.075):
        self.S = S
        self.W = max(2, int(round(w * S)))
        self.im = Image.new("L", (S, S), 0)
        self.d = ImageDraw.Draw(self.im)

    def P(self, pts):
        return [(x * self.S, y * self.S) for x, y in pts]

    def line(self, pts, closed=False):
        P = self.P(pts + ([pts[0]] if closed else []))
        self.d.line(P, fill=255, width=self.W, joint="curve")
        r = self.W / 2.0
        for x, y in P:
            self.d.ellipse((x - r, y - r, x + r, y + r), fill=255)

    def dot(self, cx, cy, r, fill=255):
        S = self.S
        self.d.ellipse(((cx - r) * S, (cy - r) * S, (cx + r) * S, (cy + r) * S), fill=fill)

    def ring(self, cx, cy, r):
        S = self.S
        self.d.ellipse(((cx - r) * S, (cy - r) * S, (cx + r) * S, (cy + r) * S), outline=255, width=self.W)

    def rrect(self, box, rad):
        self.d.rounded_rectangle([v * self.S for v in box], radius=rad * self.S, outline=255, width=self.W)

    def arc(self, box, a0, a1):
        self.d.arc([v * self.S for v in box], a0, a1, fill=255, width=self.W)

    def fill_union(self, circles, rects):
        S = self.S
        for cx, cy, r in circles:
            self.d.ellipse(((cx - r) * S, (cy - r) * S, (cx + r) * S, (cy + r) * S), fill=255)
        for x0, y0, x1, y1 in rects:
            self.d.rectangle((x0 * S, y0 * S, x1 * S, y1 * S), fill=255)


def _arc_pts(cx, cy, r, a0, a1, n=48):
    return [(cx + r * math.cos(math.radians(a0 + (a1 - a0) * i / n)),
             cy + r * math.sin(math.radians(a0 + (a1 - a0) * i / n))) for i in range(n + 1)]


def _laptop(S):
    p = _Pen(S, 0.07)
    p.rrect((0.20, 0.20, 0.80, 0.64), 0.04)
    p.line([(0.08, 0.77), (0.92, 0.77)])
    p.line([(0.40, 0.77), (0.44, 0.71), (0.56, 0.71), (0.60, 0.77)])
    return p.im


def _gear(S):
    p = _Pen(S, 0.07)
    n, R, r = 8, 0.43, 0.32
    per = 2 * math.pi / n
    pts = []
    for k in range(n):
        c = k * per
        for rr, off in ((r, -0.30), (R, -0.17), (R, 0.17), (r, 0.30)):
            a = c + off * per
            pts.append((0.5 + rr * math.cos(a), 0.5 + rr * math.sin(a)))
    p.line(pts, closed=True)
    p.ring(0.5, 0.5, 0.13)
    return p.im


def _database(S):
    p = _Pen(S, 0.07)
    p.d.ellipse((0.20 * S, 0.12 * S, 0.80 * S, 0.30 * S), outline=255, width=p.W)
    p.line([(0.20, 0.21), (0.20, 0.79)])
    p.line([(0.80, 0.21), (0.80, 0.79)])
    for top in (0.32, 0.52, 0.70):
        p.arc((0.20, top, 0.80, top + 0.18), 0, 180)
    return p.im


def _server(S):
    p = _Pen(S, 0.06)
    for y0 in (0.12, 0.39, 0.66):
        p.rrect((0.12, y0, 0.88, y0 + 0.22), 0.04)
        p.dot(0.27, y0 + 0.11, 0.032)
        p.dot(0.39, y0 + 0.11, 0.032)
        p.line([(0.62, y0 + 0.11), (0.76, y0 + 0.11)])
    return p.im


def _cloud(S):
    p = _Pen(S, 0.075)
    big = Image.new("L", (S, S), 0)
    q = _Pen(S)
    q.im, q.d = big, ImageDraw.Draw(big)
    q.fill_union([(0.27, 0.60, 0.17), (0.47, 0.42, 0.22), (0.72, 0.58, 0.18)], [(0.27, 0.58, 0.72, 0.77)])
    ring = ImageChops.subtract(big, soft_erode(big, p.W))
    p.im = ring
    p.d = ImageDraw.Draw(ring)
    p.line([(0.49, 0.66), (0.49, 0.42)])
    p.line([(0.40, 0.50), (0.49, 0.41), (0.58, 0.50)])
    return p.im


def _bulb(S):
    p = _Pen(S, 0.07)
    arc = _arc_pts(0.5, 0.40, 0.27, 125, 415)
    p.line(arc + [(0.59, 0.73), (0.41, 0.73)], closed=True)
    p.line([(0.42, 0.85), (0.58, 0.85)])
    p.line([(0.43, 0.56), (0.5, 0.47), (0.57, 0.56)])
    p.line([(0.5, 0.47), (0.5, 0.73)])
    return p.im


def _globe(S):
    p = _Pen(S, 0.06)
    p.ring(0.5, 0.5, 0.40)
    p.d.ellipse((0.33 * S, 0.10 * S, 0.67 * S, 0.90 * S), outline=255, width=p.W)
    p.line([(0.10, 0.5), (0.90, 0.5)])
    p.line([(0.5, 0.10), (0.5, 0.90)])
    p.line([(0.17, 0.30), (0.83, 0.30)])
    p.line([(0.17, 0.70), (0.83, 0.70)])
    return p.im


_NEW = {"globe": _globe, "laptop": _laptop, "gear": _gear, "database": _database, "server": _server, "cloud": _cloud, "bulb": _bulb}


def icon_mask(name, size):
    """White-on-black 'L' mask (size x size) of icon `name`, anti-aliased. Unknown name -> empty mask + one warning."""
    size = max(4, int(round(size)))
    S = size * SS
    if name in _NEW:
        big = _NEW[name](S)
    elif name in _REUSED:
        rgba = Image.new("RGBA", (S, S), (0, 0, 0, 0))
        _draw_icon(rgba, name, (0, 0, S, S))
        big = rgba.getchannel("A")
    else:
        t4_warn("t4 icon: unknown name %r (known: %s)" % (name, ", ".join(ICON_NAMES)))
        big = Image.new("L", (S, S), 0)
    return big.resize((size, size), Image.LANCZOS)


====================================================================================================
FILE: mt\templates\t4\frame.py
====================================================================================================
"""mt.templates.t4.frame - Template 4 module 'frame' (ref R10, the frame only): a full-canvas neon HUD frame drawn as ONE transparent
foreground plate (the video underneath is never touched). Parts: thin rails, thick double-line corner brackets (TL / TR / BL / BR),
side tick marks, a small neon icon top-right, and a bottom tab (dark glass trapezoid on the bottom rail) with centred TRACKED text
and '////' hazard stripes at both ends of it.
Config cfg['t4']['frame'] = {on, text, color, icon, show_ticks} (+ optional 'font' = a font file; default = the project's default font).
  text   '' (or '-') = no tab and no stripes (rails + brackets only); Arabic / RTL text is drawn untracked (tracking would break joining).
  icon   a kit icon name (code laptop phone gear globe database server cloud bulb chip shield) or '' / 'none' = no icon.
Public: T4_FRAME, t4_frame_cfg(raw), t4_ask_frame(), t4_frame_plate(W, H, fcfg) -> RGBA W x H (cached), t4_frame_part(W, H, t4) -> plate | None
(the part builder registered in t4/__init__.T4_FG_PARTS). Authored in px at short side 1080 (scaled for other sizes); rails sit 30 px
from the edge (decoration), the tab text stays well inside the 48 px safe margin."""
import os

from PIL import Image, ImageDraw

from mt.common import ask, ask_choice, c_color, yn
from mt.draw import t2_glow, t2_lighten, t2_paint, t2_poly_mask, t2_rgb
from mt.fonts import default_font, is_rtl_text, load_font, shape
from mt.templates.t4.cfg import _hex
from mt.templates.t4.kit import _glass_sprite, connector, hazard_stripes, kit_paste, neon_icon
from mt.templates.t4.kit_icons import ICON_NAMES
from mt.templates.t4.warn import t4_warn

T4_FRAME = {"on": False, "text": "CODE â€¢ BUILD â€¢ INSPIRE", "color": "#19D3FF", "icon": "code", "show_ticks": True}
INSET = 30                                                    # rail distance from the canvas edge (px at short side 1080)
ARMS = {"tl": (78, 100), "tr": (90, 140), "bl": (70, 84), "br": (70, 84)}      # bracket arms (horizontal, vertical) as in R10
TICKS = {"l": ((0.285, 0.385), (0.64, 0.665)), "r": ((0.27, 0.43), (0.60, 0.64))}   # tick groups: y ranges as fractions of H
_OFF = ("", "none", "off", "false", "no", "0", "-")
_CACHE = {}


# ---- config -----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
def _bool(v, default):
    if isinstance(v, str):
        return default if not v.strip() else v.strip().lower() not in _OFF
    return default if v is None else bool(v)


def t4_frame_cfg(raw):
    """Normalise cfg['t4']['frame'] -> {on, text, color, icon, show_ticks, ...extra keys}. Never raises; junk -> defaults."""
    out = dict(T4_FRAME)
    if not isinstance(raw, dict):
        return out
    try:
        out.update({k: v for k, v in raw.items() if k not in out})            # extras (font ...) survive
        out["on"] = _bool(raw.get("on"), False)
        t = raw.get("text")
        if isinstance(t, str):
            out["text"] = "" if t.strip().lower() in ("-", "none") else t.strip()[:60]
        out["color"] = _hex(raw.get("color"), T4_FRAME["color"])
        ic = raw.get("icon")
        out["icon"] = "" if (ic is False or (isinstance(ic, str) and ic.strip().lower() in _OFF)) \
            else (ic.strip().lower() if isinstance(ic, str) else T4_FRAME["icon"])
        out["show_ticks"] = _bool(raw.get("show_ticks"), True)
    except Exception:
        return dict(T4_FRAME)
    return out


def t4_ask_frame():
    """Question group 'frame' (called by t4_ask_config). -> the frame dict for cfg['t4']['frame']."""
    if not yn("Add the neon HUD frame (corner brackets, side ticks, bottom bar)?", default=False):
        return {"on": False}
    text = ask("Bottom bar text ('-' = no bar; Arabic works too)", default=T4_FRAME["text"], key="t4_frame_text",
               cast=lambda s: "" if s.strip() in ("-", "none") else s.strip())
    color = ask("Frame colour (hex or name)", default=T4_FRAME["color"], key="t4_frame_color", cast=c_color)
    ic = ask_choice("Small icon in the top-right corner:", ["None"] + list(ICON_NAMES), default=2, key="t4_frame_icon")
    return {"on": True, "text": text, "color": color, "icon": "" if ic == 1 else ICON_NAMES[ic - 2],
            "show_ticks": yn("Side tick marks?", default=True)}


# ---- rails, brackets, ticks, icon -------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
def _bracket(pl, cx, cy, sx, sy, arms, col, s):
    """One corner: thick chamfered L (outer) + a shorter thin line inside it. (cx, cy) = the corner, (sx, sy) = inward direction."""
    ah, av, c, d = arms[0] * s, arms[1] * s, 20 * s, 12 * s
    c2 = max(4.0, c * 0.65)

    def P(pts):
        return [(cx + sx * x, cy + sy * y) for x, y in pts]
    kit_paste(pl, connector(P([(ah, 0), (c, 0), (0, c), (0, av)]), col, False, 9.0 * s, 15 * s), 0, 0)
    kit_paste(pl, connector(P([(d + ah * 0.55, d), (d + c2, d), (d, d + c2), (d, d + av * 0.55)]), col, False, 3.0 * s, 7 * s), 0, 0)


def _rails(pl, W, H, col, s):
    m = INSET * s
    a = {k: (v[0] * s, v[1] * s) for k, v in ARMS.items()}
    for pts in ([(m + a["tl"][0], m), (W - m - a["tr"][0], m)], [(m + a["bl"][0], H - m), (W - m - a["br"][0], H - m)],
                [(m, m + a["tl"][1]), (m, H - m - a["bl"][1])], [(W - m, m + a["tr"][1]), (W - m, H - m - a["br"][1])]):
        kit_paste(pl, connector(pts, col, False, 2.8 * s, 9 * s), 0, 0)
    for key, cx, cy, sx, sy in (("tl", m, m, 1, 1), ("tr", W - m, m, -1, 1), ("bl", m, H - m, 1, -1), ("br", W - m, H - m, -1, -1)):
        _bracket(pl, cx, cy, sx, sy, ARMS[key], col, s)


def _tick_group(pl, x_edge, y0, y1, right, col, s):
    """A column of short horizontal dashes (alternating long / short, fading at both ends) hugging a side rail."""
    pad, step, th = int(10 * s), 12.0 * s, max(2, int(round(4 * s)))
    n = max(1, int((y1 - y0) // step))
    w, h = int(24 * s) + 2 * pad, int(y1 - y0) + 2 * pad
    m = Image.new("L", (w, h), 0)
    d = ImageDraw.Draw(m)
    for i in range(n):
        ln = int((21 if i % 2 == 0 else 11) * s)
        fade = min(1.0, 0.35 + 1.3 * min(i, n - 1 - i) / max(1.0, n / 2.0))
        y = pad + int(i * step)
        x0 = (w - pad - ln) if right else pad
        d.rectangle((x0, y, x0 + ln, y + th), fill=int(255 * fade))
    sp = Image.new("RGBA", (w, h), (0, 0, 0, 0))
    c = t2_rgb(col)
    t2_paint(sp, t2_glow((m, (0, 0)), 4 * s, 1.1), c, 0.55)
    t2_paint(sp, (m, (0, 0)), c)
    t2_paint(sp, (m, (0, 0)), t2_lighten(c, 0.6), 0.35)
    kit_paste(pl, sp, (x_edge - (w - pad) if right else x_edge - pad), y0 - pad)


def _ticks(pl, W, H, col, s):
    m = INSET * s
    for side, groups in TICKS.items():
        for a, b in groups:
            _tick_group(pl, (W - m - 14 * s) if side == "r" else (m + 14 * s), H * a, H * b, side == "r", col, s)


def _corner_icon(pl, W, name, col, s):
    sz = int(84 * s)
    ic = neon_icon(name, sz, col)
    kit_paste(pl, ic, W - (INSET + 100) * s - sz / 2.0, (INSET + 98) * s - sz / 2.0)


# ---- bottom bar -------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
def _fit_text(text, fp, size, track, avail):
    """Shrink size, then tracking, then truncate until the run fits `avail` px -> (font, text, track_px, width). RTL: untracked."""
    rtl = is_rtl_text(text)
    for _ in range(60):
        f = load_font(fp, max(8, int(round(size))))
        t = shape(text) if rtl else text
        tp = 0.0 if rtl else track * size
        w = f.getlength(t) if rtl else sum(f.getlength(ch) for ch in t) + tp * max(0, len(t) - 1)
        if w <= avail or len(text) <= 1:
            return f, t, tp, w
        if size > 14:
            size -= 1.5
        elif track > 0.04 and not rtl:
            track -= 0.05
        else:
            text = text[:-2].rstrip() + "â€¦"
    return f, t, tp, w


def _draw_tab_text(pl, f, t, tp, w, cx, base_y, rtl, col, s):
    """White text (tracked per character for LTR) with a faint neon halo."""
    m = Image.new("L", pl.size, 0)
    d = ImageDraw.Draw(m)
    if rtl:
        d.text((cx - w / 2.0, base_y), t, font=f, fill=255, anchor="ls")
    else:
        x = cx - w / 2.0
        for ch in t:
            d.text((x, base_y), ch, font=f, fill=255, anchor="ls")
            x += f.getlength(ch) + tp
    bb = m.getbbox()
    if not bb:
        return
    pad = int(14 * s)
    crop = m.crop((bb[0] - pad, bb[1] - pad, bb[2] + pad, bb[3] + pad))
    box = (bb[0] - pad, bb[1] - pad)
    t2_paint(pl, t2_glow((crop, box), 5 * s, 1.0), t2_rgb(col), 0.45)
    t2_paint(pl, (crop, box), (255, 255, 255))


def _hazards(pl, xl, xr, y, hw, hh, col):
    """'////' marks: the group ending at xl (left of the tab) and the one starting at xr (right of it)."""
    for x in (xl - hw, xr):
        kit_paste(pl, hazard_stripes((hw, hh), 32.0, col, 8, hh * 0.52, hh * 0.40), x, y)


def _bottom_bar(pl, W, H, text, fp, col, s):
    m, th, sl = INSET * s, 60 * s, 34 * s
    rtl = is_rtl_text(text)
    bw_max = min(0.64 * W, 860 * s)
    f, t, tp, tw = _fit_text(text, fp, 26 * s, 0.30, bw_max - 2 * sl - 80 * s)
    top_w = tw + 80 * s
    bw = top_w + 2 * sl
    x0, yb = (W - bw) / 2.0, H - m
    yt = yb - th
    mask = t2_poly_mask([(sl, 0), (bw - sl, 0), (bw, th), (0, th)], round_r=5 * s)
    kit_paste(pl, _glass_sprite(mask, (6, 14, 34, 218), col, max(2.0, 2.4 * s), 12 * s), x0, yt)
    asc, desc = f.getmetrics()
    _draw_tab_text(pl, f, t, tp, tw, W / 2.0, (yt + yb) / 2.0 + (asc - desc) / 2.0, rtl, col, s)
    hw = min(108 * s, x0 - 40 * s - (m + 14 * s))
    if hw >= 40 * s:
        _hazards(pl, x0 - 40 * s, x0 + bw + 40 * s, yb - 44 * s, hw, 26 * s, col)


# ---- plate ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
def t4_frame_plate(W, H, fcfg):
    """The transparent W x H RGBA frame plate for a normalised frame dict (cached per size + look; returns a private copy)."""
    W, H = int(W), int(H)
    fp = fcfg.get("font")
    fp = fp if isinstance(fp, str) and os.path.isfile(fp) else default_font()
    ic = fcfg["icon"] if fcfg["icon"] in ICON_NAMES else ""
    key = (W, H, fcfg["color"], fcfg["text"], ic, bool(fcfg["show_ticks"]), fp)
    if key not in _CACHE:
        s, col = min(W, H) / 1080.0, fcfg["color"]
        pl = Image.new("RGBA", (W, H), (0, 0, 0, 0))
        _rails(pl, W, H, col, s)
        if fcfg["show_ticks"]:
            _ticks(pl, W, H, col, s)
        if ic:
            _corner_icon(pl, W, ic, col, s)
        if fcfg["text"]:
            _bottom_bar(pl, W, H, fcfg["text"], fp, col, s)
        if len(_CACHE) >= 4:
            _CACHE.pop(next(iter(_CACHE)))
        _CACHE[key] = pl
    return _CACHE[key].copy()


def t4_frame_part(W, H, t4):
    """Part builder for t4_compose_fg: the frame plate, or None when the module is off. Warns once for an unknown icon."""
    f = t4_frame_cfg((t4 or {}).get("frame"))
    if not f["on"]:
        return None
    if f["icon"] and f["icon"] not in ICON_NAMES:
        t4_warn(f"frame icon '{f['icon']}' unknown (use one of: {', '.join(ICON_NAMES)}) - no icon")
    return t4_frame_plate(W, H, f)


====================================================================================================
FILE: mt\templates\t4\tiles.py
====================================================================================================
"""mt.templates.t4.tiles - Template 4 module 'tiles' (ref R2, left side): groups of dark glass tiles, each holding a user logo (PNG ...,
fitted + centred), laid out in a grid, optionally with a neon connector polyline that ends in a glowing dot at a target point.
cfg['t4']['tiles'] = [group, ...]   (also accepted: one group dict, or {on, groups: [...]})   group = {
    images [paths]       the logos, row by row (<= 12). A missing / unreadable file -> gray placeholder tile + one t4_warn line.
    cols 2, gap 18, size 150   grid columns (a short last row is centred), px between tiles, tile edge (px at short side 1080)
    anchor 'left' | xy     anchor = one of the 9 positions (top-left top top-right left center right bottom-left bottom bottom-right) with
                           inset_x / inset_y px; xy = [x, y] = the CENTRE of the group (px, or '40%' strings). Default anchor 'left'.
    border '#C4D6EF'       tile border colour (light, as R2)
    connector {points [[x, y] ...], color, end_dot True|'both'|'start'|False, width, glow}   points in canvas px or '%' strings; a point may
                           also be 'group', 'group-top|bottom|left|right' = that point of the tile group. >= 2 points, drawn after the tiles.
    start | trigger, end | hold (default 5 s), anim 'pop' (default; 'fade' / 'none' / any mt.anim name), anim_dur 0.35, stagger 0.12 s }
Behaviour: the tiles pop in one after another (t4_stagger), the connector fades in after the last one, and everything fades out together at the
group's end (anim 'pop' = anim 't4_pop' in mt/anim.py: pop in, pure fade out; other anims run reversed). Ink stays >= 48 px from the canvas
edges (connector points are clamped into the safe area). Frame on (P8): an anchored group moves inward until it clears the frame (same search as
the name tag); an xy group is only clamped to the safe area. Each tile = ONE sprite Item (cached); nothing is drawn per frame but the animation.
Public: T4_TILES, t4_tiles_cfg(raw) -> [group], t4_tile_sprite(path, size, border, scale), t4_tiles_items(cfg, duration), t4_ask_tiles()."""
import copy
import math
import os
from pathlib import Path

from PIL import Image

from mt.anim import ANIMS, PRESETS
from mt.ask import ask_anim
from mt.common import IMAGE_EXTS, ask, ask_choice, c_color, c_existing_file, c_float, c_int, yn
from mt.draw import Item, t2_paint, t2_rrect_mask
from mt.templates.t4.cfg import _hex
from mt.templates.t4.frame import t4_frame_cfg
from mt.templates.t4.kit import connector, glass_panel, kit_paste
from mt.templates.t4.nameplate import T4_SAFE, _bool, _cached, _flt, _frame_mask, _place, _window
from mt.templates.t4.timing import t4_ask_when, t4_stagger
from mt.templates.t4.warn import t4_warn

ANCHORS = ("top-left", "top", "top-right", "left", "center", "right", "bottom-left", "bottom", "bottom-right")
MAX_IMAGES = 12
LOGO_FRAC = 0.60                                       # the logo fills this fraction of the tile (R2: ~55-65 %)
TINT = (8, 16, 36, 150)                                # tile glass (rgba): translucent, the video shows through
T4_TILES = {"on": True, "images": [], "cols": 2, "gap": 18.0, "size": 150.0, "anchor": "left", "xy": None, "inset_x": 0.0, "inset_y": 0.0,
            "border": "#c4d6ef", "connector": {}, "start": 0.0, "trigger": "", "end": None, "hold": None, "anim": "pop", "anim_dur": 0.35,
            "stagger": 0.12}
_CONN = {"points": [], "color": "#19d3ff", "end_dot": True, "width": 2.5, "glow": 9.0}
_TOKENS = ("group", "group-top", "group-bottom", "group-left", "group-right")
_TILE = {}


# ---- config -----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
def _anchor(v, default):
    s = str(v).strip().lower().replace("_", "-").replace(" ", "-") if isinstance(v, (str, int)) and not isinstance(v, bool) else ""
    s = PRESETS.get(s, s)
    s = {"middle": "center", "centre": "center", "top-center": "top", "bottom-center": "bottom"}.get(s, s)
    return s if s in ANCHORS else default


def _num_or_pct(v):
    """12, '12', '40%' -> kept as given (number or 'NN%' string); anything else -> None."""
    if isinstance(v, bool):
        return None
    if isinstance(v, (int, float)):
        return float(v) if math.isfinite(v) else None
    if isinstance(v, str):
        t = v.strip()
        try:
            return t if t.endswith("%") and math.isfinite(float(t[:-1])) else float(t)
        except ValueError:
            return None
    return None


def _point(p):
    """[x, y] / (x, y) / 'x,y' / a group token -> [x, y] | token | None."""
    if isinstance(p, str):
        t = p.strip().lower()
        if t in _TOKENS:
            return t
        p = [a for a in t.replace(";", ",").split(",")]
    if isinstance(p, (list, tuple)) and len(p) == 2:
        a, b = _num_or_pct(p[0]), _num_or_pct(p[1])
        return [a, b] if a is not None and b is not None else None
    return None


def _conn(raw):
    out = copy.deepcopy(_CONN)
    if not isinstance(raw, dict):
        return {}
    pts = [_point(p) for p in raw.get("points", [])] if isinstance(raw.get("points"), (list, tuple)) else []
    out["points"] = [p for p in pts if p is not None][:12]
    out["color"] = _hex(raw.get("color"), out["color"])
    ed = raw.get("end_dot")
    out["end_dot"] = ed if ed in (True, False, "both", "start") else True
    out["width"], out["glow"] = _flt(raw.get("width"), 0.5, 30, 2.5), _flt(raw.get("glow"), 0, 60, 9.0)
    return out if len(out["points"]) >= 2 else {}


def _group(raw):
    out = copy.deepcopy(T4_TILES)
    out.update({k: copy.deepcopy(v) for k, v in raw.items() if k not in out})                       # extras survive
    imgs = raw.get("images")
    out["images"] = [i.strip() for i in imgs if isinstance(i, str) and i.strip()][:MAX_IMAGES] if isinstance(imgs, (list, tuple)) else []
    try:
        out["cols"] = max(1, min(8, int(raw.get("cols", 2))))
    except (TypeError, ValueError, OverflowError):
        out["cols"] = 2
    out["gap"], out["size"] = _flt(raw.get("gap"), 0, 200, 18.0), _flt(raw.get("size"), 40, 600, 150.0)
    out["anchor"] = _anchor(raw.get("anchor"), "left")
    xy = _point(raw.get("xy"))
    out["xy"] = xy if isinstance(xy, list) else None
    out["inset_x"], out["inset_y"] = _flt(raw.get("inset_x"), 0, 600, 0.0), _flt(raw.get("inset_y"), 0, 600, 0.0)
    out["border"] = _hex(raw.get("border"), "#c4d6ef")
    out["connector"] = _conn(raw.get("connector"))
    trig = raw.get("trigger")
    out["trigger"] = trig.strip() if isinstance(trig, str) else ""
    out["start"] = raw["start"] if raw.get("start") is not None else (None if out["trigger"] else 0.0)      # a phrase never falls back to 0
    out["end"], out["hold"] = raw.get("end"), raw.get("hold")
    a = raw.get("anim")
    a = ("reveal" if a.strip().lower() == "typewriter" else a.strip().lower()) if isinstance(a, str) else "pop"
    out["anim"] = a if a in ANIMS or a in ("pop", "none") else "pop"
    out["anim_dur"], out["stagger"] = _flt(raw.get("anim_dur"), 0.0, 5.0, 0.35), _flt(raw.get("stagger"), 0.0, 2.0, 0.12)
    out["on"] = _bool(raw.get("on"), True)
    return out


def t4_tiles_cfg(raw):
    """Normalise cfg['t4']['tiles'] -> list of complete groups. Accepts a list of groups, one group dict or {on, groups: [...]} (module
    default {'on': False} = no groups). Never raises; junk entries are dropped."""
    try:
        if isinstance(raw, dict) and "images" in raw:
            raw = [raw]
        elif isinstance(raw, dict):
            raw = raw.get("groups") if _bool(raw.get("on"), False) else []
        return [_group(g) for g in raw if isinstance(g, dict)] if isinstance(raw, (list, tuple)) else []
    except Exception:
        return []


# ---- sprites ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
def _load_logo(path):
    """-> RGBA image, or None (missing / not an image). First frame of animated files."""
    try:
        if not path or not os.path.isfile(str(path)):
            return None
        with Image.open(str(path)) as im:
            im.seek(0)
            return im.convert("RGBA")
    except Exception:
        return None


def _placeholder(box):
    """Gray rounded square with a small 'image' mark - stands in for a missing logo (box = px)."""
    n = max(8, int(box))
    ph = Image.new("RGBA", (n, n), (0, 0, 0, 0))
    t2_paint(ph, t2_rrect_mask((0, 0, n, n), n * 0.16), (128, 132, 140), 0.85)
    t2_paint(ph, t2_rrect_mask((n * 0.20, n * 0.24, n * 0.80, n * 0.76), n * 0.06), (60, 64, 72), 0.9)
    t2_paint(ph, t2_rrect_mask((n * 0.30, n * 0.56, n * 0.50, n * 0.68), n * 0.03), (160, 166, 176))
    return ph


def _build_tile(path, size, border, scale):
    sz = max(24, int(round(size * scale)))
    sp = glass_panel((sz, sz), sz * 0.22, TINT, border, 0, max(1.4, sz * 0.010), max(2.5, sz * 0.022))      # thin soft border as R2
    ox, oy = sp.info["origin"]
    box, msg = int(round(sz * LOGO_FRAC)), None
    logo = _load_logo(path)
    if logo is None:
        msg = "tiles: image '%s' is missing or not a readable image - gray placeholder." % (Path(str(path)).name or "(empty path)")
        logo = _placeholder(box)
    k = min(box / logo.width, box / logo.height)
    lw, lh = max(1, int(round(logo.width * k))), max(1, int(round(logo.height * k)))
    logo = logo.resize((lw, lh), Image.LANCZOS)
    kit_paste(sp, logo, ox + (sz - lw) / 2.0, oy + (sz - lh) / 2.0)
    sp.info["panel"] = sz
    return sp, msg


def t4_tile_sprite(path, size=150, border="#c4d6ef", scale=1.0):
    """One glass tile with the logo fitted + centred -> RGBA sprite (glow margin included; sprite.info['origin'] = the tile's top-left,
    sprite.info['panel'] = tile edge px). A missing / unreadable file gives a gray placeholder (the one-line warning goes through t4_warn).
    Cached per (path, size, border, scale); a cache hit warns again so a new render still reports it (t4_warn prints once per render)."""
    key = (str(path), round(float(size) * float(scale)), str(border), round(float(scale), 3), os.path.getmtime(path) if path and os.path.isfile(str(path)) else 0)
    sp, msg = _cached(_TILE, key, lambda: _build_tile(path, float(size), border, float(scale)), 48)
    if msg:
        t4_warn(msg)
    return sp


# ---- layout -----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
def _grid(n, cols, size, gap):
    """Top-left of every tile inside the group box + the box size; a short last row is centred. -> ([(x, y)], gw, gh)."""
    cols = max(1, min(cols, n))
    rows = int(math.ceil(n / float(cols)))
    gw, gh = cols * size + (cols - 1) * gap, rows * size + (rows - 1) * gap
    pos = []
    for i in range(n):
        r, c = divmod(i, cols)
        in_row = min(cols, n - r * cols)
        pos.append((c * (size + gap) + (cols - in_row) * (size + gap) / 2.0, r * (size + gap)))
    return pos, gw, gh


def _footprint(tiles, pos, gw, gh, pad):
    """All tiles composited into one sprite - only used to measure the group's ink for the anchor / frame placement."""
    fp = Image.new("RGBA", (int(gw) + 2 * pad, int(gh) + 2 * pad), (0, 0, 0, 0))
    for sp, (x, y) in zip(tiles, pos):
        ox, oy = sp.info["origin"]
        fp.alpha_composite(sp, (int(round(pad + x)) - ox, int(round(pad + y)) - oy))
    return fp


def _coord(v, total, s):
    return total * float(v[:-1]) / 100.0 if isinstance(v, str) else float(v) * s


def _conn_points(c, gbox, W, H, s):
    """Resolve numbers / '%' / group tokens to canvas px, clamped so the line's VISIBLE ink (glow tail + end dot) stays in the 48 px safe area."""
    m, out = (T4_SAFE + c["glow"] * 1.6 + 2) * s, []
    gx0, gy0, gx1, gy1 = gbox
    named = {"group": ((gx0 + gx1) / 2.0, (gy0 + gy1) / 2.0), "group-top": ((gx0 + gx1) / 2.0, gy0), "group-bottom": ((gx0 + gx1) / 2.0, gy1),
             "group-left": (gx0, (gy0 + gy1) / 2.0), "group-right": (gx1, (gy0 + gy1) / 2.0)}
    for p in c["points"]:
