        x, y = named[p] if isinstance(p, str) else (_coord(p[0], W, s), _coord(p[1], H, s))
        out.append((min(max(x, m), W - m), min(max(y, m), H - m)))
    return out


# ---- items ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
class TileItem(Item):
    """Tile with anim 't4_pop': in = pop, out = pure fade (progress < 0 encodes the exit, see mt/anim.py)."""

    def progress(self, t):
        L = self.end - self.start
        d = min(self.dur, L / 2.0)
        if d <= 1e-6:
            return 1.0
        pin, pout = (t - self.start) / d, (self.end - t) / d
        if pout < 1.0 and pout <= pin:
            return -max(0.0, pout)
        return max(0.0, min(1.0, pin))


def _make_item(sp, st, en, cx, cy, c):
    if c["anim"] == "pop":
        return TileItem(sp, st, en, cx, cy, "t4_pop", c["anim_dur"], out=True)
    return Item(sp, st, en, cx, cy, c["anim"], c["anim_dur"], out=True)


def _conn_sprite(c, pts, s):
    """Connector sprite + the item centre that puts its local (0, 0) back on the canvas origin (points are canvas coordinates)."""
    k = c["connector"]
    sp = connector(pts, k["color"], k["end_dot"], k["width"] * s, k["glow"] * s)
    ox, oy = sp.info["origin"]
    return sp, -ox + sp.width / 2.0, -oy + sp.height / 2.0


def _one_group(cfg, c, idx, duration, W, H, s, fm, placed):
    label = "tiles group %d" % (idx + 1)
    if not c["images"]:
        t4_warn("%s: no images - skipped." % label)
        return []
    win = _window(cfg, c, label, duration, 5.0)
    if win is None:
        return []
    n, size, gap = len(c["images"]), c["size"] * s, c["gap"] * s
    tiles = [t4_tile_sprite(p, c["size"], c["border"], s) for p in c["images"]]
    pos, gw, gh = _grid(n, c["cols"], size, gap)
    pad = tiles[0].info["origin"][0]
    fp = _footprint(tiles, pos, gw, gh, pad)
    if c["xy"]:
        pl = _place_xy(fp, c["xy"], W, H, s)
    else:
        pl = _place(fp, c["anchor"], W, H, s, c["inset_x"], c["inset_y"], fm, label)
    if pl is None:
        return []
    fcx, fcy, rect, _ = pl
    placed.append((label, rect))
    left, top = fcx - fp.width / 2.0, fcy - fp.height / 2.0                       # the footprint sprite's top-left on the canvas
    steps = t4_stagger(n, c["stagger"])
    last = max(win[0], win[1] - 0.1)
    items = [_make_item(sp, min(win[0] + d, last), win[1], left + pad + x + size / 2.0, top + pad + y + size / 2.0, c)
             for sp, (x, y), d in zip(tiles, pos, steps)]
    if c["connector"]:
        gbox = (left + pad, top + pad, left + pad + gw, top + pad + gh)
        sp, cx, cy = _conn_sprite(c, _conn_points(c["connector"], gbox, W, H, s), s)
        items.append(Item(sp, min(win[0] + (steps[-1] if steps else 0.0) + c["anim_dur"], last), win[1], cx, cy, "fade", c["anim_dur"], out=True))
    return items


def _place_xy(fp, xy, W, H, s):
    """Group centred on xy (px / '%'), then clamped so its ink keeps the 48 px safe margin -> same tuple as nameplate._place."""
    from mt.templates.t4.nameplate import _ink_box
    box = _ink_box(fp)
    if box is None:
        return None
    w, h, m = box[2] - box[0], box[3] - box[1], T4_SAFE * s
    x = int(round(min(max(_coord(xy[0], W, s) - w / 2.0, m), W - m - w)))
    y = int(round(min(max(_coord(xy[1], H, s) - h / 2.0, m), H - m - h)))
    return x - box[0] + fp.width / 2.0, y - box[1] + fp.height / 2.0, (x, y, x + w, y + h), 0


def _warn_overlaps(placed):
    for i, (a, ra) in enumerate(placed):
        for b, rb in placed[i + 1:]:
            if ra[0] < rb[2] and rb[0] < ra[2] and ra[1] < rb[3] and rb[1] < ra[3]:
                t4_warn("%s and %s overlap - give one of them another anchor / xy." % (a, b))


def t4_tiles_items(cfg, duration):
    """Items of every tiles group: one per tile (staggered pop-in) + one for the connector; all fade out together. Never raises."""
    t4 = cfg.get("t4") if isinstance(cfg, dict) and isinstance(cfg.get("t4"), dict) else {}
    groups = [g for g in t4_tiles_cfg(t4.get("tiles")) if g["on"]]
    if not groups:
        return []
    try:
        W, H = int(cfg["canvas"][0]), int(cfg["canvas"][1])
    except (KeyError, TypeError, ValueError, IndexError):
        t4_warn("t4 tiles: the job has no canvas - skipped.")
        return []
    s, items, placed = min(W, H) / 1080.0, [], []
    try:
        fm = _frame_mask(W, H, t4_frame_cfg(t4.get("frame")), s)
        for i, g in enumerate(groups):
            items += _one_group(cfg, g, i, duration, W, H, s, fm, placed)
        _warn_overlaps(placed)
    except Exception as e:
        t4_warn("t4 tiles: could not be drawn (%s: %s) - skipped." % (type(e).__name__, e))
    return items


# ---- questions --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
def _c_points(s):
    pts = [_point(p.strip()) for p in s.replace("|", ";").split(";") if p.strip()]
    if len(pts) < 2 or any(p is None for p in pts):
        raise ValueError("enter at least 2 points like  100,900; 250,1000  (px or %, or the words group-bottom / group-right ...)")
    return pts


def t4_ask_tiles():
    """Question group 'tiles' (called by t4_ask_config). -> a list with ONE group, or {'on': False}. More groups: edit the job file."""
    if not yn("Add glass logo tiles (PNG logos in a grid - reference R2)?", default=False):
        return {"on": False}
    imgs = []
    while len(imgs) < MAX_IMAGES:
        p = ask("Logo %d (PNG / image path, Enter = %s)" % (len(imgs) + 1, "done" if imgs else "at least one is needed"),
                key="t4_tiles_img%d" % (len(imgs) + 1), cast=c_existing_file(IMAGE_EXTS), allow_empty=bool(imgs))
        if not p:
            break
        imgs.append(p)
    g = {"images": imgs, "cols": ask("Columns", default=2, key="t4_tiles_cols", cast=c_int),
         "size": ask("Tile size (px, for a 1080 px short side)", default=T4_TILES["size"], key="t4_tiles_size", cast=c_float)}
    a = ask_choice("Where?", ["Top-left", "Top", "Top-right", "Left", "Center", "Right", "Bottom-left", "Bottom", "Bottom-right"],
                   default=4, key="t4_tiles_anchor")
    g["anchor"] = ANCHORS[a - 1]
    if yn("Add a connector line ending in a glowing dot?", default=False):
        pts = ask("Connector points  x,y; x,y; ...  (px or %, first may be group-bottom / group-right)", default="group-bottom; 30%,55%",
                  key="t4_tiles_conn", cast=_c_points)
        g["connector"] = {"points": pts, "end_dot": yn("Dot at both ends (No = only at the target)?", default=False) and "both" or True}
    g.update(t4_ask_when("Logo tiles", 5.0, key="t4_tiles_when"))
    g["anim"] = ask_anim("Tile animation (pop = staggered pop-in, fade-out together)", default="pop", key="t4_tiles_anim")
    return [g]


====================================================================================================
FILE: mt\templates\t4\timing.py
====================================================================================================
"""mt.templates.t4.timing - Template 4: when does an overlay module show? Pure functions, no drawing.
t4_time(entry, cues, duration, default_hold) -> (start, end) | None;  t4_stagger(n, step) -> [delay];  t4_ask_when(prompt, default_hold).
t4_warn / t4_warn_reset live in t4/warn.py (P2, once per render - the same pattern as t3_warn) and are re-exported here so every
timing user imports from one place; a second message set would print the same warning twice."""
import math

from mt.captions import find_trigger
from mt.common import ask, ask_choice, c_float, c_time
from mt.templates.t4.warn import t4_warn, t4_warn_reset  # noqa: F401  (re-export)

MIN_SPAN = 0.05                                  # a window shorter than this is not worth drawing


def _num(v):
    """Seconds from a number or '12', '0:45', '3s' -> float; anything else (None, bool, nan, junk) -> None. Never raises."""
    if v is None or isinstance(v, bool):
        return None
    try:
        x = float(c_time(v)) if isinstance(v, str) else float(v)
    except (ValueError, TypeError, OverflowError):
        return None
    return x if math.isfinite(x) else None


def _streams(cues):
    """cues = one SRT cue list [(s, e, text)] or a list of such lists (every caption stream) -> list of cue lists."""
    try:
        if cues and isinstance(cues[0], (list, tuple)) and cues[0] and isinstance(cues[0][0], (list, tuple)):
            return [c for c in cues if c]
        return [cues] if cues else []
    except (TypeError, IndexError, KeyError):
        return []


def _find(cues, phrase):
    """First stream that contains the phrase wins -> its start time, else None. Never raises."""
    for st in _streams(cues):
        try:
            t = find_trigger(st, phrase)
        except Exception:
            t = None
        if t is not None:
            return float(t)
    return None


def _label(entry):
    for k in ("label", "name", "title", "text", "trigger"):
        v = entry.get(k)
        if isinstance(v, str) and v.strip():
            return "'%s'" % v.strip()[:40]
    return "entry"


def _start(entry, cues, lab):
    """trigger (spoken phrase) first, else the fixed start; None + one warning when neither gives a time."""
    fixed = _num(entry.get("start"))
    phrase = entry.get("trigger")
    phrase = phrase.strip() if isinstance(phrase, str) else ""
    if phrase:
        t = _find(cues, phrase)
        if t is not None:
            return t
        if fixed is None:
            t4_warn("%s: phrase '%s' not found in the captions and no start time - skipped." % (lab, phrase))
            return None
        t4_warn("%s: phrase '%s' not found in the captions - using its start time." % (lab, phrase))
    if fixed is None:
        t4_warn("%s: no start time and no trigger phrase - skipped." % lab)
    return fixed


def t4_time(entry, cues, duration, default_hold):
    """-> (start, end) in seconds, or None (skip the entry).
    start: `trigger` (spoken phrase found in cues via find_trigger; cues = one cue list or a list of streams) else `start`.
    end:   `end` (absolute, must be after start) else start + `hold` else start + default_hold (None / <= 0 -> to the video end).
    Start at or after the video end -> None; an end past the video end is clamped to `duration` (duration None / <= 0 = unknown,
    no clamping). A negative start becomes 0. Junk input never raises (one t4_warn line, None)."""
    if not isinstance(entry, dict):
        t4_warn("timing: entry is not a dict - skipped.")
        return None
    lab = _label(entry)
    st = _start(entry, cues, lab)
    if st is None:
        return None
    st = max(0.0, st)
    dur = _num(duration)
    dur = dur if dur and dur > 0 else None
    if dur is not None and st >= dur:
        t4_warn("%s: starts at %.2f s, after the video end (%.2f s) - skipped." % (lab, st, dur))
        return None
    en = _num(entry.get("end"))
    if en is not None and en <= st + MIN_SPAN:
        t4_warn("%s: end %.2f s is not after the start %.2f s - using the hold time." % (lab, en, st))
        en = None
    if en is None:
        hold = _num(entry.get("hold"))
        if hold is None or hold <= 0:
            hold = _num(default_hold)
        en = st + hold if hold and hold > 0 else (dur if dur is not None else None)
    if en is None:
        t4_warn("%s: no end / hold and the video length is unknown - skipped." % lab)
        return None
    if dur is not None:
        en = min(en, dur)
    return (st, en) if en - st >= MIN_SPAN else None


def t4_stagger(n, step):
    """Entry delays for n items that appear one after another: [0, step, 2*step, ...]. n <= 0 -> []; bad step -> 0.0."""
    try:
        n = max(0, int(n))
    except (ValueError, TypeError, OverflowError):
        return []
    s = _num(step)
    s = s if s is not None and s > 0 else 0.0
    return [round(i * s, 6) for i in range(n)]


def _c_hold(s):
    v = c_float(s)
    if not v > 0:
        raise ValueError("enter a number of seconds above 0")
    return v


def t4_ask_when(prompt, default_hold=4.0, key=None):
    """The one shared 'when does it show' question. -> {"start": seconds, "hold": s} or {"trigger": phrase, "hold": s}
    (both shapes are accepted by t4_time). key = optional prefix that remembers the answers (mt.common.D)."""
    k = (lambda n: key + n) if key else (lambda n: None)
    mode = ask_choice(f"{prompt} - when should it appear?",
                      ["At a fixed time (seconds or mm:ss)", "When a phrase is spoken (it must be in the captions)"],
                      default=1, key=k("_mode"))
    if mode == 1:
        out = {"start": ask("Start at  (seconds or mm:ss)", default=0, key=k("_start"), cast=c_time)}
    else:
        out = {"trigger": ask("Spoken phrase", key=k("_trigger")).strip()}
    out["hold"] = ask("Keep it on screen for how many seconds", default=default_hold, key=k("_hold"), cast=_c_hold)
    return out


====================================================================================================
FILE: mt\templates\t4\warn.py
====================================================================================================
"""mt.templates.t4.warn - Template 4: one-line [!] messages, printed once per render (items are rebuilt per frame / segment)."""
_MSGS = set()


def t4_warn_reset():
    _MSGS.clear()


def t4_warn(msg):
    """Print '   [!] msg' once per render; never raises."""
    msg = "   [!] " + str(msg).strip()
    if msg not in _MSGS:
        _MSGS.add(msg)
        print(msg)


====================================================================================================
FILE: mt\templates\t4\__init__.py
====================================================================================================
"""mt.templates.t4 - Template 4 "Overlay Studio" (registry id 5): hooks. Config namespace cfg["t4"] (see cfg.py).
T4-0: only the plain video on a solid backdrop; the design modules (frame, nameplate, tiles, ...) arrive in later tasks."""
import os
import tempfile

from mt.templates.t4.captions import t4_caption_items, t4_caption_style  # noqa: F401  (t4_caption_style = registry hook)
from mt.common import D, IMAGE_EXTS, ask, ask_choice, c_color, c_existing_file, c_int, probe
from mt.templates.t4.base import t4_apply_backdrop
from mt.templates.t4.cards import t4_ask_image_cards, t4_cards_cfg, t4_cards_items
from mt.templates.t4.cards_video import t4_prepare_card_layers  # noqa: F401  (registry hook prepare_layers, id 5)
from mt.templates.t4.frame import t4_ask_frame, t4_frame_cfg, t4_frame_part
from mt.templates.t4.nameplate import t4_ask_corner_list, t4_ask_nameplate, t4_corner_list_cfg, t4_nameplate_cfg, t4_nameplate_items
from mt.templates.t4.tiles import t4_ask_tiles, t4_tiles_cfg, t4_tiles_items
from mt.templates.t4.warn import t4_warn, t4_warn_reset
from mt.templates.t4.scenes import t4_ask_scenes, t4_scenes_cfg, t4_scenes_items
from mt.templates.t4.cfg import CANVAS_REELS, T4_DEFAULTS, T4_MODULES, t4_canvas, t4_cfg  # noqa: F401


def _full_box(canvas, fit):
    return {"x": 0, "y": 0, "w": int(canvas[0]), "h": int(canvas[1]), "mode": fit}


def t4_ask_config(video_info):
    """Canvas (auto / reels / youtube), fill or fit, letterbox colour (fit only). -> tpl dict; video_box = the whole canvas."""
    print("\n=== Template 4 - Overlay Studio ===")
    cm = ask_choice("Canvas:", ["Auto (by the video shape)", "Reels / Shorts 1080x1920", "YouTube 1920x1080"], default=1,
                    key="t4_canvas")
    mode = ("auto", "reels", "youtube")[cm - 1]
    canvas = t4_canvas(mode, video_info)
    print(f"   -> canvas {canvas[0]}x{canvas[1]}")
    ft = ask_choice("Video inside the canvas:", ["Fill (crop to the canvas)", "Fit (whole video, letterboxed)"], default=1,
                    key="t4_fit")
    fit = "fill" if ft == 1 else "fit"
    t4 = {"canvas_mode": mode, "fit": fit}
    if fit == "fit":
        t4["backdrop"] = _ask_backdrop()
    t4["frame"] = t4_ask_frame()                       # question group 'frame' (T4-8)
    t4["nameplate"] = t4_ask_nameplate()               # question groups 'nameplate' (tag_a / tag_b) and 'corner_list' (T4-9)
    t4["corner_list"] = t4_ask_corner_list()
    t4["tiles"] = t4_ask_tiles()                       # question group 'tiles' (T4-10): a list with one group, or {'on': False}
    t4["cards"] = t4_ask_image_cards()                 # question group 'cards' (T4-11): a list with one image card, or {'on': False}
    t4["scenes"] = t4_ask_scenes()
    t4 = t4_cfg(t4)
    return {"template_id": 5, "canvas": canvas, "video_box": _full_box(canvas, fit), "background": {"type": "color", "value": t4["backdrop"]["value"]},
            "layers": [], "t4": t4}


def _ask_backdrop():
    """What shows around a 'fit' video: solid colour / image / blurred copy of the video. -> backdrop dict (t4_cfg normalises it)."""
    kind = ask_choice("Around the video (backdrop):", ["Solid colour (black by default)", "Image", "Blurred copy of the video"],
                      default=1, key="t4_backdrop_type")
    if kind == 2:
        return {"type": "image", "path": ask("Backdrop image (PNG/JPG...)", cast=c_existing_file(IMAGE_EXTS))}
    if kind == 3:
        return {"type": "blur", "strength": ask("Blur strength 1-10", default=5, key="t4_blur", cast=c_int)}
    return {"type": "color", "value": ask("Backdrop colour (hex or name)", default="#000000", key="t4_backdrop", cast=c_color)}


def t4_ask_cards(cues):
    return []                      # Template 4 has no generic cards; its card modules come later (cfg["t4"]["cards"])


def _canvas_ok(c):
    try:
        return len(c) == 2 and all(int(v) > 0 for v in c)
    except (TypeError, ValueError):
        return False


# ---- the T4 foreground plate (cfg['_t2_fg']): ONE transparent full-canvas PNG over the video, merged from the static parts ---------------
# Each part = (name, builder); builder(W, H, t4) -> RGBA image W x H | None (module off). List order = bottom -> top. A later module
# (nameplate, tags, ...) adds ONE line here: T4_FG_PARTS.append(("nameplate", t4_nameplate_part)) - never its own _t2_fg.
T4_FG_PARTS = [("frame", t4_frame_part)]


def t4_compose_fg(cfg, tmp):
    """Alpha-composite every active part into one plate, save tmp/t4_fg.png and point cfg['_t2_fg'] at it (the pipeline's generic
    foreground slot). No active part -> cfg['_t2_fg'] removed, returns None. A failing part warns once and is skipped; never raises."""
    cfg.pop("_t2_fg", None)
    try:
        W, H = int(cfg["canvas"][0]), int(cfg["canvas"][1])
    except (KeyError, TypeError, ValueError, IndexError):
        return None
    plate = None
    for name, build in T4_FG_PARTS:
        try:
            part = build(W, H, cfg.get("t4"))
        except Exception as e:
            t4_warn(f"{name}: could not be drawn ({type(e).__name__}: {e}) - skipped")
            continue
        if part is None:
            continue
        if part.size != (W, H):
            t4_warn(f"{name}: plate is {part.size[0]}x{part.size[1]}, canvas is {W}x{H} - skipped")
            continue
        if plate is None:
            plate = part.convert("RGBA")
        else:
            plate.alpha_composite(part.convert("RGBA"))
    if plate is None:
        return None
    path = os.path.join(tmp or tempfile.gettempdir(), "t4_fg.png")
    plate.save(path, compress_level=1)
    cfg["_t2_fg"] = path
    return path


def t4_build_static_layers(cfg, tmp):
    """Hook build_background: solid backdrop + the foreground plate. The video is the base, so video_box is forced to the full canvas
    (fill / fit from cfg["t4"]); a job without a usable canvas gets one from canvas_mode + the video shape."""
    t4_warn_reset()
    t4 = t4_cfg(cfg.get("t4"))
    cfg["t4"] = t4
    if not _canvas_ok(cfg.get("canvas")):
        info = None
        try:
            info = probe(cfg["video"]) if cfg.get("video") and os.path.isfile(str(cfg["video"])) else None
        except Exception:
            info = None
        cfg["canvas"] = t4_canvas(t4["canvas_mode"], info)
    cfg["canvas"] = [int(cfg["canvas"][0]), int(cfg["canvas"][1])]
    cfg["video_box"] = _full_box(cfg["canvas"], t4["fit"])
    t4_apply_backdrop(cfg, t4)
    t4["frame"] = t4_frame_cfg(t4.get("frame"))        # complete {on, text, color, icon, show_ticks} (extras kept)
    t4["nameplate"] = t4_nameplate_cfg(t4.get("nameplate"))          # complete dicts (extras kept); drawn as Items by t4_build_items
    t4["corner_list"] = t4_corner_list_cfg(t4.get("corner_list"))
    t4["tiles"] = t4_tiles_cfg(t4.get("tiles")) or {"on": False}      # complete groups (extras kept), drawn as Items by t4_build_items
    t4["cards"] = t4_cards_cfg(t4.get("cards")) or {"on": False}      # complete image cards (extras kept), drawn as Items by t4_build_items
    t4["scenes"] = t4_scenes_cfg(t4.get("scenes")) or {"on": False}
    t4_compose_fg(cfg, tmp)


def t4_build_items(cfg, duration, splits, lbox, rbox):
    return t4_caption_items(cfg, duration) + t4_nameplate_items(cfg, duration) + t4_tiles_items(cfg, duration) + t4_cards_items(cfg, duration) + t4_scenes_items(cfg, duration)


====================================================================================================
FILE: mt\templates\t4\scenes_widgets.py
====================================================================================================
"""mt.templates.t4.scenes_widgets - Template 4 scenes: widget 'code_window' (refs R5 portrait / R6 landscape, measured in T4_P15_ADDENDUM).
[ red yellow green dots | tab: file icon + file name ]     tab bar (49 px at R5 size)
  1  def create_better_future():                           line numbers (gutter) + syntax-coloured monospace code
  2      return "Learn â€¢ Build â€¢ Grow"
spec keys (all optional): file 'main.py' (tab name; the extension picks the icon: .py / .js / .html / other), lang '' ('py' | 'js' | 'plain';
  '' = from the extension; python and javascript rules), code (str with \\n, or a list of lines; missing = the R5 demo snippet), font '' (path;
  '' = first monospace font found: Consolas, Cascadia Mono, Source Code Pro, Ubuntu Mono, Lucida Console, DejaVu Sans Mono, Courier New, else the
  default font), numbers True (line numbers), reveal 'none' | 'typewriter' (one Item per non-empty line, 'reveal' anim, staggered),
  line_step 0.35 (s between lines), line_dur 0.4 (s one line takes to be typed).
Everything scales with u = min(box.w / 434, box.h / 242) (R5 window = 434 x 242 px). The code font shrinks (down to 60 %) when a line is too
wide or there are too many lines; more lines than fit are cut with one t4_warn. Characters the monospace font lacks (the bullet) and Arabic
runs are drawn with the default font per run (Arabic shaped with mt.fonts.shape); the line stays left-aligned at the code column.
The static window (frame + tab + gutter) is ONE cached sprite. 'typewriter': the lines are NOT in it; they sit in sprite.info['lines'] =
[(line sprite, x, y)] (top-left in sprite coords) with info['line_step'] / ['line_dur'] and the engine adds one Item per line through
t4_widget_line_items(). Public: T4_CODE_WINDOW, t4_tokenize(line, lang), t4_code_window(spec, box, ctx), t4_widget_line_items(...),
t4_ask_code_window()."""
import os
import re
import textwrap

from PIL import Image, ImageChops, ImageDraw

from mt.common import ask, ask_choice
from mt.draw import Item
from mt.fonts import default_font, find_font, load_font, shape
from mt.templates.t4.kit import glass_panel, kit_paste
from mt.templates.t4.nameplate import _bool, _cached, _flt
from mt.templates.t4.scenes_reg import register_widget
from mt.templates.t4.warn import t4_warn

try:
    from mt.fonts import _font_cmap
except ImportError:                                            # pragma: no cover - older mt.fonts
    def _font_cmap(path):
        return None

R5_CODE = 'def create_better_future():\n    return "Learn â€¢ Build â€¢ Grow"\n\n# Eduvia\nprint(create_better_future())'
T4_CODE_WINDOW = {"file": "main.py", "lang": "", "code": R5_CODE, "font": "", "numbers": True, "reveal": "none", "line_step": 0.35, "line_dur": 0.4}
MAX_LINES = 16
BASE_W, BASE_H = 434.0, 242.0                                   # R5 window; every size below is px at that scale (x u)
BAR_H, DOT_D, DOT_X, DOT_PITCH, TAB_X = 49, 16, 25, 24, 99
FONT_PX, PITCH_K, TOP_PAD, CODE_X, NUM_X = 18, 1.65, 24, 89, 50
TINT_BODY, BORDER, BAR_RGB, TAB_RGB, DIVIDER = (10, 20, 36, 235), (27, 58, 85), (15, 29, 49), (8, 17, 32), (4, 9, 18)
DOTS = ((255, 95, 86), (255, 189, 46), (39, 201, 63))
NAME_RGB, NUM_RGBA, GUIDE_RGB = (142, 232, 240), (143, 201, 221, 178), (36, 64, 90)
COLORS = {"kw_a": (255, 210, 31), "kw_b": (255, 92, 225), "call_b": (47, 140, 255), "str": (255, 201, 60), "com": (107, 224, 74),
          "num": (255, 158, 74), "fn": (242, 246, 250), "text": (242, 246, 250)}
MONO_NAMES = ("consola", "CascadiaMono", "SourceCodePro-Regular", "UbuntuMono-Regular", "lucon", "DejaVuSansMono", "cour")
_CW, _MONO = {}, {}


# ---- tokenizer -------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
_NUM = r"(?P<num>\b\d[\d_]*(?:\.\d+)?\b)"
_TAIL = _NUM + r"|(?P<id>[A-Za-z_$][\w$]*)|(?P<sp>\s+)|(?P<other>.)"
_TOK = {"py": re.compile(r"(?P<com>#.*)|(?P<str>[rbfRBF]{0,2}(?:\"(?:\\.|[^\"\\])*\"?|'(?:\\.|[^'\\])*'?))|" + _TAIL),
        "js": re.compile(r"(?P<com>//.*|/\*.*?(?:\*/|$))|(?P<str>\"(?:\\.|[^\"\\])*\"?|'(?:\\.|[^'\\])*'?|`(?:\\.|[^`\\])*`?)|" + _TAIL)}
_KW = {"py": ({"def", "class", "lambda"}, {"return", "if", "elif", "else", "for", "while", "import", "from", "as", "in", "not", "and", "or", "is",
                                           "try", "except", "finally", "with", "raise", "pass", "break", "continue", "yield", "None", "True", "False"}),
       "js": ({"const", "let", "var", "function", "class"}, {"return", "if", "else", "for", "while", "import", "from", "export", "new", "typeof",
                                                             "await", "async", "of", "in", "switch", "case", "break", "continue", "throw", "try",
                                                             "catch", "default", "null", "true", "false", "undefined"})}
_DEFWORD = {"py": {"def", "class"}, "js": {"function", "class"}}
_BUILTIN = {"py": {"print", "len", "range", "input", "int", "str", "float", "open", "list", "dict", "set", "sum", "min", "max", "enumerate", "zip", "type"},
            "js": {"log", "warn", "error", "info", "require", "fetch", "parseInt", "parseFloat", "setTimeout", "alert"}}


def _next_char(toks, i):
    for t, k in toks[i + 1:]:
        if k != "sp":
            return t[0]
    return ""


def t4_tokenize(line, lang):
    """One code line -> [(text, kind)], kind in kw_a / kw_b / fn / call_b / str / com / num / text. Unknown lang = no colouring. Never raises."""
    line = line if isinstance(line, str) else ""
    if lang not in _TOK:
        return [(line, "text")] if line else []
    ka, kb = _KW[lang]
    toks = [(m.group(), m.lastgroup) for m in _TOK[lang].finditer(line)]
    out, prev = [], ""
    for i, (t, k) in enumerate(toks):
        kind = k if k in ("str", "com", "num") else "text"
        if k == "id":
            kind = ("kw_a" if t in ka else "kw_b" if t in kb else "fn" if prev in _DEFWORD[lang]
                    else "call_b" if t in _BUILTIN[lang] and _next_char(toks, i) == "(" else "text")
        out.append((t, kind))
        if k != "sp":
            prev = t if k == "id" else ""
    return out


# ---- spec -------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
def _lang(raw, name):
    v = raw.strip().lower() if isinstance(raw, str) else ""
    alias = {"py": "py", "python": "py", "js": "js", "javascript": "js", "ts": "js", "typescript": "js", "jsx": "js", "plain": "plain", "text": "plain"}
    if v in alias:
        return alias[v]
    if v:
        t4_warn("code_window: unknown lang '%s' - taken from the file extension." % v)
    ext = os.path.splitext(name)[1].lower()
    return "py" if ext == ".py" else "js" if ext in (".js", ".mjs", ".ts", ".jsx", ".tsx", ".json") else "plain"


def _code_lines(code):
    if isinstance(code, (list, tuple)):
        code = "\n".join(str(x) for x in code)
    code = str(code).replace("\r\n", "\n").replace("\r", "\n").expandtabs(4)
    lines = [ln.rstrip() for ln in textwrap.dedent(code).rstrip("\n").split("\n")]
    if len(lines) > MAX_LINES:
        t4_warn("code_window: %d lines - only the first %d are used." % (len(lines), MAX_LINES))
        lines = lines[:MAX_LINES]
    return lines


def _spec(raw):
    """Complete, clean spec (never raises; junk -> defaults); out['_lines'] = the code lines."""
    raw = raw if isinstance(raw, dict) else {}
    out = dict(T4_CODE_WINDOW)
    f = raw.get("file")
    out["file"] = re.split(r"[\\/]", f.strip())[-1] if isinstance(f, str) and f.strip() else out["file"]
    out["lang"] = _lang(raw.get("lang"), out["file"])
    fo = raw.get("font")
    out["font"] = fo.strip() if isinstance(fo, str) else ""
    out["numbers"] = _bool(raw.get("numbers"), True)
    r = raw.get("reveal")
    out["reveal"] = "typewriter" if r is True or (isinstance(r, str) and r.strip().lower() in ("typewriter", "type", "reveal", "line", "lines")) else "none"
    out["line_step"], out["line_dur"] = _flt(raw.get("line_step"), 0.0, 5.0, 0.35), _flt(raw.get("line_dur"), 0.05, 5.0, 0.4)
    code = raw.get("code") if "code" in raw and isinstance(raw.get("code"), (str, list, tuple)) else R5_CODE
    out["code"] = code
    out["_lines"] = _code_lines(code)
    return out


# ---- fonts / runs -----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
_AR = "\u0600-\u06FF\u0750-\u077F\u08A0-\u08FF\uFB50-\uFDFF\uFE70-\uFEFF"
_AR_RUN = re.compile("[%s]+(?:[ ]+[%s]+)*" % (_AR, _AR))


def _mono_font(path):
    """Monospace font path: the given one, else the first of MONO_NAMES found, else the default font (one warning each)."""
    if path in _MONO:
        return _MONO[path]
    found = None
    if path and os.path.isfile(path):
        found = path
    else:
        if path:
            t4_warn("code_window: font '%s' not found - looking for a monospace font." % os.path.basename(path))
        for n in MONO_NAMES:
            try:
                p = find_font(n)
            except Exception:
                p = None
            if p and os.path.isfile(p):
                found = p
                break
        if not found:
            t4_warn("code_window: no monospace font found - using the default font (put e.g. SourceCodePro-Regular.ttf in fonts/).")
            found = default_font()
    _MONO[path] = found
    return found


def _mono_runs(text, mono, fb):
    cm, out = _font_cmap(mono) if mono else None, []
    for ch in text:
        p = mono if (not cm or ord(ch) in cm or ch.isspace()) else fb
        if out and out[-1][1] == p:
            out[-1] = (out[-1][0] + ch, p, False)
        else:
            out.append((ch, p, False))
    return out


def _split_runs(text, mono, fb):
    """-> [(text, font path, arabic)]: Arabic runs -> fb (shaped when drawn); chars the monospace font lacks -> fb; the rest -> mono."""
    out, pos = [], 0
    for m in _AR_RUN.finditer(text):
        out += _mono_runs(text[pos:m.start()], mono, fb) + [(m.group(), fb, True)]
        pos = m.end()
    return out + _mono_runs(text[pos:], mono, fb)


def _line_runs(line, lang, mono, fb):
    return [(t, p, ar, COLORS[kind]) for text, kind in t4_tokenize(line, lang) for t, p, ar in _split_runs(text, mono, fb)]


def _measure(runs, size):
    w = 0.0
    for t, p, ar, _ in runs:
        f = load_font(p, size)
        w += f.getlength(shape(t) if ar else t)          # load_font first: shape() reads the font that was loaded last
    return w


def _draw_runs(d, x, base, runs, size):
    for t, p, ar, c in runs:
        f = load_font(p, size)
        s = shape(t) if ar else t
        d.text((x, base), s, font=f, fill=c + (255,), anchor="ls")
        x += f.getlength(s)
    return x


def _baseline(font, pitch):
    asc, desc = font.getmetrics()
    return pitch / 2.0 + (asc - desc) / 2.0


# ---- drawing helpers --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
def _shape_layer(size, paint, k=4):
    """RGBA layer drawn at k x and scaled down (anti-aliased). paint(draw, k)."""
    big = Image.new("RGBA", (size[0] * k, size[1] * k), (0, 0, 0, 0))
    paint(ImageDraw.Draw(big), k)
    return big.resize(size, Image.LANCZOS)


def _file_icon(name, size):
    """Simple vector file icon by extension: .py two squares, .js yellow 'JS', .html orange '<>', else a page glyph."""
    ext = os.path.splitext(name)[1].lower()

    def paint(d, k):
        S = size * k
        if ext == ".py":
            d.rounded_rectangle((.04 * S, .04 * S, .64 * S, .64 * S), radius=.18 * S, fill=(62, 142, 222, 255))
            d.rounded_rectangle((.36 * S, .36 * S, .96 * S, .96 * S), radius=.18 * S, fill=(255, 212, 59, 255))
            for cx, cy in ((.22, .22), (.78, .78)):
                d.ellipse(((cx - .05) * S, (cy - .05) * S, (cx + .05) * S, (cy + .05) * S), fill=(255, 255, 255, 255))
        elif ext in (".js", ".mjs"):
            d.rounded_rectangle((.04 * S, .04 * S, .96 * S, .96 * S), radius=.12 * S, fill=(247, 223, 30, 255))
            d.text((.92 * S, .90 * S), "JS", font=load_font(default_font(), max(8, int(.46 * S))), fill=(30, 30, 30, 255), anchor="rs")
        elif ext in (".html", ".htm"):
            w = max(2, int(.10 * S))
            for pts in (((.38, .22), (.08, .5), (.38, .78)), ((.62, .22), (.92, .5), (.62, .78))):
                d.line([(x * S, y * S) for x, y in pts], fill=(240, 110, 40, 255), width=w, joint="curve")
        else:
            d.polygon([(.2 * S, .05 * S), (.62 * S, .05 * S), (.82 * S, .25 * S), (.82 * S, .95 * S), (.2 * S, .95 * S)], fill=(200, 215, 230, 255))
            d.polygon([(.62 * S, .05 * S), (.82 * S, .25 * S), (.62 * S, .25 * S)], fill=(140, 160, 180, 255))
    return _shape_layer((size, size), paint)


def _window(spr, g, w, h, u):
    cut = 14 * u
    kit_paste(spr, glass_panel((w, h), 0, TINT_BODY, BORDER, (cut, cut, 0, 0), max(1.0, 1.3 * u), 0), g, g)


def _tab_bar(spr, g, w, bh, u, name, ui):
    """Slightly lighter bar (kept inside the 1 px border), divider, three dots, tab with icon + file name."""
    b = max(1, int(round(1.5 * u)))
    a = spr.getchannel("A").crop((g, g, g + w, g + bh))
    inner = Image.new("L", (w, bh), 0)
    ImageDraw.Draw(inner).rectangle((b, b, w - 1 - b, bh), fill=255)
    layer = Image.new("RGBA", (w, bh), BAR_RGB + (255,))
    layer.putalpha(ImageChops.multiply(a, inner))
    spr.alpha_composite(layer, (g, g))
    ImageDraw.Draw(spr).rectangle((g + b, g + bh - 1, g + w - 1 - b, g + bh - 1), fill=DIVIDER + (255,))

    def dots(d, k):
        for i, c in enumerate(DOTS):
            cx, cy, r = (DOT_X + i * DOT_PITCH) * u * k, bh / 2.0 * k, DOT_D / 2.0 * u * k
            d.ellipse((cx - r, cy - r, cx + r, cy + r), fill=c + (255,))
    spr.alpha_composite(_shape_layer((int(round((DOT_X + 2 * DOT_PITCH + DOT_D) * u)), bh), dots), (g, g))
    f = load_font(ui, max(8, int(round(23 * u))))
    label = shape(name)
    isz, pad, gap = int(round(34 * u)), int(round(12 * u)), int(round(8 * u))
    tw = max(int(round(151 * u)), int(round(pad * 2 + gap + isz + f.getlength(label))))
    tx, r = g + int(round(TAB_X * u)), 7 * u
    spr.alpha_composite(_shape_layer((tw, bh), lambda d, k: d.rounded_rectangle((0, 0, tw * k - 1, (bh + r) * k), radius=r * k, fill=TAB_RGB + (255,))), (tx, g))
    spr.alpha_composite(_file_icon(name, isz), (tx + pad, g + (bh - isz) // 2))
    ImageDraw.Draw(spr).text((tx + pad + isz + gap, g + bh / 2.0), label, font=f, fill=NAME_RGB + (255,), anchor="lm")


def _levels(lines):
    """Indent level per line (4 spaces = 1); a blank line keeps the level of the line above (R5 line 3)."""
    out, prev = [], 0
    for ln in lines:
        if ln.strip():
            prev = (len(ln) - len(ln.lstrip(" "))) // 4
        out.append(prev)
    return out


def _gutter(spr, lines, g, top, pitch, size, u, mono, numbers, code_x):
    d, f = ImageDraw.Draw(spr), load_font(mono, size)
    base, adv, gw = _baseline(f, pitch), f.getlength(" "), max(1, int(round(u)))
    for i, lv in enumerate(_levels(lines)):
        y = top + i * pitch
        if numbers:
            d.text((g + NUM_X * u, y + base), str(i + 1), font=f, fill=NUM_RGBA, anchor="rs")
        for j in range(lv):
            x = int(round(code_x + j * 4 * adv + 2 * u))
            d.rectangle((x, y, x + gw - 1, y + pitch - 1), fill=GUIDE_RGB + (255,))


def _fit(rows, w, h, u, mono):
    """-> (font px, line pitch, lines kept): the code font shrinks (>= 60 %) for width / height, then lines are cut."""
    size, n = max(8, int(round(FONT_PX * u))), len(rows)
    if not n:
        return size, int(round(PITCH_K * size)), 0
    avail_w, avail_h = w - (CODE_X + 14) * u, h - (BAR_H + TOP_PAD + 12) * u
    k = min(1.0, avail_w / max(1.0, max(_measure(r, size) for r in rows)), avail_h / (n * PITCH_K * size))
    size = max(8, int(round(size * max(0.6, k))))
    pitch = int(round(PITCH_K * size))
    fit = max(1, int(avail_h // pitch))
    if n > fit:
        t4_warn("code_window: only %d of %d lines fit the window - the rest are cut." % (fit, n))
        n = fit
    return size, pitch, n


def _line_sprite(runs, size, pitch, maxw, mono):
    """One code line as its own sprite (clipped at maxw); None for a blank line."""
    if not runs:
        return None
    w = int(min(maxw, _measure(runs, size) + 4))
    if w < 2:
        return None
    im = Image.new("RGBA", (w, pitch), (0, 0, 0, 0))
    _draw_runs(ImageDraw.Draw(im), 0.0, _baseline(load_font(mono, size), pitch), runs, size)
    return im


# ---- build ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
def _build(sp, w, h, u, mono, fb, ui):
    g, lang = max(2, int(round(3 * u))), sp["lang"]
    rows = [_line_runs(t, lang, mono, fb) for t in sp["_lines"]]
    size, pitch, n = _fit(rows, w, h, u, mono)
    rows, lines = rows[:n], sp["_lines"][:n]
    spr = Image.new("RGBA", (w + 2 * g, h + 2 * g), (0, 0, 0, 0))
    bh = int(round(BAR_H * u))
    _window(spr, g, w, h, u)
    _tab_bar(spr, g, w, bh, u, sp["file"], ui)
    top, cx0 = g + bh + int(round(TOP_PAD * u)), g + int(round(CODE_X * u))
    _gutter(spr, lines, g, top, pitch, size, u, mono, sp["numbers"], cx0)
    maxw = g + w - 8 * u - cx0
    parts = [(_line_sprite(r, size, pitch, maxw, mono), cx0, top + i * pitch) for i, r in enumerate(rows)]
    parts = [p for p in parts if p[0] is not None]
    if sp["reveal"] == "typewriter":
        spr.info["lines"] = parts
    else:
        for ls, x, y in parts:
            spr.alpha_composite(ls, (x, y))
    spr.info.update({"origin": (g, g), "line_step": sp["line_step"], "line_dur": sp["line_dur"]})
    return spr


def t4_code_window(spec, box, ctx):
    """Widget function: code window sprite for the slot `box` = (x, y, w, h) canvas px. sprite.info['origin'] = the box's top-left; cached sprite
    (do not draw into it). 'typewriter': code lines are in sprite.info['lines'] (see t4_widget_line_items)."""
    sp = _spec(spec)
    w, h = max(120, int(round(box[2]))), max(80, int(round(box[3])))
    u = min(w / BASE_W, h / BASE_H)
    mono, ui = _mono_font(sp["font"]), default_font()
    fb = ui if ui else mono
    key = (tuple(sorted((k, str(v)) for k, v in sp.items())), w, h, round(u, 3), str(mono), str(fb))
    return _cached(_CW, key, lambda: _build(sp, w, h, u, mono, fb, ui), 6)


def t4_widget_line_items(sp, cx, cy, start, end, delay):
    """Typewriter Items of a widget sprite: one 'reveal' Item per line, `delay` s after `start` (the window's fade-in), then line_step apart.
    (cx, cy) = the centre of the placed widget sprite. A sprite without info['lines'] -> []. Never raises."""
    out = []
    try:
        lines = sp.info.get("lines") or []
        step, dur = float(sp.info.get("line_step", 0.35)), float(sp.info.get("line_dur", 0.4))
        last = max(start, end - 0.1)
        anim = sp.info.get("line_anim", "reveal")
        for i, (ls, x, y) in enumerate(lines):
            t0 = min(start + delay + i * step, last)
            out.append(Item(ls, t0, end, cx - sp.width / 2.0 + x + ls.width / 2.0, cy - sp.height / 2.0 + y + ls.height / 2.0, anim, dur, out=True))            
    except Exception as e:
        t4_warn("code_window: typewriter lines could not be built (%s: %s)." % (type(e).__name__, e))
    return out


# ---- questions --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
def t4_ask_code_window():
    """Question group of the widget (called by t4_ask_scenes) -> spec dict. Font / lang / numbers: edit the job file."""
    d = T4_CODE_WINDOW
    out = {"file": ask("File name shown in the tab (.py / .js / .html pick the icon and colours)", default=d["file"], key="t4_cw_file")}
    code = ask("Code (Enter = the demo snippet; write \\n between lines)", key="t4_cw_code", allow_empty=True) or ""
    if code.strip():
        out["code"] = code.replace("\\n", "\n")
    out["reveal"] = ["none", "typewriter"][ask_choice("Code appears", ["All at once", "Line by line (typewriter)"], default=1, key="t4_cw_reveal") - 1]
    return out


register_widget("code_window", t4_code_window, ask=t4_ask_code_window, slot="code")


====================================================================================================
FILE: mt\templates\t4\scenes_title.py
====================================================================================================
"""mt.templates.t4.scenes_title - Template 4 scenes: widget 'title_block' (refs R5 portrait / R6 landscape, measured in T4_P14_ADDENDUM).
[ </> icon (in a hex-cut badge on portrait) ]  TWO-TONE TITLE        row centred on the icon, text left-aligned (RTL: mirrored)
                                               T R A C K E D   S U B T I T L E
spec keys (all optional): title 'Web Development', subtitle 'Building Digital Solutions', accent '' (word(s) of the title to colour; ''
  = the last word, a one-word title = all of it), icon 'code' (kit icon name, ''/'none' = no icon), color '#14F1FA' (accent + icon + badge),
  text_color '#FFFFFF', badge None (auto: portrait yes / landscape no) | True | False, panel False (dark glass plate over the whole slot,
  R5), font '' (path; '' = project default), caps True, size None (title font px at short side 1080; None = from the slot height),
  tracking 'auto' (subtitle tracking in em; 'auto' = fit the subtitle to 0.95 x the title width, as R5 / R6) | a number, rtl None (auto).
Everything is derived from the slot box: icon ~ 0.62 h (badge 0.65 h), title font ~ 0.39 h (badge) / 0.64 h (bare), shrunk to fit the
width left of the icon (min 55 %, then cut with an ellipsis), subtitle font 0.465 x title. Arabic / RTL: no tracking, words shaped one by one.
One static sprite per (spec, size), cached. Public: T4_TITLE_BLOCK, t4_title_block(spec, box, ctx), t4_ask_title_block()."""
import os

from PIL import Image, ImageColor, ImageDraw, ImageFilter

from mt.common import ask, ask_choice, c_color, yn
from mt.draw import t2_rgb
from mt.fonts import default_font, is_rtl_text, load_font, shape
from mt.templates.t4.kit import glass_panel, icon, kit_paste, neon_icon
from mt.templates.t4.nameplate import _cached, _flt
from mt.templates.t4.nameplate_tags import _is_bold
from mt.templates.t4.scenes_reg import register_widget
from mt.templates.t4.warn import t4_warn

T4_TITLE_BLOCK = {"title": "Web Development", "subtitle": "Building Digital Solutions", "accent": "", "icon": "code", "color": "#14F1FA",
                  "text_color": "#FFFFFF", "badge": None, "panel": False, "font": "", "caps": True, "size": None, "tracking": "auto", "rtl": None}
GAP_ICON, GAP_LINES, PANEL_PAD, BARE_PAD, GLOW_PAD = 32.0, 20.0, 21.0, 4.0, 40.0      # px at short side 1080
ICON_H = {True: 0.65, False: 0.62}          # icon block height / slot height (badge / bare glyph)
TITLE_K = {True: 0.39, False: 0.64}         # title font px / slot height: 86 / 220 (R5), 74 / 116 (R6)
SUB_K, SUB_FIT = 0.465, 0.95                # subtitle font = 0.465 x title; subtitle width = 0.95 x title width
BADGE_ASPECT, GLYPH_IN_BADGE = 175 / 143.0, (0.82, 0.60)     # R5 badge 175 x 143; glyph 144 x 86 inside it (share of the badge w / h)
TINT_BADGE, TINT_PANEL = (4, 10, 26, 235), (5, 12, 30, 200)
_TB = {}


# ---- spec -------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
def _yes(v, default):
    if isinstance(v, bool):
        return v
    if isinstance(v, (int, float)):
        return bool(v)
    s = str(v).strip().lower() if isinstance(v, str) else ""
    return True if s in ("1", "true", "yes", "y", "on") else False if s in ("0", "false", "no", "n", "off") else default


def _color(v, default):
    """'#rrggbb' / name / (r, g, b) -> kept; anything Pillow cannot read -> default (t2_rgb would fall back to the poster lime)."""
    try:
        if isinstance(v, (tuple, list)) and len(v) >= 3:
            return tuple(int(c) for c in v[:3])
        return v if isinstance(v, str) and ImageColor.getrgb(v.strip()) else default
    except (ValueError, TypeError):
        return default


def _spec(raw):
    """Complete, clean spec (never raises; junk -> defaults)."""
    raw = raw if isinstance(raw, dict) else {}
    out = dict(T4_TITLE_BLOCK)
    for k in ("title", "subtitle", "accent", "font"):
        v = raw.get(k, out[k])
        out[k] = str(v).strip() if isinstance(v, (str, int, float)) and not isinstance(v, bool) else out[k]
    ic = raw.get("icon", out["icon"])
    ic = str(ic).strip().lower() if isinstance(ic, str) else out["icon"]
    out["icon"] = "" if ic in ("", "none", "off", "-", "false") else ic
    for k in ("color", "text_color"):
        out[k] = _color(raw.get(k), out[k])
    b = raw.get("badge")
    out["badge"] = None if b is None or (isinstance(b, str) and b.strip().lower() in ("", "auto")) else _yes(b, False)
    out["panel"], out["caps"] = _yes(raw.get("panel"), False), _yes(raw.get("caps"), True)
    sz = raw.get("size")
    out["size"] = _flt(sz, 8, 600, 0.0) or None if sz is not None else None
    tr = raw.get("tracking", "auto")
    out["tracking"] = "auto" if isinstance(tr, str) and tr.strip().lower() in ("", "auto") else _flt(tr, -0.1, 1.0, 0.21)
    r = raw.get("rtl")
    out["rtl"] = None if r is None else _yes(r, False)
    return out


def _tokens(title, accent):
    """[(word, is_accent)]: accent = the given word(s) (case-insensitive, must match whole consecutive words), else the last word."""
    words = title.split()
    flags = [False] * len(words)
    acc = accent.split() if accent else []
    if acc:
        low, n = [w.casefold() for w in words], len(acc)
        for i in range(len(words) - n + 1):
            if low[i:i + n] == [a.casefold() for a in acc]:
                flags[i:i + n] = [True] * n
                break
        else:
            t4_warn("title_block: accent '%s' is not a word of the title - using the last word." % accent)
    if not any(flags) and words:
        flags[-1] = True                       # default: the last word (a one-word title = all of it)
    return list(zip(words, flags))


# ---- text -------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
def _put(d, x, y, text, font, fill, track, rtl, stroke):
    """Draw text with its baseline at y, left edge x. track (px) > 0 / < 0 and LTR -> char by char; otherwise one run (kerning / shaping kept)."""
    kw = {"font": font, "fill": fill, "anchor": "ls", "stroke_width": stroke, "stroke_fill": fill}
    if track and not rtl:
        for ch in text:
            d.text((x, y), ch, **kw)
            x += font.getlength(ch) + track
    else:
        d.text((x, y), text, **kw)


def _words(tokens, font, track, rtl):
    """-> ([(shaped word, accent, offset from the start edge, width)], total width). RTL words run right to left (offset from the right edge)."""
    gap, out, off = font.getlength(" ") + track, [], 0.0
    for word, acc in tokens:
        if track and not rtl:
            sh, ww = word, sum(font.getlength(c) for c in word) + track * (len(word) - 1)
        else:
            sh = shape(word)
            ww = font.getlength(sh)
        out.append((sh, acc, off, ww))
        off += ww + gap
    return out, (off - gap if out else 0.0)


def _fit_title(tokens, fp, base, avail, track_em, rtl):
    """Shrink the font (down to 55 %), then cut the last word with an ellipsis. -> (font, size, words, width, tokens)."""
    k, tokens = 1.0, list(tokens)
    while True:
        fs = max(8, int(round(base * k)))
        font = load_font(fp, fs)
        words, tw = _words(tokens, font, track_em * fs, rtl)
        if tw <= avail or k <= 0.55 + 1e-9:
            break
        k = max(0.55, k - 0.05)
    for _ in range(200):
        if tw <= avail or not tokens:
            break
        w, a = tokens[-1]
        tokens[-1] = (w.rstrip("â€¦")[:-1] + "â€¦", a) if len(w.rstrip("â€¦")) > 1 else None
        if tokens[-1] is None:
            tokens.pop()
        words, tw = _words(tokens, font, track_em * fs, rtl)
    return font, fs, words, tw, tokens


def _sub_metrics(text, font, fs, tw, track_em, rtl):
    """-> (shaped text, tracking px, width). Tracking: given em, else fitted so the width = SUB_FIT x the title width (0.04 .. 0.45 em)."""
    if rtl or len(text) < 2:
        sh = shape(text)
        return sh, 0.0, font.getlength(sh)
    if track_em is None and tw <= 0:
        track_em = 0.21                                    # no title to fit to
    adv, n = sum(font.getlength(c) for c in text), len(text) - 1
    tr = track_em * fs if track_em is not None else min(max((tw * SUB_FIT - adv) / n, 0.04 * fs), 0.45 * fs)
    return text, tr, adv + tr * n


def _fit_sub(text, fp, base, avail, tw, track_em, rtl):
    """Subtitle font shrinks (55 %) and the text is cut with an ellipsis until it fits `avail`. -> (font, fs, shaped, tracking px, width)."""
    k = 1.0
    while True:
        fs = max(8, int(round(base * k)))
        font = load_font(fp, fs)
        sh, tr, w = _sub_metrics(text, font, fs, tw, track_em, rtl)
        if w <= avail or k <= 0.55 + 1e-9:
            break
        k = max(0.55, k - 0.05)
    for _ in range(200):
        if w <= avail or len(text) <= 2:
            break
        text = text.rstrip("â€¦")[:-1] + "â€¦"
        sh, tr, w = _sub_metrics(text, font, fs, tw, track_em, rtl)
    return font, fs, sh, tr, w


def _cap(font):
    return float(-font.getbbox("H", anchor="ls")[1])


def _text_block(spr, sp, g, x0, x1, cy, avail, h, s, rtl, fp, col, txt):
    """Title (two-tone, faint glow on the accent) + tracked subtitle. x0 / x1 = text area left / right edge in slot coords."""
    caps = sp["caps"] and not rtl
    title, sub = (sp["title"].upper() if caps else sp["title"]), (sp["subtitle"].upper() if caps else sp["subtitle"])
    base = sp["size"] * s if sp["size"] else TITLE_K[sp["_badge"]] * h
    font, fs, words, tw, _ = _fit_title(_tokens(title, sp["accent"]), fp, base, avail, 0.0, rtl)
    sfont, sfs, ssh, tr, sw = _fit_sub(sub, fp, SUB_K * fs, avail, tw, None if sp["tracking"] == "auto" else sp["tracking"], rtl) if sub else (None,) * 5
    ct = _cap(font)
    cs = _cap(sfont) if sub else 0.0
    gap = GAP_LINES * s if sub else 0.0
    top = cy - (ct + gap + cs) / 2.0
    by, sy = g + top + ct, g + top + ct + gap + cs
    stroke = 0 if _is_bold(fp) else max(1, int(round(fs * 0.025)))
    xa, xb = g + x0, g + x1
    if title:
        m = Image.new("L", spr.size, 0)
        _draw_words(ImageDraw.Draw(m), words, font, xa, xb, by, rtl, 0.0, (255, 255), stroke, True)
        k = max(2.0, fs * 0.12)
        gl = m.filter(ImageFilter.GaussianBlur(k)).point(lambda v: int(min(255, v * 0.8)))
        glow = Image.new("RGBA", spr.size, col + (0,))
        glow.putalpha(gl)
        spr.alpha_composite(glow)
        _draw_words(ImageDraw.Draw(spr), words, font, xa, xb, by, rtl, 0.0, (txt + (255,), col + (255,)), stroke, False)
    if sub:
        sstroke = 0 if _is_bold(fp) else (1 if sfs >= 28 else 0)
        _put(ImageDraw.Draw(spr), (xb - sw) if rtl else xa, sy, ssh, sfont, txt + (255,), tr, rtl, sstroke)
    return tw


def _draw_words(d, words, font, x0, x1, base_y, rtl, track, fills, stroke, only_accent):
    for sh, acc, off, ww in words:
        if only_accent and not acc:
            continue
        _put(d, (x1 - off - ww) if rtl else (x0 + off), base_y, sh, font, fills[1 if acc else 0], track, rtl, stroke)


# ---- icon / badge / panel ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
def _glyph(name, th, mw, col, glow):
    """Neon icon sized so its VISIBLE glyph is th high (and at most mw wide). -> (sprite, (glyph centre x, y) from the icon's top-left, glyph w, glyph h)
    or None (unknown icon: the kit already warned)."""
    bb = icon(name, 100, (255, 255, 255)).getchannel("A").point(lambda v: 255 if v > 40 else 0).getbbox()
    if not bb or bb[2] <= bb[0] or bb[3] <= bb[1]:
        return None
    gw, gh = bb[2] - bb[0], bb[3] - bb[1]
    size = max(8, int(round(100.0 * min(th / gh, mw / gw))))
    k = size / 100.0
    return neon_icon(name, size, col, max(4.0, size * 0.10)), ((bb[0] + bb[2]) / 2.0 * k, (bb[1] + bb[3]) / 2.0 * k), gw * k, gh * k


def _icon_block(spr, sp, g, left, right, cy, h, s, col, badge, rtl):
    """Badge (portrait) or bare glyph, centred on cy. Block is placed at `left` (LTR) or ends at `right` (RTL), slot coords.
    -> (block width, block left edge); width 0 = no icon."""
    ih = ICON_H[badge] * h
    if not sp["icon"]:
        return 0.0, (right if rtl else left)
    if badge:
        bh = ih
        bw = bh * BADGE_ASPECT
        gl = _glyph(sp["icon"], GLYPH_IN_BADGE[1] * bh, GLYPH_IN_BADGE[0] * bw, col, 8 * s)
    else:
        gl = _glyph(sp["icon"], ih, ih * 1.8, col, 8 * s)
        bw = gl[2] if gl else 0.0
    if gl is None:
        return 0.0, (right if rtl else left)
    x = (right - bw) if rtl else left
    if badge:
        cut = 0.28 * bh
        kit_paste(spr, glass_panel((int(round(bw)), int(round(bh))), 0, TINT_BADGE, col, (cut,) * 4, 2.2 * s, 9 * s), g + x, g + cy - bh / 2.0)
    ic, (gcx, gcy), _, _ = gl
    kit_paste(spr, ic, g + x + bw / 2.0 - gcx, g + cy - gcy)
    return bw, x


# ---- build ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
def _build(sp, w, h, s, badge, fp, rtl):
    g = int(round(GLOW_PAD * s))
    col, txt = t2_rgb(sp["color"]), t2_rgb(sp["text_color"])
    spr = Image.new("RGBA", (w + 2 * g, h + 2 * g), (0, 0, 0, 0))
    pad = (PANEL_PAD if (sp["panel"] or badge) else BARE_PAD) * s          # R5: badge 21 px in from the slot edge; R6: glyph 4 px
    if sp["panel"]:
        kit_paste(spr, glass_panel((w, h), 0, TINT_PANEL, col, (20 * s,) * 4, 2.2 * s, 10 * s), g, g)
    cy, pad_i = h / 2.0, (PANEL_PAD if (sp["panel"] or badge) else BARE_PAD) * s            # RTL: the icon sits pad in from the right edge
    iw, ix = _icon_block(spr, sp, g, pad, w - pad_i, cy, h, s, col, badge, rtl)
    gap = GAP_ICON * s if iw else 0.0
    pad_r = PANEL_PAD * s if sp["panel"] else 0.0                          # right inset only inside a plate
    x0, x1 = (pad_r, ix - gap) if rtl else (ix + iw + gap, w - pad_r)    # text area, slot coords
    avail = max(40.0, x1 - x0)
    _text_block(spr, dict(sp, _badge=badge), g, x0, x1, cy, avail, h, s, rtl, fp, col, txt)
    spr.info["origin"], spr.info["rtl"] = (g, g), rtl
    return spr


def t4_title_block(spec, box, ctx):
    """Widget function: title block sprite for the slot `box` = (x, y, w, h) canvas px. None when title and subtitle are both empty.
    sprite.info['origin'] = the slot's top-left inside the sprite; the sprite is cached (do not draw into it)."""
    sp = _spec(spec)
    ctx = ctx if isinstance(ctx, dict) else {}
    if not sp["title"] and not sp["subtitle"]:
        t4_warn("title_block: no title and no subtitle - skipped.")
        return None
    s = float(ctx.get("s") or 1.0)
    w, h = max(40, int(round(box[2]))), max(30, int(round(box[3])))
    badge = sp["badge"] if sp["badge"] is not None else ctx.get("aspect") == "portrait"
    if sp["font"] and not os.path.isfile(sp["font"]):
        t4_warn("title_block: font '%s' not found - using the default font." % os.path.basename(sp["font"]))
    fp = sp["font"] if sp["font"] and os.path.isfile(sp["font"]) else default_font()
    rtl = sp["rtl"] if sp["rtl"] is not None else is_rtl_text(sp["title"] + " " + sp["subtitle"])
    key = (tuple(sorted((k, str(v)) for k, v in sp.items())), w, h, round(s, 3), badge, str(fp), rtl)
    return _cached(_TB, key, lambda: _build(sp, w, h, s, badge, fp, rtl), 8)


# ---- questions --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
def t4_ask_title_block():
    """Question group of the widget (called by t4_ask_scenes) -> spec dict. Badge / panel / font / tracking: edit the job file."""
    d = T4_TITLE_BLOCK
    out = {"title": ask("Title (the last word is coloured)", default=d["title"], key="t4_scene_title"),
           "subtitle": ask("Subtitle (Enter = none)", default=d["subtitle"], key="t4_scene_subtitle", allow_empty=True) or ""}
    out["accent"] = ask("Word(s) to colour (Enter = the last word)", key="t4_scene_accent", allow_empty=True) or ""
    out["color"] = ask("Accent colour", default=d["color"], key="t4_scene_color", cast=c_color)
    b = ask_choice("Hex badge behind the icon", ["Automatic (portrait yes, landscape no)", "Always", "Never"], default=1, key="t4_scene_badge")
    out["badge"] = [None, True, False][b - 1]
    out["panel"] = yn("Dark glass plate behind the whole title block?", default=False)
    return out


register_widget("title_block", t4_title_block, ask=t4_ask_title_block, slot="title")

====================================================================================================
FILE: mt\templates\t4\scenes_card.py
====================================================================================================
"""mt.templates.t4.scenes_card - Template 4 scenes: widget 'info_card' (glass panel: icon + title + subtitle).
spec keys (all optional; px at short side 1080): icon 'globe' (kit name | PNG path | ''/'-'/'none'), title, subtitle,
  color (accent: icon + border), icon_color / border_color ('' = color), glow 10, border 3, fill '#0B1A3A', fill_alpha 0.55,
  title_size 44, sub_size 26, title_color, sub_color, radius 26 | cut 0 (chamfer px, overrides radius), pad 28, gap 24,
  icon_size 0.62 (fraction of inner box height), icon_glow None (auto), align auto|left|right (auto: Arabic -> right), font ''.
Title wraps to <= 2 lines; everything shrinks to 60 % when the panel is taller than the box. Panel is centred vertically in the slot.
Public: T4_CARD, t4_info_card(spec, box, ctx), t4_ask_card()."""
import math

from PIL import Image, ImageDraw

from mt.common import ask, ask_choice
from mt.draw import t2_draw_mask, t2_glow, t2_paint, t2_rgb
from mt.fonts import is_rtl_text, load_font, shape
from mt.templates.t4.kit import kit_paste
from mt.templates.t4.nameplate import _cached, _flt
from mt.templates.t4.scenes_bullets import _cap, _font, _wrap
from mt.templates.t4.scenes_reg import register_widget
from mt.templates.t4.scenes_ring import _icon
from mt.templates.t4.scenes_title import _color
from mt.templates.t4.warn import t4_warn

T4_CARD = {"icon": "globe", "title": "Web Technology", "subtitle": "Build \u2022 Develop \u2022 Deploy",
           "color": "#19D3FF", "glow": 10.0, "border": 3.0, "fill": "#0B1A3A", "fill_alpha": 0.55,
           "title_size": 44.0, "sub_size": 26.0, "title_color": "#FFFFFF", "sub_color": "#9FB8DD",
           "radius": 26.0, "cut": 0.0, "pad": 28.0, "gap": 24.0, "icon_size": 0.62}
_CC = {}


# ---- spec ----------------------------------------------------------------------
def _spec(raw):
    """Complete, clean spec (never raises; junk -> defaults)."""
    raw, d = raw if isinstance(raw, dict) else {}, T4_CARD

    def txt(k):
        v = raw.get(k, d.get(k, ""))
        return v.strip() if isinstance(v, str) else ""
    title, sub, ic = txt("title"), txt("subtitle"), txt("icon")
    al = raw.get("align")
    al = {"left": "left", "l": "left", "ltr": "left", "right": "right", "r": "right", "rtl": "right"}.get(
        al.strip().lower() if isinstance(al, str) else "", "auto")
    if al == "auto":
        al = "right" if (title or sub) and is_rtl_text(title or sub) else "left"
    col = _color(raw.get("color"), d["color"])
    fo = raw.get("font")
    return {"icon": "" if ic.lower() in ("-", "none") else ic, "title": title, "subtitle": sub, "color": col,
            "icon_color": _color(raw.get("icon_color"), col), "border_color": _color(raw.get("border_color"), col),
            "glow": _flt(raw.get("glow"), 0, 60, d["glow"]), "border": _flt(raw.get("border"), 0, 20, d["border"]),
            "fill": _color(raw.get("fill"), d["fill"]), "fill_alpha": _flt(raw.get("fill_alpha"), 0.0, 1.0, d["fill_alpha"]),
            "title_size": _flt(raw.get("title_size"), 8, 200, d["title_size"]),
            "sub_size": _flt(raw.get("sub_size"), 8, 200, d["sub_size"]),
            "title_color": _color(raw.get("title_color"), d["title_color"]),
            "sub_color": _color(raw.get("sub_color"), d["sub_color"]),
            "radius": _flt(raw.get("radius"), 0, 80, d["radius"]), "cut": _flt(raw.get("cut"), 0, 80, d["cut"]),
            "pad": _flt(raw.get("pad"), 0, 120, d["pad"]), "gap": _flt(raw.get("gap"), 0, 120, d["gap"]),
            "icon_size": _flt(raw.get("icon_size"), 0.1, 1.0, d["icon_size"]),
            "icon_glow": None if raw.get("icon_glow") is None else _flt(raw.get("icon_glow"), 0, 60, 8.0),
            "align": al, "font": fo.strip() if isinstance(fo, str) else ""}


# ---- drawing -------------------------------------------------------------------
def _pts(x0, y0, x1, y1, r, cut):
    """Outline polygon: chamfered corners if cut > 0, else rounded corners (r), else a plain rectangle."""
    hw, hh = (x1 - x0) / 2.0, (y1 - y0) / 2.0
    r, c = max(0.0, min(r, hw, hh)), max(0.0, min(cut, hw, hh))
    if c > 0:
        return [(x0 + c, y0), (x1 - c, y0), (x1, y0 + c), (x1, y1 - c), (x1 - c, y1), (x0 + c, y1), (x0, y1 - c), (x0, y0 + c)]
    if r < 1:
        return [(x0, y0), (x1, y0), (x1, y1), (x0, y1)]
    out = []
    for cx, cy, a0 in ((x1 - r, y0 + r, -90), (x1 - r, y1 - r, 0), (x0 + r, y1 - r, 90), (x0 + r, y0 + r, 180)):
        for k in range(9):
            a = math.radians(a0 + 90.0 * k / 8)
            out.append((cx + r * math.cos(a), cy + r * math.sin(a)))
    return out


def _mask(bb, outer, inner=None, clip_y=None):
    """Polygon mask in sprite pixel coords; `inner` is cut out (border ring); below clip_y is cleared (top highlight)."""
    def fn(d, L, s):
        d.polygon([L(x, y) for x, y in outer], fill=255)
        if inner:
            d.polygon([L(x, y) for x, y in inner], fill=0)
        if clip_y is not None:
            a, b = L(bb[0] - 1, clip_y), L(bb[2] + 1, bb[3] + 1)
            d.rectangle((a[0], a[1], b[0], b[1]), fill=0)
    return t2_draw_mask(bb, fn)


def _text(path, txt, size, maxw):
    """-> (font, shaped lines, widths). Wrap + shape right after loading the font (shape() depends on the last loaded font)."""
    f = load_font(path, size)
    shaped = [shape(t) for t in _cap(_wrap(txt, f, maxw), f, maxw)] if txt else []
    return f, shaped, [f.getlength(t) for t in shaped]


def _build(sp, w, h, s):
    if not (sp["title"] or sp["subtitle"] or sp["icon"]):
        t4_warn("info_card: no icon, title or subtitle - skipped.")
        return None
    right, path = sp["align"] == "right", _font(sp["font"])
    pad, gap, bw, glow = sp["pad"] * s, sp["gap"] * s, sp["border"] * s, sp["glow"] * s
    m = int(glow * 2.6 + bw + 6)
    for k in (1.0, 0.85, 0.7, 0.6):
        iw = int(round(sp["icon_size"] * max(8.0, h - 2 * pad) * k)) if sp["icon"] else 0
        avail = max(30.0, w - 2 * pad - (iw + gap if iw else 0))
        tsz, ssz = max(8, int(round(sp["title_size"] * s * k))), max(8, int(round(sp["sub_size"] * s * k)))
        tf, tl, tw = _text(path, sp["title"], tsz, avail)
        sf, sl, sw = _text(path, sp["subtitle"], ssz, avail)
        lht, lhs, sgap = tsz * 1.2, ssz * 1.25, tsz * 0.25
        bh = len(tl) * lht + (len(sl) * lhs + (sgap if tl else 0) if sl else 0)
        ph = max(bh, iw) + 2 * pad
        if ph <= h:
            break
    if ph > h * 1.02:
        t4_warn("info_card: the card is taller than its box (%d > %d px) - give it a bigger slot or less text." % (ph, h))
    ph = min(ph, float(h))
    x0, y0 = float(m), m + (h - ph) / 2.0
    x1, y1 = x0 + w, y0 + ph
    bb = (x0 - 2, y0 - 2, x1 + 2, y1 + 2)
    outer = _pts(x0, y0, x1, y1, sp["radius"] * s, sp["cut"] * s)
    inner = _pts(x0 + bw, y0 + bw, x1 - bw, y1 - bw, max(0.0, sp["radius"] * s - bw), max(0.0, sp["cut"] * s - bw * 0.41))
    spr = Image.new("RGBA", (w + 2 * m, h + 2 * m), (0, 0, 0, 0))
    ring, fill, cb = _mask(bb, outer, inner), _mask(bb, outer), t2_rgb(sp["border_color"])
    if glow > 0 and bw > 0:
        t2_paint(spr, t2_glow(ring, max(2.0, glow * 0.6), 1.1), cb, 0.6)           # glow under the glass
    t2_paint(spr, fill, t2_rgb(sp["fill"]), sp["fill_alpha"])                      # glass body
    t2_paint(spr, _mask(bb, inner, clip_y=y0 + ph * 0.45), (255, 255, 255), 0.07)  # top sheen
    if bw > 0:
        t2_paint(spr, ring, cb)
    if iw:
        ig = iw * 0.12 if sp["icon_glow"] is None else sp["icon_glow"] * s
        ic = _icon({"icon": sp["icon"]}, iw, ig, sp["icon_color"])
        if ic is not None:
            kit_paste(spr, ic, (x1 - pad - iw) if right else (x0 + pad), y0 + ph / 2.0 - iw / 2.0)
    tx, rx = x0 + pad + (iw + gap if iw else 0), x1 - pad - (iw + gap if iw else 0)
    dr, top = ImageDraw.Draw(spr), y0 + (ph - bh) / 2.0
    for f, lines, widths, lh, y, col in ((tf, tl, tw, lht, top, sp["title_color"]),
                                         (sf, sl, sw, lhs, top + len(tl) * lht + (sgap if tl else 0), sp["sub_color"])):
        asc, desc = f.getmetrics()
        for i, (t, wd) in enumerate(zip(lines, widths)):
            base = y + i * lh + (lh - (asc + desc)) / 2.0 + asc
            dr.text(((rx - wd) if right else tx, base), t, font=f, fill=t2_rgb(col) + (255,), anchor="ls")
    spr.info["origin"] = (m, m)
    return spr


def t4_info_card(spec, box, ctx):
    """Widget function: glass panel (icon + title + subtitle) in the slot `box` = (x, y, w, h) -> RGBA sprite (origin = box top-left)."""
    sp = _spec(spec)
    s = float(ctx.get("s", 1.0)) if isinstance(ctx, dict) else 1.0
    w, h = max(120, int(round(box[2]))), max(60, int(round(box[3])))
    return _cached(_CC, (repr(sp), w, h, round(s, 3)), lambda: _build(sp, w, h, s))


# ---- questions -----------------------------------------------------------------
def t4_ask_card():
    """Question group of the widget (called by t4_ask_scenes) -> spec dict."""
    d = T4_CARD
    out = {"icon": ask("Card icon (kit name or PNG path, '-' = none)", default=d["icon"], key="t4_card_icon"),
           "title": ask("Card title", default=d["title"], key="t4_card_title"),
           "subtitle": ask("Card subtitle (empty = none)", default=d["subtitle"], key="t4_card_subtitle")}
    out["align"] = ["auto", "left", "right"][ask_choice("Alignment", ["Auto (Arabic = right)", "Left", "Right"], default=0)]
    return out


register_widget("info_card", t4_info_card, ask=t4_ask_card, slot="card")

====================================================================================================
FILE: mt\templates\t4\scenes_bullets.py
====================================================================================================
"""mt.templates.t4.scenes_bullets - Template 4 scenes: widget 'bullet_list' (cyan glow dot + text rows, staggered; refs R5 / R6).
spec keys (all optional; px at short side 1080): items ['..', ..] (<= 12; missing = demo list), color '#19D3FF' (dot), text_color '#FFFFFF',
  dot 12 (diameter), glow 8 (dot glow), size 34 (font px), gap 22 (between rows), align 'auto' | 'left' | 'right' (auto = right when the first item is
  Arabic / RTL: dot on the right, text right-aligned), max_width None (px; wrap width, default = the box width), font '' (path), anim 'fade' | 'reveal' | 'pop',
  line_step 0.3 (s between rows), line_dur 0.35.
Text wraps to <= 2 lines per row (then '...'); everything shrinks to 60 % when the rows are taller than the box. Arabic goes through mt.fonts.shape.
The static sprite is EMPTY: each row is its own sprite in sprite.info['lines'] (+ 'lines_only', 'ink', 'line_anim', 'line_step', 'line_dur') and the engine adds one
staggered Item per row through t4_widget_line_items(). Public: T4_BULLETS, t4_bullet_list(spec, box, ctx), t4_ask_bullets()."""
import math

from PIL import Image

from mt.common import ask, ask_choice
from mt.draw import t2_draw_mask, t2_glow, t2_lighten, t2_paint, t2_rgb
from mt.fonts import default_font, is_rtl_text, load_font, shape
from mt.templates.t4.nameplate import _cached, _flt
from mt.templates.t4.scenes_reg import register_widget
from mt.templates.t4.scenes_title import _color
from mt.templates.t4.scenes_widgets import _baseline
from mt.templates.t4.warn import t4_warn

T4_BULLETS = {"items": ["Responsive websites", "Fast and secure", "Modern UI / UX"], "color": "#19D3FF", "text_color": "#FFFFFF", "dot": 12.0,
              "glow": 8.0, "size": 34.0, "gap": 22.0, "align": "auto", "max_width": None, "font": "", "anim": "fade", "line_step": 0.3, "line_dur": 0.35}
MAX_ITEMS = 12
_BC = {}


# ---- spec -------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
def _items(raw):
    if "items" not in raw:
        return list(T4_BULLETS["items"])
    v = raw["items"]
    v = v.replace("\\n", "\n").split("\n") if isinstance(v, str) else v if isinstance(v, (list, tuple)) else []
    out = [str(x).strip() for x in v if x is not None and str(x).strip()]
    if len(out) > MAX_ITEMS:
        t4_warn("bullet_list: %d items - only the first %d are used." % (len(out), MAX_ITEMS))
        out = out[:MAX_ITEMS]
    return out


def _spec(raw):
    """Complete, clean spec (never raises; junk -> defaults)."""
    raw = raw if isinstance(raw, dict) else {}
    d, items = T4_BULLETS, _items(raw)
    al = raw.get("align")
    al = {"left": "left", "l": "left", "ltr": "left", "right": "right", "r": "right", "rtl": "right"}.get(al.strip().lower() if isinstance(al, str) else "", "auto")
    if al == "auto":
        al = "right" if items and is_rtl_text(items[0]) else "left"
    mw, fo, an = raw.get("max_width"), raw.get("font"), raw.get("anim")
    an = an.strip().lower() if isinstance(an, str) else "fade"
    return {"items": items, "color": _color(raw.get("color"), d["color"]), "text_color": _color(raw.get("text_color"), d["text_color"]),
            "dot": _flt(raw.get("dot"), 3, 80, d["dot"]), "glow": _flt(raw.get("glow"), 0, 40, d["glow"]), "size": _flt(raw.get("size"), 8, 200, d["size"]),
            "gap": _flt(raw.get("gap"), 0, 200, d["gap"]), "align": al, "max_width": None if mw is None else _flt(mw, 40, 4000, 400.0),
            "font": fo.strip() if isinstance(fo, str) else "", "anim": an if an in ("fade", "reveal", "pop") else "fade",
            "line_step": _flt(raw.get("line_step"), 0.0, 5.0, d["line_step"]), "line_dur": _flt(raw.get("line_dur"), 0.05, 5.0, d["line_dur"])}


# ---- text -------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
def _font(path):
    if path and __import__("os").path.isfile(path):
        return path
    if path:
        t4_warn("bullet_list: font '%s' not found - using the default font." % path.replace("\\", "/").split("/")[-1])
    return default_font()


def _wrap(text, f, maxw):
    """Greedy word wrap (logical order); f must be the font loaded last (shape() reads it)."""
    lines, cur = [], ""
    for wd in text.split():
        t = (cur + " " + wd).strip()
        if cur and f.getlength(shape(t)) > maxw:
            lines.append(cur)
            cur = wd
        else:
            cur = t
    return lines + [cur] if cur else lines or [""]


def _cap(lines, f, maxw):
    """<= 2 lines: the rest of the text is folded into line 2 and cut with an ellipsis."""
    if len(lines) <= 2:
        return lines
    last = " ".join(lines[1:])
    while len(last) > 1 and f.getlength(shape(last + "\u2026")) > maxw:
        last = last[:-1]
    return [lines[0], last + "\u2026"]


def _layout(sp, bw, bh, s, path):
    """-> (size, dot d, dot-text gap, line height, row gap, rows = [[line]]); everything x k (>= 0.6) when the rows are taller than the box."""
    k, maxw = 1.0, min(bw, sp["max_width"] * s) if sp["max_width"] else bw
    for _ in range(2):
        size, d = max(8, int(round(sp["size"] * s * k))), max(4.0, sp["dot"] * s * k)
        gx, f = max(8.0, d * 1.1), load_font(path, max(8, int(round(sp["size"] * s * k))))
        avail = max(20.0, maxw - d - gx)
        rows = [_cap(_wrap(t, f, avail), f, avail) for t in sp["items"]]
        lh, gap = size * 1.25, sp["gap"] * s * k
        total = sum(len(r) for r in rows) * lh + gap * (len(rows) - 1)
        if total <= bh or k < 1.0:
            break
        k = max(0.6, bh / total)
    if total > bh * 1.02:
        t4_warn("bullet_list: the rows are taller than the box (%d > %d px) - give it a taller box / fewer items." % (total, bh))
    return size, d, gx, lh, gap, rows


# ---- drawing ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
def _dot(im, cx, cy, r, color, glow):
    """Filled disc with a light glow + near-white core, drawn straight into `im` (local coords = sprite px)."""
    def disc(rr):
        def fn(d, L, s):
            a, b = L(cx - rr, cy - rr), L(cx + rr, cy + rr)
            d.ellipse((a[0], a[1], b[0] - 1, b[1] - 1), fill=255)
        return t2_draw_mask((cx - rr, cy - rr, cx + rr, cy + rr), fn)
    c, m = t2_rgb(color), disc(r)
    if glow > 0:
        t2_paint(im, t2_glow(m, max(2.0, glow * 0.6), 1.1), c, 0.6)
    t2_paint(im, m, c)
    t2_paint(im, disc(r * 0.45), t2_lighten(c, 0.85))


def _row(sp, f, lines, size, d, gx, lh, glow, pad):
    """One row sprite (dot + text lines) -> (RGBA, content w, content h). f = the font loaded last."""
    from PIL import ImageDraw
    shaped = [shape(t) for t in lines]
    tw = max(f.getlength(t) for t in shaped)
    w, h, right = int(math.ceil(d + gx + tw)), int(math.ceil(len(lines) * lh)), sp["align"] == "right"
    im = Image.new("RGBA", (w + 2 * pad, h + 2 * pad), (0, 0, 0, 0))
    _dot(im, pad + (w - d / 2.0 if right else d / 2.0), pad + lh / 2.0, d / 2.0, sp["color"], glow)
    dr, fill = ImageDraw.Draw(im), t2_rgb(sp["text_color"]) + (255,)
    x = pad + (w - d - gx if right else d + gx)
    for i, t in enumerate(shaped):
        dr.text((x, pad + i * lh + _baseline(f, lh)), t, font=f, fill=fill, anchor="rs" if right else "ls")
    return im, w, h


def _build(sp, bw, bh, s):
    if not sp["items"]:
        t4_warn("bullet_list: no items - skipped.")
        return None
    path = _font(sp["font"])
    size, d, gx, lh, gap, rows = _layout(sp, bw, bh, s, path)
    f, glow = load_font(path, size), sp["glow"] * s
    pad = int(glow * 2.6) + 4
    parts, y = [], 0.0
    for lines in rows:
        im, w, h = _row(sp, f, lines, size, d, gx, lh, glow, pad)
        x = float(bw - w) if sp["align"] == "right" else 0.0
        parts.append((im, int(round(x)), int(round(y)), w, h))
        y += len(lines) * lh + gap
    spr = Image.new("RGBA", (int(bw) + 2 * pad, int(max(bh, y - gap)) + 2 * pad), (0, 0, 0, 0))
    ink = (pad + min(p[1] for p in parts), pad + min(p[2] for p in parts), pad + max(p[1] + p[3] for p in parts), pad + max(p[2] + p[4] for p in parts))
    spr.info.update({"origin": (pad, pad), "ink": ink, "lines_only": True, "lines": [(p[0], p[1], p[2]) for p in parts], "line_step": sp["line_step"],
                     "line_dur": sp["line_dur"], "line_anim": {"pop": "t4_pop"}.get(sp["anim"], sp["anim"])})
    return spr


def t4_bullet_list(spec, box, ctx):
    """Widget function: staggered dot + text rows in the slot `box` = (x, y, w, h) canvas px. sprite.info['origin'] = the box's top-left;
    the sprite itself is empty, the rows are in sprite.info['lines'] (see t4_widget_line_items). Cached."""
    sp = _spec(spec)
    s = float(ctx.get("s", 1.0)) if isinstance(ctx, dict) else 1.0
    w, h = max(60, int(round(box[2]))), max(40, int(round(box[3])))
    return _cached(_BC, (repr(sp), w, h, round(s, 3)), lambda: _build(sp, w, h, s), 6)


# ---- questions --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
def t4_ask_bullets():
    """Question group of the widget (called by t4_ask_scenes) -> spec dict. Colours / sizes / timing: edit the job file."""
    d = T4_BULLETS
    raw = ask("Bullet items, separated by | (Enter = the demo list)", default=" | ".join(d["items"]), key="t4_bl_items")
    out = {"items": [t.strip() for t in str(raw).split("|") if t.strip()]}
    out["align"] = ["auto", "left", "right"][ask_choice("Alignment", ["Auto (Arabic = right)", "Left", "Right"], default=1, key="t4_bl_align") - 1]
    return out


register_widget("bullet_list", t4_bullet_list, ask=t4_ask_bullets, slot="bullets")

====================================================================================================
FILE: mt\templates\t4\scenes_charts.py
====================================================================================================
"""mt.templates.t4.scenes_charts - Template 4 scenes: widgets 'bar_chart', 'progress_list', 'badge' (refs R5 / R6 look).
bar_chart     neon gradient bars on thin axis lines. spec (px at short side 1080): values [..] (<= 12, missing = demo), labels [..] (x labels),
  color '#19D3FF' (bar top) / color2 '#1E6BFF' (bar bottom), axis_color, text_color, glow 8, bar_w 0.58 (fraction of the slot), axis_w 2, size 24
  (label px), max None (= largest value), show_values True, steps 8 (growth steps 1..8), line_step 0.1, line_dur 0.2, font '', rtl 'auto'
  (Arabic labels: first bar on the right, y axis on the right). Growth = the plot is cut into <= 8 horizontal bands (bottom to top); each band is ONE
  pre-built Item (sprite.info['lines'], staggered by the engine, fades in) - nothing is drawn per frame. The value labels are the last Item.
progress_list rows [{icon, label, pct}] (<= 8; also 'label|pct' or 'icon|label|pct' strings; missing = demo): icon (kit name | PNG | '-'), label,
