  track + gradient fill of pct %. keys: color, color2, text_color, track_color, glow 6, size 30, bar_h 14, gap 26, icon_size 0.9 (x row height),
  show_pct True, align auto|left|right (Arabic = right: icon + text right, fill grows leftward), font, anim fade|reveal|pop, line_step 0.25, line_dur 0.4.
  Rows are staggered Items (lines_only sprite, like bullet_list).
badge         small glass pill: icon (kit name | PNG | '-') + text. keys: text 'Python', icon 'code', color, text_color, fill, fill_alpha 0.7, glow 8,
  border 2, size 30, pad_x 24, pad_y 12, gap 12, icon_size 1.15 (x font size), align auto|left|center|right (auto: Arabic = right), font. One cached sprite.
Public: T4_BAR_CHART, T4_PROGRESS, T4_BADGE, t4_bar_chart / t4_progress_list / t4_badge(spec, box, ctx), t4_ask_bar_chart / t4_ask_progress / t4_ask_badge."""
import math
import re

from PIL import Image, ImageDraw

from mt.common import ask
from mt.draw import t2_glow, t2_grad, t2_paint, t2_rgb, t2_rrect_mask
from mt.fonts import is_rtl_text, load_font, shape
from mt.templates.t4.kit import glass_panel, kit_paste
from mt.templates.t4.nameplate import _bool, _cached, _flt
from mt.templates.t4.scenes_bullets import _font
from mt.templates.t4.scenes_reg import register_widget
from mt.templates.t4.scenes_ring import _icon
from mt.templates.t4.scenes_title import _color
from mt.templates.t4.scenes_widgets import _baseline
from mt.templates.t4.warn import t4_warn

T4_BAR_CHART = {"values": [35, 55, 42, 78, 95], "labels": ["Jan", "Feb", "Mar", "Apr", "May"], "color": "#19D3FF", "color2": "#1E6BFF",
                "axis_color": "#5C7FB0", "text_color": "#FFFFFF", "glow": 8.0, "bar_w": 0.58, "axis_w": 2.0, "size": 24.0, "max": None,
                "show_values": True, "steps": 8, "line_step": 0.1, "line_dur": 0.2, "font": "", "rtl": "auto"}
T4_PROGRESS = {"rows": [{"icon": "code", "label": "Python", "pct": 90}, {"icon": "database", "label": "SQL", "pct": 75},
                        {"icon": "globe", "label": "Web design", "pct": 60}],
               "color": "#19D3FF", "color2": "#1E6BFF", "text_color": "#FFFFFF", "track_color": "#12264A", "glow": 6.0, "size": 30.0,
               "bar_h": 14.0, "gap": 26.0, "icon_size": 0.9, "show_pct": True, "align": "auto", "font": "", "anim": "fade",
               "line_step": 0.25, "line_dur": 0.4}
T4_BADGE = {"text": "Python", "icon": "code", "color": "#19D3FF", "text_color": "#FFFFFF", "fill": "#0B1A3A", "fill_alpha": 0.7, "glow": 8.0,
            "border": 2.0, "size": 30.0, "pad_x": 24.0, "pad_y": 12.0, "gap": 12.0, "icon_size": 1.15, "align": "auto", "font": ""}
MAX_BARS, MAX_ROWS = 12, 8
_ICON_OFF = ("", "-", "none")
_CB, _CP, _CG = {}, {}, {}


# ---- shared helpers ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
def _numbers(v):
    v = [x for x in re.split(r"[|;,\s]+", v) if x] if isinstance(v, str) else v if isinstance(v, (list, tuple)) else []
    out = []
    for x in v:
        if isinstance(x, bool):
            continue
        try:
            f = float(x)
        except (TypeError, ValueError):
            continue
        if math.isfinite(f):
            out.append(max(0.0, f))
    if len(out) > MAX_BARS:
        t4_warn("bar_chart: %d values - only the first %d are used." % (len(out), MAX_BARS))
        out = out[:MAX_BARS]
    return out


def _strs(v):
    v = v.split("|") if isinstance(v, str) else v if isinstance(v, (list, tuple)) else []
    return [str(x).strip() if x is not None else "" for x in v]


def _text(v, default=""):
    if v is None:
        return default
    return v.strip() if isinstance(v, str) else str(v).strip() if isinstance(v, (int, float)) and not isinstance(v, bool) else ""


def _rtl(v, texts):
    if isinstance(v, bool):
        return v
    first = next((t for t in texts if t), "")
    return bool(first) and is_rtl_text(first)


def _cut(f, text, maxw):
    """Shaped text, cut with an ellipsis when wider than maxw (f must be the font loaded last: shape() reads it)."""
    if f.getlength(shape(text)) <= maxw:
        return shape(text)
    while len(text) > 1 and f.getlength(shape(text + "\u2026")) > maxw:
        text = text[:-1]
    return shape(text + "\u2026")


def _txt_fit(path, size, texts, maxw):
    """-> (font, [shaped]): font shrinks to 70 % while the widest text is wider than maxw, then texts are cut with an ellipsis."""
    lo, sz = max(8, int(size * 0.7)), max(8, int(size))
    f = load_font(path, sz)
    while sz > lo and max([f.getlength(shape(t)) for t in texts] or [0]) > maxw:
        sz -= 1
        f = load_font(path, sz)
    return f, [_cut(f, t, maxw) for t in texts]


# ---- bar_chart: spec --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
def _bar_spec(raw):
    raw = raw if isinstance(raw, dict) else {}
    d, demo = T4_BAR_CHART, "values" not in raw
    vals = list(d["values"]) if demo else _numbers(raw["values"])
    labs = _strs(raw["labels"]) if "labels" in raw else (list(d["labels"]) if demo else [])
    labs = (labs + [""] * len(vals))[:len(vals)]
    mx = raw.get("max")
    mx = _flt(mx, 1e-6, 1e12, 0.0) if mx is not None else 0.0
    try:
        steps = max(1, min(8, int(raw.get("steps", d["steps"]))))
    except (TypeError, ValueError, OverflowError):
        steps = d["steps"]
    fo = raw.get("font")
    return {"values": vals, "labels": labs, "color": _color(raw.get("color"), d["color"]), "color2": _color(raw.get("color2"), d["color2"]),
            "axis_color": _color(raw.get("axis_color"), d["axis_color"]), "text_color": _color(raw.get("text_color"), d["text_color"]),
            "glow": _flt(raw.get("glow"), 0, 40, d["glow"]), "bar_w": _flt(raw.get("bar_w"), 0.15, 1.0, d["bar_w"]),
            "axis_w": _flt(raw.get("axis_w"), 0.5, 12, d["axis_w"]), "size": _flt(raw.get("size"), 8, 100, d["size"]), "max": mx or None,
            "show_values": _bool(raw.get("show_values"), True), "steps": steps,
            "line_step": _flt(raw.get("line_step"), 0.0, 2.0, d["line_step"]), "line_dur": _flt(raw.get("line_dur"), 0.03, 3.0, d["line_dur"]),
            "font": fo.strip() if isinstance(fo, str) else "", "_rtl": _rtl(raw.get("rtl"), labs)}


# ---- bar_chart: drawing -----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
def _bar_layout(sp, w, h, s, path):
    """All chart numbers in sprite px: margins, axes, bar slots / heights, label fonts."""
    vals, n = sp["values"], len(sp["values"])
    aw, glow = max(1.0, sp["axis_w"] * s), sp["glow"] * s
    m = int(glow * 2.6 + aw + 6)
    px0, px1, inner = m + 6 * s, m + w - 6 * s, 14 * s
    slot = max(8.0, (px1 - px0 - 2 * inner) / n)
    has_lab = any(sp["labels"])
    lf, lab = _txt_fit(path, sp["size"] * s, sp["labels"], slot * 0.96) if has_lab else (None, [])
    lsz = int(getattr(lf, "size", sp["size"] * s)) if lf else 0
    vsize = max(8, int(round(sp["size"] * s * 0.95)))
    base = m + h - (lsz * 1.5 if lf else 0.0) - 4 * s - aw / 2.0
    top = m + (vsize * 1.7 if sp["show_values"] else 8 * s)
    avail = max(8.0, base - aw / 2.0 - top)
    vmax = sp["max"] or max(vals) or 1.0
    bars = []
    for i, v in enumerate(vals):
        idx = n - 1 - i if sp["_rtl"] else i
        bh = 0.0 if v <= 0 else max(3.0, min(1.0, v / vmax) * avail)
        bars.append((px0 + inner + (idx + 0.5) * slot, base - aw / 2.0 - bh, bh))
    bw = max(4.0, min(slot * sp["bar_w"], slot))
    return {"m": m, "W": int(round(w)) + 2 * m, "H": int(round(h)) + 2 * m, "px0": px0, "px1": px1, "base": base, "top": top, "aw": aw,
            "bars": bars, "bw": bw, "rad": min(bw * 0.3, 12 * s), "lf": lf, "lab": lab, "lsz": lsz, "vsize": vsize, "s": s}


def _bar_static(sp, g):
    """Axes (thin) + x labels: the part that is visible from the first frame."""
    im = Image.new("RGBA", (g["W"], g["H"]), (0, 0, 0, 0))
    aw, s, ac = g["aw"], g["s"], t2_rgb(sp["axis_color"])
    ax = g["px1"] if sp["_rtl"] else g["px0"]
    for box in ((g["px0"] - aw / 2.0, g["base"] - aw / 2.0, g["px1"] + aw / 2.0, g["base"] + aw / 2.0),
                (ax - aw / 2.0, g["top"] - 4 * s, ax + aw / 2.0, g["base"] + aw / 2.0)):
        t2_paint(im, t2_rrect_mask(box, aw / 2.0), ac, 0.9)
    if g["lf"]:
        dr, y = ImageDraw.Draw(im), g["base"] + aw / 2.0 + 4 * s + g["lsz"] * 0.95
        for (cx, _y0, _bh), t in zip(g["bars"], g["lab"]):
            dr.text((cx, y), t, font=g["lf"], fill=t2_rgb(sp["text_color"]) + (230,), anchor="ms")
    return im


def _bar_plot(sp, g):
    """All bars (glow + vertical neon gradient, rounded top) on one transparent sprite-size layer; everything under the axis is cleared."""
    plot = Image.new("RGBA", (g["W"], g["H"]), (0, 0, 0, 0))
    c1, c2, glow = t2_rgb(sp["color"]), t2_rgb(sp["color2"]), sp["glow"] * g["s"]
    masks = []
    for cx, y0, bh in g["bars"]:
        if bh > 0:
            box = (cx - g["bw"] / 2.0, y0, cx + g["bw"] / 2.0, y0 + bh + g["rad"] * 2)      # bottom corners hide under the axis
            masks.append((t2_rrect_mask(box, g["rad"]), box))
    if glow > 0:
        for mk, _b in masks:
            t2_paint(plot, t2_glow(mk, glow * 0.7, 1.0), c1, 0.5)
    for mk, box in masks:
        t2_paint(plot, mk, t2_grad([(0, c1), (1, c2)], 90, box))
    cut = int(round(g["base"] - g["aw"] / 2.0))
    plot.paste((0, 0, 0, 0), (0, cut, plot.width, plot.height))
    return plot


def _bar_values(sp, g, path):
    """Value labels above the bars (one layer, shown as the LAST item)."""
    im = Image.new("RGBA", (g["W"], g["H"]), (0, 0, 0, 0))
    f, dr, col = load_font(path, g["vsize"]), None, t2_rgb(sp["text_color"]) + (255,)
    dr = ImageDraw.Draw(im)
    for v, (cx, y0, bh) in zip(sp["values"], g["bars"]):
        y = (y0 if bh > 0 else g["base"] - g["aw"] / 2.0) - 8 * g["s"]
        dr.text((cx, y), "%g" % v, font=f, fill=col, anchor="ms")
    return im


def _bands(plot, steps):
    """Cut the plot into <= steps horizontal bands, bottom first -> [(sprite, x, y)] (top-left in sprite coords); empty bands are skipped."""
    bb = plot.getbbox()
    if not bb:
        return []
    top, bot, out = bb[1], bb[3], []
    edges = [int(round(bot - j * (bot - top) / float(steps))) for j in range(steps + 1)]
    edges[-1] = top
    for j in range(steps):
        y0, y1 = edges[j + 1], edges[j]
        if y1 <= y0:
            continue
        part = plot.crop((0, y0, plot.width, y1))
        pb = part.getbbox()
        if pb:
            out.append((part.crop(pb), pb[0], y0 + pb[1]))
    return out


def _build_bars(sp, w, h, s):
    if not sp["values"]:
        t4_warn("bar_chart: no values - skipped.")
        return None
    path = _font(sp["font"])
    g = _bar_layout(sp, w, h, s, path)
    spr = _bar_static(sp, g)
    parts = _bands(_bar_plot(sp, g), sp["steps"])
    if sp["show_values"]:
        vim = _bar_values(sp, g, path)
        vb = vim.getbbox()
        if vb:
            parts.append((vim.crop(vb), vb[0], vb[1]))
    spr.info.update({"origin": (g["m"], g["m"]), "lines": parts, "line_step": sp["line_step"], "line_dur": sp["line_dur"], "line_anim": "fade"})
    return spr


def t4_bar_chart(spec, box, ctx):
    """Widget function: neon bar chart in the slot `box` = (x, y, w, h) canvas px. Static sprite = axes + x labels (origin = the box's top-left);
    the growth bands + value labels are in sprite.info['lines'] (the engine adds one staggered Item each). Cached."""
    sp = _bar_spec(spec)
    s = float(ctx.get("s", 1.0)) if isinstance(ctx, dict) else 1.0
    w, h = max(120, int(round(box[2]))), max(80, int(round(box[3])))
    return _cached(_CB, (repr(sp), w, h, round(s, 3)), lambda: _build_bars(sp, w, h, s), 6)


# ---- progress_list ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
def _pct(v):
    try:
        f = 0.0 if isinstance(v, bool) else float(str(v).strip().rstrip("%").strip())
    except (TypeError, ValueError):
        return 0.0
    return max(0.0, min(100.0, f)) if math.isfinite(f) else 0.0


def _prog_rows(raw):
    if "rows" not in raw:
        return [dict(r) for r in T4_PROGRESS["rows"]]
    v = raw["rows"]
    v = [x for x in re.split(r"[\n;]", v) if x.strip()] if isinstance(v, str) else v if isinstance(v, (list, tuple)) else []
    out = []
    for r in v:
        if isinstance(r, str):
            p = [t.strip() for t in r.split("|")]
            r = ({"icon": p[0], "label": p[1], "pct": p[2]} if len(p) >= 3 else {"label": p[0], "pct": p[1]} if len(p) == 2 else {"label": p[0]})
        if not isinstance(r, dict):
            continue
        label, ic = _text(r.get("label")), _text(r.get("icon"))
        if label or ic:
            out.append({"icon": "" if ic.lower() in _ICON_OFF else ic, "label": label, "pct": _pct(r.get("pct"))})
    if len(out) > MAX_ROWS:
        t4_warn("progress_list: %d rows - only the first %d are used." % (len(out), MAX_ROWS))
        out = out[:MAX_ROWS]
    return out


def _prog_spec(raw):
    raw = raw if isinstance(raw, dict) else {}
    d, rows = T4_PROGRESS, _prog_rows(raw)
    al = raw.get("align")
    al = {"left": "left", "l": "left", "ltr": "left", "right": "right", "r": "right", "rtl": "right"}.get(al.strip().lower() if isinstance(al, str) else "", "auto")
    if al == "auto":
        al = "right" if rows and is_rtl_text(rows[0]["label"]) else "left"
    an, fo = raw.get("anim"), raw.get("font")
    an = an.strip().lower() if isinstance(an, str) else "fade"
    return {"rows": rows, "color": _color(raw.get("color"), d["color"]), "color2": _color(raw.get("color2"), d["color2"]),
            "text_color": _color(raw.get("text_color"), d["text_color"]), "track_color": _color(raw.get("track_color"), d["track_color"]),
            "glow": _flt(raw.get("glow"), 0, 40, d["glow"]), "size": _flt(raw.get("size"), 8, 200, d["size"]),
            "bar_h": _flt(raw.get("bar_h"), 3, 80, d["bar_h"]), "gap": _flt(raw.get("gap"), 0, 200, d["gap"]),
            "icon_size": _flt(raw.get("icon_size"), 0.2, 1.5, d["icon_size"]), "show_pct": _bool(raw.get("show_pct"), True), "_rtl": al == "right",
            "font": fo.strip() if isinstance(fo, str) else "", "anim": an if an in ("fade", "reveal", "pop") else "fade",
            "line_step": _flt(raw.get("line_step"), 0.0, 5.0, d["line_step"]), "line_dur": _flt(raw.get("line_dur"), 0.05, 5.0, d["line_dur"])}


def _prog_layout(sp, w, h, s, path):
    """Row geometry; everything x k (>= 0.6) when the rows are taller than the box."""
    n, k = len(sp["rows"]), 1.0
    for _ in range(2):
        size = max(8, int(round(sp["size"] * s * k)))
        bar_h, gap, lh = max(4.0, sp["bar_h"] * s * k), sp["gap"] * s * k, size * 1.25
        rh = lh + size * 0.25 + bar_h
        total = n * rh + (n - 1) * gap
        if total <= h or k < 1.0:
            break
        k = max(0.6, h / total)
    if total > h * 1.02:
        t4_warn("progress_list: the rows are taller than the box (%d > %d px) - give it a taller box / fewer rows." % (total, h))
    isz = int(round(rh * sp["icon_size"])) if any(r["icon"] for r in sp["rows"]) else 0
    return {"size": size, "bar_h": bar_h, "gap": gap, "lh": lh, "rh": rh, "isz": isz, "gx": max(8.0, size * 0.45), "path": path, "total": total}


def _prog_row(sp, row, g, w, pad, s):
    """One row sprite: [icon] label ........ pct  /  track + gradient fill. Local coords = sprite px (content offset = pad)."""
    right, rh, bh = sp["_rtl"], g["rh"], g["bar_h"]
    im = Image.new("RGBA", (int(w) + 2 * pad, int(math.ceil(rh)) + 2 * pad), (0, 0, 0, 0))
    icw = g["isz"] + g["gx"] if g["isz"] else 0
    tx0, tx1 = pad + (0 if right else icw), pad + w - (icw if right else 0)
    f = load_font(g["path"], g["size"])
    pct_t = "%d%%" % int(round(row["pct"])) if sp["show_pct"] else ""
    lab = _cut(f, row["label"], (tx1 - tx0) - (f.getlength(pct_t) if pct_t else 0) - g["gx"])
    dr, by = ImageDraw.Draw(im), pad + _baseline(f, g["lh"])
    c1, c2 = t2_rgb(sp["color"]), t2_rgb(sp["color2"])
    dr.text((tx1 if right else tx0, by), lab, font=f, fill=t2_rgb(sp["text_color"]) + (255,), anchor="rs" if right else "ls")
    if pct_t:
        dr.text((tx0 if right else tx1, by), pct_t, font=f, fill=c1 + (255,), anchor="ls" if right else "rs")
    yb = pad + g["lh"] + g["size"] * 0.25
    t2_paint(im, t2_rrect_mask((tx0, yb, tx1, yb + bh), bh / 2.0), t2_rgb(sp["track_color"]), 0.9)
    if row["pct"] > 0:
        fw = max(bh, (tx1 - tx0) * row["pct"] / 100.0)
        fx0, fx1 = (tx1 - fw, tx1) if right else (tx0, tx0 + fw)
        mk = t2_rrect_mask((fx0, yb, fx1, yb + bh), bh / 2.0)
        if sp["glow"] > 0:
            t2_paint(im, t2_glow(mk, max(2.0, sp["glow"] * s * 0.6), 1.0), c1, 0.5)
        t2_paint(im, mk, t2_grad([(0, c1), (1, c2)] if right else [(0, c2), (1, c1)], 0, (fx0, yb, fx1, yb + bh)))
    if g["isz"] and row["icon"]:
        ic = _icon({"icon": row["icon"]}, g["isz"], g["isz"] * 0.12, sp["color"])
        if ic is not None:
            kit_paste(im, ic, (pad + w - g["isz"]) if right else pad, pad + (rh - g["isz"]) / 2.0)
    return im


def _build_prog(sp, w, h, s):
    if not sp["rows"]:
        t4_warn("progress_list: no rows - skipped.")
        return None
    g = _prog_layout(sp, w, h, s, _font(sp["font"]))
    pad = int(sp["glow"] * s * 2.6) + 4
    parts, y = [], 0.0
    for row in sp["rows"]:
        parts.append((_prog_row(sp, row, g, w, pad, s), 0, int(round(y))))
        y += g["rh"] + g["gap"]
    total = int(math.ceil(y - g["gap"]))
    spr = Image.new("RGBA", (int(w) + 2 * pad, max(int(h), total) + 2 * pad), (0, 0, 0, 0))      # empty: the rows are the items
    spr.info.update({"origin": (pad, pad), "ink": (pad, pad, pad + int(w), pad + total), "lines_only": True, "lines": parts,
                     "line_step": sp["line_step"], "line_dur": sp["line_dur"], "line_anim": {"pop": "t4_pop"}.get(sp["anim"], sp["anim"])})
    return spr


def t4_progress_list(spec, box, ctx):
    """Widget function: rows of icon + label + progress bar in the slot `box`. Empty static sprite, one staggered Item per row (info['lines'])."""
    sp = _prog_spec(spec)
    s = float(ctx.get("s", 1.0)) if isinstance(ctx, dict) else 1.0
    w, h = max(120, int(round(box[2]))), max(40, int(round(box[3])))
    return _cached(_CP, (repr(sp), w, h, round(s, 3)), lambda: _build_prog(sp, w, h, s), 6)


# ---- badge ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
def _badge_spec(raw):
    raw = raw if isinstance(raw, dict) else {}
    d = T4_BADGE
    text = _text(raw.get("text", d["text"]))
    ic = _text(raw.get("icon", d["icon"]))
    al = raw.get("align")
    al = {"left": "left", "l": "left", "center": "center", "centre": "center", "c": "center", "right": "right", "r": "right"}.get(
        al.strip().lower() if isinstance(al, str) else "", "auto")
    if al == "auto":
        al = "right" if text and is_rtl_text(text) else "left"
    fo = raw.get("font")
    return {"text": text, "icon": "" if ic.lower() in _ICON_OFF else ic, "color": _color(raw.get("color"), d["color"]),
            "text_color": _color(raw.get("text_color"), d["text_color"]), "fill": _color(raw.get("fill"), d["fill"]),
            "fill_alpha": _flt(raw.get("fill_alpha"), 0.0, 1.0, d["fill_alpha"]), "glow": _flt(raw.get("glow"), 0, 40, d["glow"]),
            "border": _flt(raw.get("border"), 0, 12, d["border"]), "size": _flt(raw.get("size"), 8, 150, d["size"]),
            "pad_x": _flt(raw.get("pad_x"), 0, 120, d["pad_x"]), "pad_y": _flt(raw.get("pad_y"), 0, 80, d["pad_y"]),
            "gap": _flt(raw.get("gap"), 0, 80, d["gap"]), "icon_size": _flt(raw.get("icon_size"), 0.3, 2.5, d["icon_size"]),
            "align": al, "font": fo.strip() if isinstance(fo, str) else ""}


def _build_badge(sp, w, h, s):
    if not (sp["text"] or sp["icon"]):
        t4_warn("badge: no text and no icon - skipped.")
        return None
    path = _font(sp["font"])
    has_icon = bool(sp["icon"]) and _icon({"icon": sp["icon"]}, 16, 2.0, sp["color"]) is not None
    for k in (1.0, 0.9, 0.8, 0.7, 0.6):                                            # shrink until the pill fits the box width
        size = max(8, int(round(sp["size"] * s * k)))
        f = load_font(path, size)
        tw = f.getlength(shape(sp["text"])) if sp["text"] else 0.0
        isz = int(round(size * sp["icon_size"])) if has_icon else 0
        px, gx = sp["pad_x"] * s * k, sp["gap"] * s * k
        pw = 2 * px + isz + (gx if isz and tw else 0.0) + tw
        if pw <= w:
            break
    if pw > w * 1.02:
        t4_warn("badge: the pill is wider than its box (%d > %d px) - give it a wider box / shorter text." % (pw, w))
    ph = max(size * 1.25, isz) + 2 * sp["pad_y"] * s * k
    tint = t2_rgb(sp["fill"]) + (int(round(255 * sp["fill_alpha"])),)
    pill = glass_panel((int(round(pw)), int(round(ph))), ph / 2.0 - 0.5, tint, sp["color"], 0, sp["border"] * s, sp["glow"] * s)
    ox, oy = pill.info["origin"]
    right, f = sp["align"] == "right" or (sp["align"] == "auto" and is_rtl_text(sp["text"])), load_font(path, size)
    icon_x = (ox + pw - px - isz) if right else ox + px
    tx = (icon_x - gx if isz else ox + pw - px) if right else (icon_x + isz + gx if isz else ox + px)
    if sp["text"]:
        ImageDraw.Draw(pill).text((tx, oy + _baseline(f, ph)), shape(sp["text"]), font=f, fill=t2_rgb(sp["text_color"]) + (255,), anchor="rs" if right else "ls")
    if isz:
        ic = _icon({"icon": sp["icon"]}, isz, isz * 0.12, sp["color"])
        if ic is not None:
            kit_paste(pill, ic, icon_x, oy + (ph - isz) / 2.0)
    dx = {"left": 0.0, "center": (w - pw) / 2.0, "right": w - pw}.get(sp["align"], w - pw if right else 0.0)
    pill.info["origin"] = (int(round(ox - dx)), int(round(oy - (h - ph) / 2.0)))      # box top-left relative to the pill sprite
    return pill


def t4_badge(spec, box, ctx):
    """Widget function: small glass pill (icon or PNG + text) placed inside the slot `box` (left / centre / right, vertically centred). Cached."""
    sp = _badge_spec(spec)
    s = float(ctx.get("s", 1.0)) if isinstance(ctx, dict) else 1.0
    w, h = max(60, int(round(box[2]))), max(30, int(round(box[3])))
    return _cached(_CG, (repr(sp), w, h, round(s, 3)), lambda: _build_badge(sp, w, h, s), 8)


# ---- questions --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
def t4_ask_bar_chart():
    """Question group of 'bar_chart' (called by t4_ask_scenes) -> spec dict. Colours / steps / timing: edit the job file."""
    d = T4_BAR_CHART
    vals = ask("Bar values, separated by |  (Enter = the demo)", default=" | ".join("%g" % v for v in d["values"]), key="t4_bc_values")
    labs = ask("Bar labels, separated by |", default=" | ".join(d["labels"]), key="t4_bc_labels", allow_empty=True) or ""
    return {"values": [t.strip() for t in str(vals).split("|") if t.strip()], "labels": [t.strip() for t in str(labs).split("|")]}


def t4_ask_progress():
    """Question group of 'progress_list' -> spec dict. Rows as  icon|label|pct ; icon|label|pct  (icon optional)."""
    d = "; ".join("%s|%s|%d" % (r["icon"], r["label"], r["pct"]) for r in T4_PROGRESS["rows"])
    return {"rows": ask("Rows  icon|label|pct ; ...  (icon optional, '-' = none)", default=d, key="t4_pl_rows")}


def t4_ask_badge():
    """Question group of 'badge' -> spec dict."""
    d = T4_BADGE
    return {"text": ask("Badge text", default=d["text"], key="t4_bg_text"),
            "icon": ask("Badge icon (kit name or PNG path, '-' = none)", default=d["icon"], key="t4_bg_icon")}


register_widget("bar_chart", t4_bar_chart, ask=t4_ask_bar_chart, slot="chart")
register_widget("progress_list", t4_progress_list, ask=t4_ask_progress, slot="progress")
register_widget("badge", t4_badge, ask=t4_ask_badge, slot="badge")

====================================================================================================
FILE: mt\templates\t4\scenes_ring.py
====================================================================================================
"""mt.templates.t4.scenes_ring - Template 4 scenes: widget 'ring' (neon concentric arcs around a centre icon, refs R5 / R6).
spec keys (all optional; px at short side 1080): rings 3 | [ {arcs, gap, width, glow, color, rotate}, ... ] (a list = one dict per ring, overrides
  the global keys), arcs 2 (arcs per ring: 2 = halves; int | list per ring), gap 28 (deg between arcs; int | list), width 4, glow 10,
  color / colors (a list cycles per ring), rotate (start angle in deg, clockwise from the top; default 20 + 55 * ring), spacing 20 (px between rings),
  icon 'code' (kit icon name | PNG path | ''/'-'/'none'), icon_color '' (= colour of ring 1), icon_size 0.6 (share of the inner diameter),
  icon_glow None (auto). Rings fit min(box.w, box.h); sprite = box + glow margin, info['origin'] = the box's top-left. Cached.
Public: T4_RING, t4_ring(spec, box, ctx), t4_ask_ring()."""
import math
import os

from PIL import Image, ImageDraw, ImageFilter

from mt.common import ask, ask_choice
from mt.draw import t2_draw_mask, t2_rgb
from mt.templates.t4.kit import PALETTE, kit_paste, neon_icon, neon_stroke
from mt.templates.t4.nameplate import _cached, _flt
from mt.templates.t4.scenes_reg import register_widget
from mt.templates.t4.scenes_title import _color
from mt.templates.t4.warn import t4_warn

T4_RING = {"rings": 3, "arcs": 2, "gap": 28.0, "width": 4.0, "glow": 10.0, "spacing": 20.0, "icon": "code", "icon_color": "",
           "icon_size": 0.6, "icon_glow": None}
DEFAULT_COLORS = ("#19D3FF", "#1E6BFF", "#19D3FF")
MAX_RINGS = 8
_PNG_EXT = (".png", ".jpg", ".jpeg", ".webp")
_RC, _BAD = {}, set()


# ---- spec -------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
def _pick(v, i, default):
    if isinstance(v, (list, tuple)):
        return v[i % len(v)] if v else default
    return default if v is None else v


def _val(rd, raw, key, i, default, alt=None):
    """Value of ring i: the ring's own dict first, then the global key (scalar or per-ring list)."""
    for src in (rd, raw):
        for k in (key, alt):
            if k and src.get(k) is not None:
                return _pick(src[k], i, default)
    return default


def _ring(rd, raw, i):
    arcs = int(_flt(_val(rd, raw, "arcs", i, 2), 1, 12, 2))
    gap = min(_flt(_val(rd, raw, "gap", i, 28.0), 0, 180, 28.0), max(0.0, 360.0 / arcs - 6.0))
    rot, col = 20.0 + 55.0 * i, DEFAULT_COLORS[i % 3]
    return {"arcs": arcs, "gap": gap, "width": _flt(_val(rd, raw, "width", i, 4.0), 0.5, 30, 4.0),
            "glow": _flt(_val(rd, raw, "glow", i, 10.0), 0, 60, 10.0), "rotate": _flt(_val(rd, raw, "rotate", i, rot), -720, 720, rot),
            "color": _color(_val(rd, raw, "color", i, col, "colors"), col)}


def _spec(raw):
    """Complete, clean spec (never raises; junk -> defaults); out['_rings'] = one dict per ring."""
    raw = raw if isinstance(raw, dict) else {}
    rg = raw.get("rings", T4_RING["rings"])
    if isinstance(rg, (list, tuple)):
        rds = [r if isinstance(r, dict) else {} for r in rg][:MAX_RINGS]
    else:
        rds = [{}] * int(round(_flt(rg, 0, MAX_RINGS, 3)))
    ic = raw.get("icon", T4_RING["icon"])
    ic = ic.strip() if isinstance(ic, str) else ""
    ig = raw.get("icon_glow")
    col = raw.get("icon_color")
    return {"_rings": [_ring(rd, raw, i) for i, rd in enumerate(rds)], "spacing": _flt(raw.get("spacing"), 0, 200, 20.0),
            "icon": "" if ic.lower() in ("-", "none") else ic, "icon_color": col if isinstance(col, str) else "",
            "icon_size": _flt(raw.get("icon_size"), 0.1, 1.0, 0.6), "icon_glow": None if ig is None else _flt(ig, 0, 60, 0.0)}


# ---- drawing ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
def _arc_mask(R, a0, a1, wd):
    """Filled arc band (thickness wd, round ends) centred on local (0, 0); angles in deg, clockwise from the top."""
    ro, ri, full = R + wd / 2.0, max(0.5, R - wd / 2.0), a1 - a0 >= 359.9
    n = max(6, int(abs(a1 - a0) / 1.5))
    angs = [a0 + (a1 - a0) * k / n for k in range(n + 1)]

    def P(r, a):
        t = math.radians(a)
        return r * math.sin(t), -r * math.cos(t)
    poly = [P(ro, a) for a in angs] + [P(ri, a) for a in reversed(angs)]
    ends = [] if full else [P(R, a0), P(R, a1)]
    bb = (min(p[0] for p in poly) - wd - 1, min(p[1] for p in poly) - wd - 1, max(p[0] for p in poly) + wd + 1, max(p[1] for p in poly) + wd + 1)

    def fn(d, L, s):
        if full:
            for r, v in ((ro, 255), (ri, 0)):
                a, b = L(-r, -r), L(r, r)
                d.ellipse((a[0], a[1], b[0] - 1, b[1] - 1), fill=v)
            return
        d.polygon([L(x, y) for x, y in poly], fill=255)
        for ex, ey in ends:
            a, b = L(ex - wd / 2.0, ey - wd / 2.0), L(ex + wd / 2.0, ey + wd / 2.0)
            d.ellipse((a[0], a[1], b[0] - 1, b[1] - 1), fill=255)
    return t2_draw_mask(bb, fn)


def _plan(rings, spacing, w, h, s):
    """-> ([(radius, ring)], inner radius free for the icon). Rings that no longer fit are dropped (one warning)."""
    half, out, R = min(w, h) / 2.0, [], 0.0
    for i, r in enumerate(rings):
        wd = r["width"] * s
        R = half - wd / 2.0 - 1 if i == 0 else R - (rings[i - 1]["width"] * s + wd) / 2.0 - spacing * s
        if R < wd * 2 + 4:
            t4_warn("ring: only %d of %d rings fit the box - the inner ones are dropped." % (len(out), len(rings)))
            break
        out.append((R, r))
    inner = out[-1][0] - out[-1][1]["width"] * s / 2.0 - spacing * s * 0.6 if out else half
    return out, max(4.0, inner)


def _png_icon(path, px, glow, color):
    """PNG fitted into px x px with a soft halo (kit sprite convention: origin = the icon box's top-left). Missing file -> gray placeholder."""
    try:
        im = Image.open(path).convert("RGBA")
        im.thumbnail((px, px), Image.LANCZOS)
    except Exception:
        if path not in _BAD:
            _BAD.add(path)
            t4_warn("ring: icon '%s' could not be read - gray placeholder." % os.path.basename(path))
        im = Image.new("RGBA", (px, px), (0, 0, 0, 0))
        ImageDraw.Draw(im).rounded_rectangle((0, 0, px - 1, px - 1), radius=px // 5, fill=(120, 130, 140, 200))
    pad = int(glow * 2.6) + 4
    sp = Image.new("RGBA", (px + 2 * pad, px + 2 * pad), (0, 0, 0, 0))
    pos = (pad + (px - im.width) // 2, pad + (px - im.height) // 2)
    if glow > 0:
        a = Image.new("L", sp.size, 0)
        a.paste(im.getchannel("A"), pos)
        halo = Image.new("RGBA", sp.size, t2_rgb(color) + (0,))
        halo.putalpha(a.filter(ImageFilter.GaussianBlur(glow)).point(lambda v: min(255, int(v * 1.2))))
        sp.alpha_composite(halo)
    sp.alpha_composite(im, pos)
    sp.info["origin"] = (pad, pad)
    return sp


def _icon(sp, px, glow, color):
    v = sp["icon"]
    if not v:
        return None
    if os.path.isfile(v) or v.lower().endswith(_PNG_EXT) or "/" in v or "\\" in v:
        return _png_icon(v, px, glow, color)
    return neon_icon(v, px, color, glow)                      # unknown kit name -> kit warns + empty icon


def _build(sp, w, h, s):
    plan, r_in = _plan(sp["_rings"], sp["spacing"], w, h, s)
    first = sp["_rings"][0]["color"] if sp["_rings"] else PALETTE["cyan"]
    px = max(8, int(round(sp["icon_size"] * 2 * r_in)))
    ig = (px * 0.12 if sp["icon_glow"] is None else sp["icon_glow"] * s)
    m = int(max([r["glow"] * s for _, r in plan] + [ig]) * 2.6 + max([r["width"] * s for _, r in plan] + [0]) + 8)
    spr, cx, cy = Image.new("RGBA", (w + 2 * m, h + 2 * m), (0, 0, 0, 0)), m + w / 2.0, m + h / 2.0
    for R, r in plan:                                          # outer -> inner
        span, wd = 360.0 / r["arcs"], r["width"] * s
        for k in range(r["arcs"]):
            a0 = r["rotate"] + k * span + r["gap"] / 2.0
            kit_paste(spr, neon_stroke(_arc_mask(R, a0, a0 + span - r["gap"], wd), r["color"], wd, r["glow"] * s), cx, cy)
    ic = _icon(sp, px, ig, sp["icon_color"] or first)
    if ic is not None:
        kit_paste(spr, ic, cx - px / 2.0, cy - px / 2.0)
    if not plan and ic is None:
        t4_warn("ring: no rings and no icon - skipped.")
        return None
    spr.info["origin"] = (m, m)
    return spr


def t4_ring(spec, box, ctx):
    """Widget function: concentric neon arcs + centre icon in the slot `box` = (x, y, w, h) canvas px. sprite.info['origin'] = the box's top-left."""
    sp = _spec(spec)
    s = float(ctx.get("s", 1.0)) if isinstance(ctx, dict) else 1.0
    w, h = max(40, int(round(box[2]))), max(40, int(round(box[3])))
    return _cached(_RC, (repr(sp), w, h, round(s, 3)), lambda: _build(sp, w, h, s), 6)


# ---- questions --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
def t4_ask_ring():
    """Question group of the widget (called by t4_ask_scenes) -> spec dict. Colours / widths / gaps / per-ring lists: edit the job file."""
    d = T4_RING
    out = {"icon": ask("Ring icon (kit: code laptop phone gear globe database server cloud bulb chip shield | PNG path | - = none)",
                        default=d["icon"], key="t4_ring_icon")}
    try:
        out["rings"] = max(0, min(MAX_RINGS, int(ask("Number of rings (1-6)", default=str(d["rings"]), key="t4_ring_n"))))
    except (TypeError, ValueError):
        out["rings"] = d["rings"]
    out["arcs"] = [2, 3, 4, 1][ask_choice("Arcs per ring", ["2 (halves)", "3", "4", "1 (closed ring)"], default=1, key="t4_ring_arcs") - 1]
    return out


register_widget("ring", t4_ring, ask=t4_ask_ring, slot="ring")

====================================================================================================
FILE: mt\templates\t4\cards.py
====================================================================================================
"""mt.templates.t4.cards - Template 4 module 'cards' (refs R2 right card, R8 PiP card + label): IMAGE cards - a picture inside a chamfered
neon border, an optional connector line that ends in a glowing dot at a target point, and an optional label tag with slanted stripes.
Video cards: the picture is baked into an alpha clip by cards_video.py (muted); the border + label stay an Item over it. Drawing lives in cards_art.py.
cfg['t4']['cards'] = [card, ...]   (also accepted: one card dict, or {on, cards: [...]})   card = {
    media  path           the picture (png / jpg / webp ...). Missing / unreadable -> gray placeholder + one t4_warn line. A video path, or 'original' (= the main video), plus seg_start / seg_end (seconds; default seg_start = the card's start for 'original', 0 for a file; a file without seg_end loops) is baked into a muted clip.
    label  'TEXT' | {text, color, anchor}   tag overlapping a card corner (anchor bottom-right default | bottom-left | top-right | top-left).
                          Latin text is capitalised; > 11 characters -> two balanced lines (or use '\\n' / '|'); '' / '-' = no label.
    anchor 'top-right' | xy   one of the 9 positions (top-left top top-right left center right bottom-left bottom bottom-right) with inset_x /
                          inset_y px; xy = [x, y] = the centre of the card block (px or '40%'). Default anchor 'top-right' (R8).
    width 480, height     picture width px (short side 1080); height default = the picture's own shape (width / height clamped 0.75 - 1.8).
    color '#19D3FF'       border / stripes / label colour
    connector {target [x, y] | points [...], side auto|left|right|top|bottom, style elbow|line, attach 0.35, color, end_dot True|'both'|'start'|
               False, width, glow}   target = canvas px or '%' strings; points may also be 'card' / 'card-top|bottom|left|right'. Shorthand:
               connector = [x, y]. Elbow = straight - 45 degree diagonal - straight (R2); line = one straight segment (R8).
    start | trigger, end | hold (default 5 s), anim 'auto' (= 'pop'; 'fade' / 'none' / any mt.anim name), anim_dur 0.45 }
Behaviour: the card pops in (anim 't4_pop'), the connector fades in right after it, everything fades out together at the end. The connector is
drawn UNDER the card (its start is hidden inside the border). Ink (card + label + glow) stays >= 48 px from the canvas edges; frame on (P8): the
card moves inward until it clears the frame plate (same search as the name tag). One sprite Item per card (+ one for the connector), cached.
Public: T4_CARD, t4_cards_cfg(raw) -> [card], t4_cards_items(cfg, duration), t4_ask_image_cards()."""
import copy
import os

from pathlib import Path

from PIL import Image, ImageChops

from mt.anim import ANIMS
from mt.ask import ask_anim
from mt.common import IMAGE_EXTS, VIDEO_EXTS, ask, ask_choice, c_existing_file, c_float, c_time, probe, yn
from mt.draw import Item
from mt.templates.t4.cards_art import (ASPECT_MAX, ASPECT_MIN, _aspect, _placeholder, t4_card_mask, t4_card_sprite, t4_load_media,
                                       t4_video_frame)
from mt.templates.t4.cfg import _hex
from mt.templates.t4.frame import t4_frame_cfg
from mt.templates.t4.kit import connector
from mt.templates.t4.nameplate import T4_SAFE, _anchor as _corner, _bool, _cached, _flt, _font, _frame_mask, _place, _window
from mt.templates.t4.tiles import ANCHORS, TileItem, _anchor, _coord, _place_xy, _point, _warn_overlaps
from mt.templates.t4.timing import _num as _secs, t4_ask_when
from mt.templates.t4.warn import t4_warn

T4_CARD = {"on": True, "media": "", "label": {}, "anchor": "top-right", "xy": None, "width": 480.0, "height": None, "inset_x": 0.0, "inset_y": 0.0,
           "color": "#19d3ff", "connector": {}, "start": 0.0, "trigger": "", "end": None, "hold": None, "anim": "auto", "anim_dur": 0.45, "seg_start": None, "seg_end": None}
_CONN = {"target": None, "points": [], "side": "auto", "style": "elbow", "attach": 0.35, "color": "", "end_dot": True, "width": 2.5, "glow": 9.0}
_TOKENS = ("card", "card-top", "card-bottom", "card-left", "card-right")
_SPR = {}


# ---- config -----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
def _cpoint(p):
    """[x, y] | 'x,y' | a card token -> [x, y] | token | None."""
    if isinstance(p, str) and p.strip().lower() in _TOKENS:
        return p.strip().lower()
    q = _point(p)
    return q if isinstance(q, list) else None


def _label(raw):
    """'TEXT' | {text, color, anchor} -> {text, color, anchor} or {} (no label)."""
    d = raw if isinstance(raw, dict) else {"text": raw}
    t = d.get("text")
    t = str(t).strip()[:80] if isinstance(t, (str, int, float)) and not isinstance(t, bool) else ""
    if t.lower() in ("", "-", "none"):
        return {}
    c = d.get("color")
    return {"text": t, "color": _hex(c, "") if isinstance(c, str) else "", "anchor": _corner(d.get("anchor"), "bottom-right")}


def _connector(raw):
    """Connector dict | [x, y] shorthand -> complete dict, or {} when it has neither a target nor >= 2 points."""
    if isinstance(raw, (list, tuple, str)):
        raw = {"target": raw}
    if not isinstance(raw, dict):
        return {}
    out = copy.deepcopy(_CONN)
    out["target"] = _cpoint(raw.get("target"))
    out["target"] = out["target"] if isinstance(out["target"], list) else None
    pts = [_cpoint(p) for p in raw.get("points", [])] if isinstance(raw.get("points"), (list, tuple)) else []
    out["points"] = [p for p in pts if p is not None][:12]
    side, style = str(raw.get("side", "")).strip().lower(), str(raw.get("style", "")).strip().lower()
    out["side"] = side if side in ("left", "right", "top", "bottom") else "auto"
    out["style"] = "line" if style in ("line", "straight") else "elbow"
    out["attach"] = _flt(raw.get("attach"), 0.05, 0.95, 0.35)
    out["color"] = _hex(raw.get("color"), "") if isinstance(raw.get("color"), str) else ""
    ed = raw.get("end_dot")
    out["end_dot"] = ed if ed in (True, False, "both", "start") else True
    out["width"], out["glow"] = _flt(raw.get("width"), 0.5, 30, 2.5), _flt(raw.get("glow"), 0, 60, 9.0)
    return out if (out["target"] is not None or len(out["points"]) >= 2) else {}


def _card(raw):
    out = copy.deepcopy(T4_CARD)
    out.update({k: copy.deepcopy(v) for k, v in raw.items() if k not in out})                          # extras survive
    m = raw.get("media")
    out["media"] = m.strip() if isinstance(m, str) else ""
    out["seg_start"], out["seg_end"] = _secs(raw.get("seg_start")), _secs(raw.get("seg_end"))
    if out["seg_start"] is not None and out["seg_start"] < 0:
        out["seg_start"] = 0.0
    if out["seg_end"] is not None and out["seg_end"] <= (out["seg_start"] or 0.0) + 0.05:
        out["seg_end"] = None
    out["label"] = _label(raw.get("label"))
    out["anchor"] = _anchor(raw.get("anchor"), "top-right")
    xy = _point(raw.get("xy"))
    out["xy"] = xy if isinstance(xy, list) else None
    out["width"] = _flt(raw.get("width"), 120, 1800, 480.0)
    h = _flt(raw.get("height"), 80, 2400, None)
    out["height"] = h
    out["inset_x"], out["inset_y"] = _flt(raw.get("inset_x"), 0, 600, 0.0), _flt(raw.get("inset_y"), 0, 600, 0.0)
    out["color"] = _hex(raw.get("color"), "#19d3ff")
    out["connector"] = _connector(raw.get("connector"))
    trig = raw.get("trigger")
    out["trigger"] = trig.strip() if isinstance(trig, str) else ""
    out["start"] = raw["start"] if raw.get("start") is not None else (None if out["trigger"] else 0.0)      # a phrase never falls back to 0
    out["end"], out["hold"] = raw.get("end"), raw.get("hold")
    a = raw.get("anim")
    a = ("reveal" if a.strip().lower() == "typewriter" else a.strip().lower()) if isinstance(a, str) else "auto"
    out["anim"] = a if a in ANIMS or a in ("auto", "pop", "none") else "auto"
    out["anim_dur"] = _flt(raw.get("anim_dur"), 0.0, 5.0, 0.45)
    out["on"] = _bool(raw.get("on"), True)
    return out


def t4_cards_cfg(raw):
    """Normalise cfg['t4']['cards'] -> list of complete cards. Accepts a list, one card dict or {on, cards: [...]} (module default {'on': False}
    = no cards). Never raises; junk entries are dropped."""
    try:
        if isinstance(raw, dict) and "media" in raw:
            raw = [raw]
        elif isinstance(raw, dict):
            raw = raw.get("cards") if _bool(raw.get("on"), False) else []
        return [_card(c) for c in raw if isinstance(c, dict)] if isinstance(raw, (list, tuple)) else []
    except Exception:
        return []


# ---- connector geometry -----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
def _side(side, rect, tx, ty):
    """'auto' = the card side that faces the target: the axis with the larger gap decides (left / right beside the card, top / bottom above or
    below it); a target inside the card's footprint leaves from the bottom."""
    if side != "auto":
        return side
    x0, y0, x1, y1 = rect
    gx, gy = max(x0 - tx, tx - x1, 0.0), max(y0 - ty, ty - y1, 0.0)
    if gx > 0 and gx >= gy:
        return "left" if tx < x0 else "right"
    return ("top" if ty < y0 else "bottom") if gy > 0 else "bottom"


def _edge_point(rect, side, attach):
    x0, y0, x1, y1 = rect
    return {"left": (x0, y0 + (y1 - y0) * attach), "right": (x1, y0 + (y1 - y0) * attach),
            "top": (x0 + (x1 - x0) * attach, y0), "bottom": (x0 + (x1 - x0) * attach, y1)}[side]


def _elbow(start, target, side, style):
    """start -> target: one straight segment ('line'), or straight - 45 degree diagonal - straight along the leaving direction (R2)."""
    (sx, sy), (tx, ty) = start, target
    horiz = side in ("left", "right")
    along, across = (abs(tx - sx), abs(ty - sy)) if horiz else (abs(ty - sy), abs(tx - sx))
    if style == "line" or across < 2 or along <= across + 4:
        return [start, target]
    a = (along - across) / 2.0                                   # the diagonal eats `across` of the run; the rest is split before / after it
    d = (1 if (tx > sx if horiz else ty > sy) else -1)
    p1, p2 = ((sx + d * a, sy), (sx + d * (a + across), ty)) if horiz else ((sx, sy + d * a), (tx, sy + d * (a + across)))
    return [start, p1, p2, target]


def _conn_points(c, rect, W, H, s):
    """Resolve the connector to canvas px points; everything is clamped so the visible ink (glow tail + end dot) stays in the 48 px safe area."""
    x0, y0, x1, y1 = rect
    named = {"card": ((x0 + x1) / 2.0, (y0 + y1) / 2.0), "card-top": ((x0 + x1) / 2.0, y0), "card-bottom": ((x0 + x1) / 2.0, y1),
             "card-left": (x0, (y0 + y1) / 2.0), "card-right": (x1, (y0 + y1) / 2.0)}
    if c["points"] and len(c["points"]) >= 2:
        pts = [named[p] if isinstance(p, str) else (_coord(p[0], W, s), _coord(p[1], H, s)) for p in c["points"]]
    else:
        tx, ty = _coord(c["target"][0], W, s), _coord(c["target"][1], H, s)
        side = _side(c["side"], rect, tx, ty)
        pts = _elbow(_edge_point(rect, side, c["attach"]), (tx, ty), side, c["style"])
    m = (T4_SAFE + c["glow"] * 1.6 + 2) * s
    return [(min(max(x, m), W - m), min(max(y, m), H - m)) for x, y in pts]


# ---- items ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
def _kind(c):
    """'original' (a segment of the main video) | 'video' (a clip file) | 'image'."""
    m = str(c.get("media") or "").strip()
    if m.lower() == "original":
        return "original"
    return "video" if m.lower().endswith(VIDEO_EXTS) else "image"


def _src(c, cfg):
    return cfg.get("video") if _kind(c) == "original" else c.get("media")


_ASP = {}


def _vid_aspect(src):
    """Width / height of a video file, clamped like pictures; 4:3 when it cannot be probed."""
    key = (str(src), os.path.getmtime(src) if src and os.path.isfile(str(src)) else 0)
    if key not in _ASP:
        try:
            i = probe(src)
            _ASP[key] = max(ASPECT_MIN, min(ASPECT_MAX, i["w"] / float(i["h"])))
        except (Exception, SystemExit):
            _ASP[key] = 4.0 / 3.0
    return _ASP[key]


def _foot(spr):
    """Frame-only sprite + the picture's footprint in its alpha = what the finished card covers. Used only to place a video card."""
    x0, y0, x1, y1 = spr.info["card"]
    lay = Image.new("L", spr.size, 0)
    lay.paste(t4_card_mask(x1 - x0, y1 - y0), (x0, y0))
    foot = spr.copy()
    foot.putalpha(ImageChops.lighter(spr.getchannel("A"), lay))
    return foot


def _cut_card(csp, pos, cw, ch):
    """Remove the card's picture area from a connector sprite (its start stays hidden under the card, as with picture cards)."""
    org = csp.info["origin"]
    hole = Image.new("L", csp.size, 255)
    hole.paste(ImageChops.invert(t4_card_mask(cw, ch)), (int(round(pos[0])) + org[0], int(round(pos[1])) + org[1]))
    csp.putalpha(ImageChops.multiply(csp.getchannel("A"), hole))
    return csp


def _card_sprite(c, s, W, H, cfg=None, live=False, ft=0.0):
    """Cached (sprite, warning). Pictures: the full card. Video / original: live = the frame only (cards_video bakes the picture into a
    clip under it); not live = the full card with the frame at time ft (--still) or a gray placeholder (missing source)."""
    if _kind(c) == "image":
        return _card_sprite_img(c, s, W, H)
    src = _src(c, cfg or {})
    ok = bool(src) and os.path.isfile(str(src))
    cw = c["width"] * s
    ch = c["height"] * s if c["height"] else cw / (_vid_aspect(src) if ok else 4.0 / 3.0)
    lab, fp = c["label"], _font(c.get("font"))
    live = bool(live and ok)
    key = ("v", _kind(c), str(src), _mtime(src), live, round(float(ft), 2), repr(lab), round(cw), round(ch), c["color"], fp)

    def make():
        msg = None
        if not ok:
            msg, img = "cards: video '%s' is missing - gray placeholder." % (Path(str(src)).name if src else "(none)"), _placeholder()
        elif live:
            img = Image.new("RGB", (4, 4))
        else:
            img = t4_video_frame(src, ft)
            if img is None:
                msg, img = "cards: could not read a frame of '%s' - gray placeholder." % Path(str(src)).name, _placeholder()
        spr, m2 = t4_card_sprite("", lab.get("text", ""), cw, ch, c["color"], fp, lab.get("anchor", "bottom-right"),
                                 lab.get("color") or None, with_picture=not live, media_img=img)
        return spr, msg or m2
    return _cached(_SPR, key, make, 16)


def _card_sprite_img(c, s, W, H):
    """Cached (sprite, warning) of one card at canvas scale s."""
    cw = c["width"] * s
    if c["height"]:
        ch = c["height"] * s
    else:
        img, _msg = t4_load_media(c["media"])
        ch = cw / _aspect(img)
    lab, fp = c["label"], _font(c.get("font"))
    key = (c["media"], _mtime(c["media"]), repr(lab), round(cw), round(ch), c["color"], fp)
    return _cached(_SPR, key, lambda: t4_card_sprite(c["media"], lab.get("text", ""), cw, ch, c["color"], fp, lab.get("anchor", "bottom-right"),
                                                     lab.get("color") or None), 16)


def _mtime(path):
    return os.path.getmtime(path) if path and os.path.isfile(str(path)) else 0


def _make_item(spr, st, en, cx, cy, c):
    if c["anim"] in ("auto", "pop"):
        return TileItem(spr, st, en, cx, cy, "t4_pop", c["anim_dur"], out=True)
    return Item(spr, st, en, cx, cy, c["anim"], c["anim_dur"], out=True)


def _one_card(cfg, c, idx, duration, W, H, s, fm, placed, plans=None):
    label = "card %d" % (idx + 1)
    win = _window(cfg, c, label, duration, 5.0)
    if win is None:
        return []
    kind = _kind(c)
    src = _src(c, cfg)
    live = kind != "image" and bool(src) and os.path.isfile(str(src)) and not cfg.get("_t2_still")     # live = baked into a video layer
    ft = c["seg_start"] if c["seg_start"] is not None else (win[0] if kind == "original" else 0.0)
    spr, msg = _card_sprite(c, s, W, H, cfg, live, ft)
    foot = _foot(spr) if live else spr
    if msg:
        t4_warn(msg)
    pl = _place_xy(foot, c["xy"], W, H, s) if c["xy"] else _place(foot, c["anchor"], W, H, s, c["inset_x"], c["inset_y"], fm, label)
    if pl is None:
        return []
    cx, cy, ink, _ = pl
    placed.append((label, ink))
    left, top = cx - spr.width / 2.0, cy - spr.height / 2.0
    x0, y0, x1, y1 = spr.info["card"]
    rect = (left + x0, top + y0, left + x1, top + y1)
    last = max(win[0], win[1] - 0.1)
    if live and plans is not None:                            # cards_video bakes the picture of this card
        plans.append({"c": c, "idx": idx, "win": win, "cx": cx, "cy": cy, "size": spr.size, "pic": (x0, y0, x1 - x0, y1 - y0),
                      "src": src, "kind": kind})
    items = []
    if c["connector"]:
        k = c["connector"]
        col = k["color"] or c["color"]
        csp = connector(_conn_points(k, rect, W, H, s), col, k["end_dot"], k["width"] * s, k["glow"] * s)
        ox, oy = csp.info["origin"]
        if live:
            _cut_card(csp, (left + x0, top + y0), x1 - x0, y1 - y0)
        items.append(Item(csp, min(win[0] + c["anim_dur"] * 0.8, last), win[1], -ox + csp.width / 2.0, -oy + csp.height / 2.0, "fade", c["anim_dur"], out=True))
    items.append(_make_item(spr, win[0], win[1], cx, cy, c))                              # last = on top of the connector
    return items


def t4_cards_items(cfg, duration, plans=None):
    """Items of every card: [connector (fade), card (pop)] per card, all ending together. Never raises; [] when the module is off."""
    t4 = cfg.get("t4") if isinstance(cfg, dict) and isinstance(cfg.get("t4"), dict) else {}
    cards = [c for c in t4_cards_cfg(t4.get("cards")) if c["on"]]
    if not cards:
        return []
    try:
        W, H = int(cfg["canvas"][0]), int(cfg["canvas"][1])
    except (KeyError, TypeError, ValueError, IndexError):
        t4_warn("t4 cards: the job has no canvas - skipped.")
        return []
    s, items, placed = min(W, H) / 1080.0, [], []
    try:
        fm = _frame_mask(W, H, t4_frame_cfg(t4.get("frame")), s)
        for i, c in enumerate(cards):
            items += _one_card(cfg, c, i, duration, W, H, s, fm, placed, plans)
        _warn_overlaps(placed)
    except Exception as e:
        t4_warn("t4 cards: could not be drawn (%s: %s) - skipped." % (type(e).__name__, e))
    return items


# ---- questions --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
def t4_card_plans(cfg, duration):
    """Placement of every video / original-segment card, computed by the same code as the Items: [{c, idx, win, cx, cy, size, pic, src, kind}]
    (pic = x, y, w, h of the picture inside the sprite). Used by cards_video to bake the clips."""
    plans = []
    t4_cards_items(cfg, duration, plans)
    return plans


def _c_target(s):
    p = _cpoint(s.strip())
    if p is None or isinstance(p, str):
        raise ValueError("enter a point like  300,900  or  25%,60%  (px or %)")
    return p


def t4_ask_image_cards():
    """Question group 'cards' (called by t4_ask_config). -> a list with ONE card, or {'on': False}. More cards: edit the job file."""
    if not yn("Add an image card (picture in a neon border, optional label + connector - references R2 / R8)?", default=False):
        return {"on": False}
    def _c_media(s):
        return "original" if s.strip().lower() == "original" else c_existing_file(IMAGE_EXTS + VIDEO_EXTS)(s)
    c = {"media": ask("Card picture or video (PNG / JPG / MP4 path, or the word original = a segment of the main video)", key="t4_card_img", cast=_c_media)}
    if c["media"] == "original" or c["media"].lower().endswith(VIDEO_EXTS):
        for k, q in (("seg_start", "Segment start (seconds or mm:ss; Enter = %s)" % ("the card's start time" if c["media"] == "original" else "0")),
                     ("seg_end", "Segment end (seconds or mm:ss; Enter = %s)" % ("the card's hold time" if c["media"] == "original" else "the clip's end / loop"))):
            v = ask(q, allow_empty=True, cast=c_time)
            if v not in (None, ""):
                c[k] = v
    lab = ask("Label text on the card (Enter / '-' = none, e.g. LIVE CODING DEVELOPMENT)", default="-", key="t4_card_label", cast=lambda s: s.strip())
    c["label"] = "" if lab in ("", "-") else lab
    c["width"] = ask("Card width (px, for a 1080 px short side)", default=T4_CARD["width"], key="t4_card_width", cast=c_float)
    a = ask_choice("Where?", ["Top-left", "Top", "Top-right", "Left", "Center", "Right", "Bottom-left", "Bottom", "Bottom-right"], default=3, key="t4_card_anchor")
    c["anchor"] = ANCHORS[a - 1]
    if yn("Add a connector line ending in a glowing dot?", default=False):
        c["connector"] = {"target": ask("Dot position  x,y  (px or %, e.g. 22%,38%)", default="20%,40%", key="t4_card_target", cast=_c_target)}
    c.update(t4_ask_when("Image card", 5.0, key="t4_card_when"))
    c["anim"] = ask_anim("Card animation (auto = pop in, fade out)", default="pop", key="t4_card_anim")
    return [c]


====================================================================================================
FILE: mt\templates\t4\cards_art.py
====================================================================================================
"""mt.templates.t4.cards_art - Template 4: the drawing half of the 'cards' module (refs R2 right card, R8 PiP card + label).
One IMAGE card = the picture cut to a chamfered rectangle, a neon border (glow + band + bright core) around it, two small bright accent dashes
inside the top-left corner and - optionally - a LABEL TAG overlapping one corner of the card: dark glass slanted panel, bold slanted capitals
(line 1 white, line 2 in the card colour), three slanted stripes tucked behind the panel's outer end (R8: LIVE CODING / DEVELOPMENT).
Everything is composed into ONE RGBA sprite (glow margins included, cropped to its ink); nothing is drawn per frame.
Public: t4_load_media(path), t4_split_label(text), t4_label_sprite(lines, color, font, size, left), t4_card_sprite(media, label, cw, ch, color, font)
-> (sprite, msg|None); sprite.info['card'] = (x0, y0, x1, y1) = the picture rectangle inside the sprite."""
import math
import os
from pathlib import Path

from PIL import Image, ImageChops, ImageDraw, ImageOps

from mt.common import FFMPEG, VIDEO_EXTS
from mt.draw import _t2_alpha_paste, t2_lighten, t2_line_mask, t2_paint, t2_poly_mask, t2_rgb, t2_text_mask
from mt.fonts import has_arabic
from mt.templates.t4.kit import _glass_sprite, chamfer_rect, hazard_stripes, kit_paste, neon_stroke

ASPECT_MIN, ASPECT_MAX = 0.75, 1.8          # picture width / height limits (outside: centre-cropped to the limit)
LABEL_FRAC = 0.082                          # label font size = this x card width (R8: ~0.08)
LABEL_SHEAR = 0.20                          # fake italic of the label capitals
SHORT_LABEL = 11                            # labels up to this many characters stay on ONE line
LABEL_TINT = (6, 14, 36, 240)               # label glass: nearly opaque (R8) so the picture does not show through the text
OVERHANG = 0.38                             # share of the label height that sticks out of the card edge (R8: ~0.4)
_WHITE = (255, 255, 255)


# ---- media ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
def t4_load_media(path):
    """-> (RGB image | None, warning | None). Missing / unreadable file -> None + a one-line message; video files are P13 (not yet)."""
    name = Path(str(path)).name if path else ""
    if not path or not os.path.isfile(str(path)):
        return None, "cards: image '%s' is missing - gray placeholder." % (name or "(empty path)")
    if str(path).lower().endswith(VIDEO_EXTS):
        return None, "cards: '%s' is a video - videos are baked by cards_video.py (P13), not loaded as a picture - gray placeholder." % name
    try:
        with Image.open(str(path)) as im:
            im.seek(0)
            return im.convert("RGB"), None
    except Exception:
        return None, "cards: '%s' is not a readable image - gray placeholder." % name


def _placeholder(w=640, h=480):
    im = Image.new("RGB", (w, h), (112, 116, 124))
    d = ImageDraw.Draw(im)
    d.rectangle((w * 0.2, h * 0.24, w * 0.8, h * 0.76), fill=(70, 74, 82))
    d.polygon([(w * 0.26, h * 0.68), (w * 0.42, h * 0.46), (w * 0.54, h * 0.60), (w * 0.62, h * 0.52), (w * 0.74, h * 0.68)], fill=(158, 164, 174))
    d.ellipse((w * 0.62, h * 0.32, w * 0.70, h * 0.32 + w * 0.08), fill=(158, 164, 174))
    return im


def _aspect(media):
    """Picture width / height for a card whose height is not given: the media's own shape, clamped."""
    return max(ASPECT_MIN, min(ASPECT_MAX, media.width / float(max(1, media.height)))) if media is not None else 4.0 / 3.0


# ---- label ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
def t4_split_label(text):
    """'LIVE CODING DEVELOPMENT' -> ['LIVE CODING', 'DEVELOPMENT'] (balanced); an explicit newline or '|' decides; short / one-word text = 1 line."""
    t = " ".join(str(text or "").split()) if "\n" not in str(text or "") and "|" not in str(text or "") else str(text)
    for sep in ("\n", "|"):
        if sep in t:
            parts = [p.strip() for p in t.split(sep, 1)]
            return [p for p in parts if p][:2]
    words = t.split(" ") if t else []
    if len(words) < 2 or len(t) <= SHORT_LABEL:
        return [t] if t else []
    best = min(range(1, len(words)), key=lambda i: (max(len(" ".join(words[:i])), len(" ".join(words[i:]))), i))
    return [" ".join(words[:best]), " ".join(words[best:])]


def _label_masks(lines, font, size):
    return [t2_text_mask(t.upper() if not has_arabic(t) else t, font, size, 0.01, LABEL_SHEAR) for t in lines]


def _label_geometry(masks, size):
    gap = size * 0.22
    tw, th = max(m.width for m in masks), sum(m.height for m in masks) + gap * (len(masks) - 1)
    ph = th + 2 * size * 0.46
    sl = ph * 0.16                                                      # lean of the outer edge
    return tw, th, gap, ph, sl, tw + 2 * size * 0.62 + sl


def t4_label_sprite(lines, color, font, size, left=False):
    """The label tag: stripes + dark glass slanted panel + text. left=True mirrors the shape (outer end on the left, for left corners).
    -> RGBA image (glow included); .info['panel'] = (x0, y0, x1, y1) of the panel inside it. Empty lines -> None."""
    lines = [t for t in lines if t]
    if not lines:
        return None
    masks = _label_masks(lines, font, size)
    tw, th, gap, ph, sl, pw = _label_geometry(masks, size)
    cr, bw, glow = ph * 0.20, max(2.0, size * 0.085), max(4.0, size * 0.28)
    pts = [(sl, 0), (pw - cr * 0.55, 0), (pw, cr * 0.55), (pw, ph - cr), (pw - cr, ph), (0, ph)]
    panel = _glass_sprite(t2_poly_mask(pts), LABEL_TINT, color, bw, glow)
    sh, stripe = ph * 0.62, ph * 0.10
    off = sh * math.tan(math.radians(28))
    sw = off + 3 * stripe + 2 * ph * 0.07 + 0.5
    bars = hazard_stripes((sw, sh), 28, color, max(3.0, size * 0.12), stripe=stripe, gap=ph * 0.07)
    pad = int(glow * 2.6) + 6
    ox, oy = pad + int(sw), pad
    cv = Image.new("RGBA", (int(ox + pw + pad), int(ph + 2 * pad)), (0, 0, 0, 0))
    kit_paste(cv, bars, ox - sw * 0.80, oy + ph - sh - ph * 0.04)
    kit_paste(cv, panel, ox, oy)
    box = (ox, oy, ox + pw, oy + ph)
    cx = ox + (sl + pw) / 2.0
    if left:
        cv = ImageOps.mirror(cv)
        cx, box = cv.width - cx, (cv.width - box[2], box[1], cv.width - box[0], box[3])
    y = oy + size * 0.46
    for i, m in enumerate(masks):
        x = int(round(cx - m.width / 2.0))
        t2_paint(cv, (m, (x, int(round(y + size * 0.05)))), (0, 0, 0), 0.55)                       # soft drop shadow
        t2_paint(cv, (m, (x, int(round(y)))), _WHITE if (i == 0 or len(masks) == 1) else t2_lighten(t2_rgb(color), 0.12))
        y += m.height + gap
    cv.info["panel"] = tuple(int(round(v)) for v in box)
    return cv


# ---- card -------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
def _picture(media, cw, ch, mask):
    """Media cover-cropped to cw x ch and cut by the chamfer mask -> RGBA."""
    pic = ImageOps.fit(media.convert("RGB"), (cw, ch), Image.LANCZOS, centering=(0.5, 0.5)).convert("RGBA")
    m, (mx, my) = mask
    a = Image.new("L", (cw, ch), 0)
    a.paste(m, (mx, my))
    pic.putalpha(a)
    return pic


def _accent(cv, ox, oy, cw, cut, bw, color):
    """Two short bright dashes under the top edge, right of the top-left cut (R8): a pale thick one and a shorter coloured one."""
    y, x0 = oy + bw * 2.6, ox + cut * 1.7
    c = t2_rgb(color)
    t2_paint(cv, t2_line_mask([(x0, y), (x0 + cw * 0.15, y)], max(1.5, bw * 0.8), caps=False), t2_lighten(c, 0.85), 0.95)
    t2_paint(cv, t2_line_mask([(x0 + cw * 0.17, y), (x0 + cw * 0.215, y)], max(1.5, bw * 0.8), caps=False), c, 0.95)


def _label_pos(lab, rect, anchor):
    """Top-left of the label image so its panel overlaps the card corner `anchor` and sticks out by OVERHANG of its height."""
    px0, py0, px1, py1 = lab.info["panel"]
    ph, (cx0, cy0, cx1, cy1) = py1 - py0, rect
    x = (cx1 + 0.012 * (cx1 - cx0) - px1) if anchor.endswith("right") else (cx0 - 0.012 * (cx1 - cx0) - px0)
    y = (cy1 + OVERHANG * ph - py1) if anchor.startswith("bottom") else (cy0 - OVERHANG * ph - py0)
    return int(round(x)), int(round(y))


def t4_card_mask(cw, ch):
    """The picture's chamfer mask as an 'L' image cw x ch - the exact shape t4_card_sprite cuts its picture with (cards_video bakes clips with it)."""
    cw, ch = max(40, int(round(cw))), max(40, int(round(ch)))
    k = min(cw, ch) * 0.06
    m, (mx, my) = chamfer_rect((cw, ch), (k, k * 0.7, k, k * 1.2))
    a = Image.new("L", (cw, ch), 0)
    a.paste(m, (mx, my))
    return a


def t4_video_frame(path, t=0.0):
    """One RGB frame of a video at time t (seconds) -> PIL image, or None when it cannot be read. Used by --still."""
    import subprocess
    import tempfile
    if not path or not os.path.isfile(str(path)) or not FFMPEG:
        return None
    fd, png = tempfile.mkstemp(suffix=".png")
    os.close(fd)
    try:
        r = subprocess.run([FFMPEG, "-y", "-hide_banner", "-loglevel", "error", "-ss", "%.3f" % max(0.0, float(t)), "-i", str(path),
                            "-frames:v", "1", png], capture_output=True)
        return Image.open(png).convert("RGB").copy() if r.returncode == 0 and os.path.getsize(png) > 0 else None
    except Exception:
        return None
    finally:
        try:
            os.remove(png)
        except OSError:
            pass


def t4_card_sprite(media, label, cw, ch, color, font, label_anchor="bottom-right", label_color=None, with_picture=True, media_img=None):
    """One finished card -> (RGBA sprite, warning | None). media = path; label = text ('' = none); cw / ch = picture size px; sprite.info['card']
    = the picture rect inside the sprite. A missing / unreadable / video media gives a gray placeholder (+ the one-line warning)."""
    if media_img is not None:                                   # a frame supplied by the caller (video cards in a --still)
        img, msg = media_img, None
    else:
        img, msg = t4_load_media(media)
    cw, ch = max(40, int(round(cw))), max(40, int(round(ch)))
    k = min(cw, ch) * 0.06
    mask = chamfer_rect((cw, ch), (k, k * 0.7, k, k * 1.2))
    bw, glow = max(2.5, cw * 0.0085), max(6.0, cw * 0.026)
    lab = None
    if label:
        size = cw * LABEL_FRAC
        lab = t4_label_sprite(t4_split_label(label), label_color or color, font, size, label_anchor.endswith("left"))
        if lab is not None and lab.info["panel"][2] - lab.info["panel"][0] > cw * 1.0:               # too wide: shrink the text once
            size *= max(0.55, cw * 1.0 / (lab.info["panel"][2] - lab.info["panel"][0]))
            lab = t4_label_sprite(t4_split_label(label), label_color or color, font, size, label_anchor.endswith("left"))
    M = int(glow * 2.6) + 6 + (max(lab.width, lab.height) if lab is not None else 0)
    cv = Image.new("RGBA", (cw + 2 * M, ch + 2 * M), (0, 0, 0, 0))
    pic = _picture(img if img is not None else _placeholder(), cw, ch, mask)
    if with_picture:                                            # False = frame only: the picture is a baked video layer under it
        cv.alpha_composite(pic, (M, M))
    kit_paste(cv, neon_stroke(mask, color, bw, glow), M, M)
    _accent(cv, M, M, cw, k, bw, color)
    rect = (M, M, M + cw, M + ch)
    if lab is not None:
        lx, ly = _label_pos(lab, rect, label_anchor)
        _t2_alpha_paste(cv, lab, lx, ly)
    ab = cv.getchannel("A")
    if not with_picture:                                        # same crop box as the full card: add the picture's footprint
        fa = Image.new("L", cv.size, 0)
        fa.paste(pic.getchannel("A"), (M, M))
        ab = ImageChops.lighter(ab, fa)
    bb = ab.getbbox() or (0, 0, cv.width, cv.height)
    out = cv.crop(bb)
    out.info["card"] = (rect[0] - bb[0], rect[1] - bb[1], rect[2] - bb[0], rect[3] - bb[1])
    return out, msg


====================================================================================================
FILE: mt\templates\t4\cards_video.py
====================================================================================================
"""mt.templates.t4.cards_video - Template 4: VIDEO cards (refs R2 right card, R8 PiP card) - the baking half of the 'cards' module.
A card whose media is a clip file or a segment of the original video ('original' + seg_start / seg_end, see cards.py) is drawn in two layers:
  - the PICTURE = a muted clip cut by the card's chamfer mask, baked here into a small alpha video (qtrle) and overlaid by FFmpeg as a video
    layer (area 'canvas', key '_xy'), i.e. above the foreground plate, exactly like the T3 floating video cards;
  - the neon border + accent dashes + label tag = the ordinary card sprite (cards_art, with_picture=False), an Item over the clip.
Both use the same sprite size, centre and Item animation (t4_pop / chosen anim), so border and picture pop in and fade out together.
Memory: FFmpeg decodes the source straight at the picture's pixel size (-ss / -t / fps / scale / crop / rgb24 on a pipe = the pre-scaled proxy of
prepare_video_layers), so only one small frame is held at a time; nothing is written but the final alpha clip. The clip is muted (-an).
Source rules: 'original' starts at seg_start (default = the card's start time); a clip file loops when it has no seg_end and starts at 0, otherwise
it plays once; when the source ends before the card does, the last frame is held. Missing source / bake failure -> one t4_warn, that card has no picture.
Public: t4_prepare_card_layers(cfg, info, tmpdir) = the registry hook prepare_layers for id 5 (render.py calls it for template ids 2, 3, 5)."""
import math
import os
import subprocess
from pathlib import Path

from PIL import Image

from mt.anim import anim_state
from mt.common import FFMPEG, _read_exact
from mt.draw import draw_item
from mt.templates.t4.cards import _make_item, t4_card_plans
from mt.templates.t4.cards_art import t4_card_mask
from mt.templates.t4.warn import t4_warn

SAMPLES = 40                    # anim_state samples per direction when measuring how far the animated card reaches
MARGIN = 6                      # px around that reach
GRAY = b"\x5c"                  # filler byte when the source gives no frame at all


def _anim_name(c):
    return "t4_pop" if c["anim"] in ("auto", "pop") else c["anim"]


def _even(a0, a1, limit):
    if (a1 - a0) % 2:
        if a1 < limit:
            a1 += 1
        elif a0 > 0:
            a0 -= 1
        else:
            a1 -= 1
    return a0, a1


def _extent(plan, W, H):
    """Canvas rect (x0, y0, x1, y1) that covers the card at every moment of its animation: anim_state is sampled over the whole 0..1 range
    (plus the pop-out range for t4_pop), clamped to the canvas, even size."""
    w, h = plan["size"]
    name = _anim_name(plan["c"])
    ps = [i / float(SAMPLES) for i in range(SAMPLES + 1)]
    if name == "t4_pop":
        ps += [-p for p in ps[1:]]
    lo_x = lo_y = 1e9
    hi_x = hi_y = -1e9
    for p in ps:
        try:
            _a, dx, dy, sc, rot, _r = anim_state(name, p, W, H)
        except Exception:
            dx, dy, sc, rot = 0.0, 0.0, 1.0, 0.0
        sc = float(sc)
        if abs(rot) > 0.05:
            hw = hh = math.hypot(w, h) * sc / 2.0
        else:
            hw, hh = w * sc / 2.0, h * sc / 2.0
        lo_x, hi_x = min(lo_x, plan["cx"] + dx - hw), max(hi_x, plan["cx"] + dx + hw)
        lo_y, hi_y = min(lo_y, plan["cy"] + dy - hh), max(hi_y, plan["cy"] + dy + hh)
    x0, x1 = _even(max(0, int(math.floor(lo_x)) - MARGIN), min(W, int(math.ceil(hi_x)) + MARGIN), W)
    y0, y1 = _even(max(0, int(math.floor(lo_y)) - MARGIN), min(H, int(math.ceil(hi_y)) + MARGIN), H)
    if x1 - x0 < 2 or y1 - y0 < 2:
        raise ValueError("the card is outside the canvas")
    return x0, y0, x1, y1


def _reader_cmd(plan, win, nfr, cw, ch, fps_s):
    """FFmpeg command that decodes the source at the picture size and writes raw rgb24 frames to stdout (muted)."""
    c, original = plan["c"], plan["kind"] == "original"
    seg_s, seg_e = c["seg_start"], c["seg_end"]
    ss = seg_s if seg_s is not None else (win[0] if original else 0.0)
    if seg_e is not None and seg_e - ss < 0.05:
        seg_e = None
    cmd = [FFMPEG, "-hide_banner", "-loglevel", "error"]
    if (not original) and seg_e is None and ss == 0.0:
        cmd += ["-stream_loop", "-1"]                                     # a whole clip file loops while the card is shown
    cmd += ["-ss", "%.3f" % ss, "-i", str(plan["src"])]
    if seg_e is not None:
        cmd += ["-t", "%.3f" % (seg_e - ss)]
    cmd += ["-an", "-vf", f"fps={fps_s},scale={cw}:{ch}:force_original_aspect_ratio=increase,crop={cw}:{ch},format=rgb24",
            "-frames:v", str(nfr), "-f", "rawvideo", "-pix_fmt", "rgb24", "pipe:1"]
    return cmd


def _picture_sprite(raw, cw, ch, mask, size, pos):
    """One decoded frame -> RGBA sprite of the card's full size: the frame cut by the chamfer mask, at the picture rect, rest transparent."""
    pic = Image.frombytes("RGB", (cw, ch), raw).convert("RGBA")
    pic.putalpha(mask)
    spr = Image.new("RGBA", size, (0, 0, 0, 0))
    spr.paste(pic, pos)
    return spr


def _bake(cfg, plan, n, tmpdir, info, dur_eff):
    """Bake one card's picture into tmpdir/t4cardN.mov -> the video-layer dict for render.py."""
    c, win = plan["c"], plan["win"]
    W, H = int(cfg["canvas"][0]), int(cfg["canvas"][1])
    fps = info["fps"]
    fps_f = float(fps)
    fps_s = f"{fps.numerator}/{fps.denominator}" if fps.denominator != 1 else str(fps.numerator)
    s0, e_true = win
    e = min(e_true, dur_eff)
    nfr = max(1, int(math.ceil((e - s0) * fps_f)))
    x0, y0, x1, y1 = _extent(plan, W, H)
    Wp, Hp = x1 - x0, y1 - y0
    px, py, cw, ch = plan["pic"]
    mask, need = t4_card_mask(cw, ch), cw * ch * 3
    out = os.path.join(tmpdir, "t4card%d.mov" % n)
    print("Preparing video card %d (%s, %d frames)..." % (n + 1, Path(str(plan["src"])).name, nfr))
    rd = subprocess.Popen(_reader_cmd(plan, win, nfr, cw, ch, fps_s), stdout=subprocess.PIPE, stderr=subprocess.DEVNULL,
                          stdin=subprocess.DEVNULL)
    wr = subprocess.Popen([FFMPEG, "-y", "-hide_banner", "-loglevel", "error", "-f", "rawvideo", "-pix_fmt", "rgba",
                           "-video_size", f"{Wp}x{Hp}", "-framerate", fps_s, "-i", "pipe:0", "-c:v", "qtrle", "-pix_fmt", "argb", out],
                          stdin=subprocess.PIPE, stderr=subprocess.PIPE, stdout=subprocess.DEVNULL)
    last, it = None, None
    try:
        for i in range(nfr):
            raw = _read_exact(rd.stdout, need)
            if len(raw) != need:
                raw = last if last is not None else GRAY * need          # the source ended: hold the last frame
            last = raw
            spr = _picture_sprite(raw, cw, ch, mask, plan["size"], (px, py))
            if it is None:
                it = _make_item(spr, s0, e_true, plan["cx"] - x0, plan["cy"] - y0, c)
            else:
                it.sprite = spr
            canvas = Image.new("RGBA", (Wp, Hp), (0, 0, 0, 0))
            draw_item(canvas, it, anim_state(it.anim, it.progress(s0 + i / fps_f), W, H))
            wr.stdin.write(canvas.tobytes())
        wr.stdin.close()
    except (BrokenPipeError, OSError):
        pass
    finally:
        rd.kill()
        rd.wait()
    err = wr.stderr.read().decode("utf-8", "replace")
    if wr.wait() != 0 or not os.path.isfile(out):
        raise RuntimeError(err[-300:] or "FFmpeg could not write the clip")
    return {"path": out, "start": s0, "end": e, "area": "canvas", "mode": "fit", "fade": 0.0, "_xy": [x0, y0]}


def t4_prepare_card_layers(cfg, info, tmpdir):
    """Registry hook prepare_layers (id 5): one alpha clip per video / original-segment card -> list of video layers (drawn above the
    foreground plate, under the card's border Item). [] when there is none. Never raises: a failing card warns once and has no picture."""
    try:
        int(cfg["canvas"][0]), int(cfg["canvas"][1])
    except (KeyError, TypeError, ValueError, IndexError):
        return []
    dur_eff = info["duration"]
    if cfg.get("preview"):
        dur_eff = min(dur_eff, float(cfg["preview"]))
    layers = []
    for n, plan in enumerate(t4_card_plans(cfg, info["duration"])):
        if plan["win"][0] >= dur_eff:
            continue
        try:
            layers.append(_bake(cfg, plan, n, tmpdir, info, dur_eff))
        except (Exception, SystemExit) as e:
            t4_warn("cards: video card %d could not be prepared (%s: %s) - skipped." % (plan["idx"] + 1, type(e).__name__, e))
    return layers

====================================================================================================
FILE: mt\templates\t4\nameplate.py
====================================================================================================
"""mt.templates.t4.nameplate - Template 4 modules 'nameplate' (name tag, styles tag_a = ref R2 / tag_b = ref R10) and 'corner_list'
(ref R10: laptop icon in one bottom corner + a 3-row icon list in the other). Both are timed + animated overlay ITEMS (one sprite each,
drawn above the frame plate) - not part of the static foreground plate.
cfg['t4']['nameplate'] = {on, style 'tag_a'|'tag_b', name, role, icon, color, anchor, inset_x, inset_y, start|trigger, end|hold, anim, anim_dur}
cfg['t4']['corner_list'] = {on, color, icon ('laptop' | kit icon | '' = none), icon_anchor, anchor, rows [{icon, text}], size, inset_x, inset_y,
                            start|trigger, end|hold, anim, anim_dur}
  anchor     top-left | top-right | bottom-left | bottom-right (default: tag_a bottom-left, tag_b top-left, list bottom-right, laptop bottom-left).
  inset_x/y  extra px (short side 1080) inward from the corner margin.
  timing     as t4/timing.t4_time: `trigger` (spoken phrase in the captions) or `start` (default 0); `end` or `hold` (tag default 5 s; list
             default = to the end of the video); trigger not found -> one warning, skipped.
  anim       'auto' (default: slides in from its own side) | none | any mt.anim name; anim_dur seconds (default 0.45).
Placement: the VISIBLE ink (alpha > 24, glow tails included) sits at least 48 px (short side 1080) from every canvas edge. Frame on (cfg['t4']['frame']):
the item is moved along the vertical (down from a top corner, up from a bottom one) just far enough that its ink keeps ~10 px away from every
solid frame pixel (brackets, hazard stripes, tab, icon) - measured against the real frame plate, so a frame redesign needs no change here.
Public: T4_NAMEPLATE, T4_CORNER_LIST, t4_nameplate_cfg(raw), t4_corner_list_cfg(raw), t4_nameplate_items(cfg, duration), t4_ask_nameplate(), t4_ask_corner_list()."""
import copy
import math
import os
from pathlib import Path

from PIL import Image, ImageChops, ImageFilter

from mt.anim import ANIMS, PRESETS
from mt.ask import ask_anim
from mt.captions import caption_streams, parse_srt
from mt.common import ask, ask_choice, c_color, c_float, yn
from mt.draw import Item
from mt.fonts import default_font, t2_font
from mt.templates.t4.cfg import _hex
from mt.templates.t4.frame import t4_frame_cfg, t4_frame_plate
from mt.templates.t4.kit_icons import ICON_NAMES
from mt.templates.t4.nameplate_list import LIST_ICONS, T4_LIST, t4_laptop_sprite, t4_list_sprite
from mt.templates.t4.nameplate_tags import TAG_STYLES, t4_tag_sprite
from mt.templates.t4.timing import t4_ask_when, t4_time
from mt.templates.t4.warn import t4_warn

T4_SAFE = 48                                       # px at short side 1080: no ink closer than this to a canvas edge
INK_THR, FRAME_THR, GAP = 24, 60, 10               # sprite alpha = ink | frame alpha = solid | px kept between ink and frame
CORNERS = ("top-left", "top-right", "bottom-left", "bottom-right")
STYLE_ANCHOR = {"tag_a": "bottom-left", "tag_b": "top-left"}             # R2 / R10
DEFAULT_ROWS = [{"icon": "globe", "text": "Web Development"}, {"icon": "phone", "text": "Mobile Development"},
