                {"icon": "gear", "text": "Tech Content Creator"}]
MAX_ROWS = 5
_COMMON = {"on": False, "color": "#19D3FF", "inset_x": 0.0, "inset_y": 0.0, "start": 0.0, "trigger": "", "end": None, "hold": None,
           "anim": "auto", "anim_dur": 0.45}
T4_NAMEPLATE = dict(_COMMON, style="tag_a", name="Ahmed Ibrahim", role="Full Stack Developer", icon="code", anchor="bottom-left")
T4_CORNER_LIST = dict(_COMMON, icon="laptop", icon_anchor="bottom-left", anchor="bottom-right", rows=DEFAULT_ROWS, size=22.0)
_OFF = ("", "none", "off", "false", "no", "0", "-")
_ALIAS = {"tl": "top-left", "tr": "top-right", "bl": "bottom-left", "br": "bottom-right", "left-top": "top-left",
          "right-top": "top-right", "left-bottom": "bottom-left", "right-bottom": "bottom-right"}


# ---- config -----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
def _bool(v, default):
    if isinstance(v, str):
        return default if not v.strip() else v.strip().lower() not in _OFF
    return default if v is None else bool(v)


def _flt(v, lo, hi, default):
    try:
        x = float(v)
    except (TypeError, ValueError, OverflowError):
        return default
    return x if not isinstance(v, bool) and lo <= x <= hi else default           # nan fails the comparison -> default


def _anchor(v, default):
    s = str(v).strip().lower().replace("_", "-").replace(" ", "-") if isinstance(v, (str, int)) and not isinstance(v, bool) else ""
    s = _ALIAS.get(PRESETS.get(s, s), PRESETS.get(s, s))
    return s if s in CORNERS else default


def _anim(v):
    s = v.strip().lower() if isinstance(v, str) else ""
    s = "reveal" if s == "typewriter" else s
    return s if s in ANIMS or s in ("none", "auto") else "auto"


def _icon(v, default):
    if v is False:
        return ""
    return ("" if v.strip().lower() in _OFF else v.strip().lower()) if isinstance(v, str) else default


def _common(raw, out, anchor):
    """Fields both modules share: on, colour, anchor, insets, timing (start / trigger / end / hold) and animation."""
    out["on"] = _bool(raw.get("on"), False)
    out["color"] = _hex(raw.get("color"), out["color"])
    out["anchor"] = _anchor(raw.get("anchor"), anchor)
    out["inset_x"], out["inset_y"] = _flt(raw.get("inset_x"), 0, 600, 0.0), _flt(raw.get("inset_y"), 0, 600, 0.0)
    trig = raw.get("trigger")
    out["trigger"] = trig.strip() if isinstance(trig, str) else ""
    out["start"] = raw["start"] if raw.get("start") is not None else (None if out["trigger"] else 0.0)    # a phrase never falls back to 0
    out["end"], out["hold"] = raw.get("end"), raw.get("hold")
    out["anim"] = _anim(raw.get("anim"))
    out["anim_dur"] = _flt(raw.get("anim_dur"), 0.0, 5.0, 0.45)


def _style(v):
    s = str(v).strip().lower().replace("-", "_") if isinstance(v, (str, int)) and not isinstance(v, bool) else ""
    return {"tag_b": "tag_b", "b": "tag_b", "2": "tag_b", "tagb": "tag_b"}.get(s, "tag_a")


def t4_nameplate_cfg(raw):
    """Normalise cfg['t4']['nameplate'] -> complete dict (extra keys such as 'font' survive). Never raises; junk -> defaults."""
    out = dict(T4_NAMEPLATE)
    if not isinstance(raw, dict):
        return out
    try:
        out.update({k: v for k, v in raw.items() if k not in out})
        out["style"] = _style(raw.get("style"))
        for k in ("name", "role"):
            v = raw.get(k)
            if isinstance(v, str):
                out[k] = "" if v.strip().lower() in ("-", "none") else v.strip()[:60]
        out["icon"] = _icon(raw.get("icon"), T4_NAMEPLATE["icon"])
        _common(raw, out, STYLE_ANCHOR[out["style"]])
    except Exception:
        return dict(T4_NAMEPLATE)
    return out


def _rows(v):
    """[{'icon', 'text'} | ('icon', 'text') | 'text' | 'icon|text'] -> clean rows (<= 5). Nothing usable -> the three R10 rows."""
    out = []
    for i, r in enumerate(v[:MAX_ROWS] if isinstance(v, (list, tuple)) else []):
        ic, tx = None, None
        if isinstance(r, dict):
            ic, tx = r.get("icon"), r.get("text")
        elif isinstance(r, (list, tuple)) and len(r) == 2:
            ic, tx = r
        elif isinstance(r, str):
            head, _, tail = r.partition("|")
            ic, tx = (head, tail) if tail and head.strip().lower() in ICON_NAMES else (None, r)
        tx = tx.strip()[:40] if isinstance(tx, str) else ""
        if tx:
            out.append({"icon": _icon(ic, LIST_ICONS[len(out) % len(LIST_ICONS)]), "text": tx})
    return out or copy.deepcopy(DEFAULT_ROWS)


def t4_corner_list_cfg(raw):
    """Normalise cfg['t4']['corner_list'] -> complete dict. Never raises; junk -> defaults (rows: the three R10 rows)."""
    out = copy.deepcopy(T4_CORNER_LIST)
    if not isinstance(raw, dict):
        return out
    try:
        out.update({k: copy.deepcopy(v) for k, v in raw.items() if k not in out})
        out["icon"] = _icon(raw.get("icon"), "laptop")
        out["icon_anchor"] = _anchor(raw.get("icon_anchor"), "bottom-left")
        out["rows"] = _rows(raw.get("rows"))
        out["size"] = _flt(raw.get("size"), 8, 120, 22.0)
        _common(raw, out, "bottom-right")
    except Exception:
        return copy.deepcopy(T4_CORNER_LIST)
    return out


# ---- placement --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
_FM, _SPR = {}, {}


def _cached(cache, key, make, limit=12):
    if key not in cache:
        if len(cache) >= limit:
            cache.pop(next(iter(cache)))
        cache[key] = make()
    return cache[key]


def _frame_mask(W, H, fcfg, s):
    """Quarter-size 'L' mask of where the frame is solid, grown by GAP px (conservative). None when the frame is off."""
    if not fcfg["on"]:
        return None

    def make():
        a = t4_frame_plate(W, H, fcfg).getchannel("A").point(lambda v: 255 if v > FRAME_THR else 0)
        q = a.resize(((W + 3) // 4, (H + 3) // 4), Image.BOX).point(lambda v: 255 if v > 0 else 0)
        return q.filter(ImageFilter.MaxFilter(2 * int(math.ceil((GAP * s + 4) / 4.0)) + 1))
    return _cached(_FM, (W, H, round(s, 3), repr(sorted(fcfg.items()))), make, 4)


def _ink_box(spr):
    return spr.getchannel("A").point(lambda v: 255 if v > INK_THR else 0).getbbox()


def _pooled(spr, box):
    """Quarter-size ink mask of the sprite (cropped to its ink), grown by one cell so a placement that is not a multiple of 4 px stays safe."""
    a = spr.getchannel("A").crop(box).point(lambda v: 255 if v > INK_THR else 0)
    q = a.resize((max(1, (a.width + 3) // 4), max(1, (a.height + 3) // 4)), Image.BOX).point(lambda v: 255 if v > 0 else 0)
    padded = Image.new("L", (q.width + 2, q.height + 2), 0)             # 1 cell of room on every side, then grow by 1 cell
    padded.paste(q, (1, 1))
    return padded.filter(ImageFilter.MaxFilter(3))


def _hits(fm, q, x, y):
    qx, qy = int(x // 4), int(y // 4)
    return ImageChops.multiply(fm.crop((qx - 1, qy - 1, qx - 1 + q.width, qy - 1 + q.height)), q).getbbox() is not None


def _place(spr, anchor, W, H, s, inx, iny, fm, label):
    """Item centre for `spr` anchored at a corner -> (cx, cy, ink rect on the canvas, moved px) or None for an empty sprite.
    Ink at the 48 px safe margin (+ inset); with a frame mask: moved vertically (down from the top, up from the bottom) until clear."""
    box = _ink_box(spr)
    if box is None:
        return None
    w, h = box[2] - box[0], box[3] - box[1]
    m = T4_SAFE * s
    sx = 1 if anchor.endswith("left") else -1 if anchor.endswith("right") else 0          # inward direction; 0 = centred on that axis
    sy = 1 if anchor.startswith("top") else -1 if anchor.startswith("bottom") else 0     # (the 4 corners behave exactly as before)
    x = m + inx * s if sx > 0 else W - m - inx * s - w if sx < 0 else (W - w) / 2.0
    y = m + iny * s if sy > 0 else H - m - iny * s - h if sy < 0 else (H - h) / 2.0
    x, y = int(round(min(max(x, m), W - m - w))), int(round(min(max(y, m), H - m - h)))
    moved = 0
    if fm is not None:
        dx, dy = _clear_shift(fm, _pooled(spr, box), x, y, sx, sy, W, H, s)
        if dx is None:
            t4_warn("%s: no free place next to the frame in the %s corner - kept at the safe margin." % (label, anchor))
        else:
            x, y, moved = x + dx, y + dy, abs(dx) + abs(dy)
    return x - box[0] + spr.width / 2.0, y - box[1] + spr.height / 2.0, (x, y, x + w, y + h), moved


def _clear_shift(fm, q, x, y, sx, sy, W, H, s):
    """Smallest inward shift (dx toward the canvas centre horizontally first, then dy vertically) that leaves the sprite clear of the
    frame mask -> (dx, dy), or (None, None). Rails run the full side height, so a horizontal step is tried before a vertical one."""
    step = max(2, int(round(4 * s)))
    nx, ny = int(180 * s) // step, int(H * 0.45) // step
    for c in range(0, nx + ny + 1):                                     # total shift in steps: the cheapest clear place wins
        for i in range(max(0, c - ny), min(c, nx) + 1):                 # i steps inward horizontally, c - i vertically
            dx, dy = i * step, (c - i) * step
            xx, yy = x + sx * dx, y + sy * dy
            if xx < 0 or yy < 0 or xx + q.width * 4 > W + 8 or yy + q.height * 4 > H + 8:
                continue
            if not _hits(fm, q, xx, yy):
                return sx * dx, sy * dy
    return None, None


# ---- items ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
def _font(path):
    if path and os.path.isfile(str(path)):
        return str(path)
    if path:
        t4_warn("t4 nameplate: font '%s' not found - using the default bold font." % Path(str(path)).name)
    p = t2_font("arabic", "Ø¹")[0]
    return p if p and os.path.isfile(p) else default_font()


def _cues(cfg):
    out = []
    for c in caption_streams(cfg):
        try:
            out.append(parse_srt(c["srt"]))
        except Exception:
            continue
    return out


def _window(cfg, entry, label, duration, default_hold):
    return t4_time(dict(entry, label=label), _cues(cfg) if entry.get("trigger") else None, duration, default_hold)


def _item(spr, win, anchor, c, W, H, s, fm, label, placed, duration):
    pos = _place(spr, anchor, W, H, s, c["inset_x"], c["inset_y"], fm, label)
    if pos is None:
        return None
    cx, cy, rect, _ = pos
    placed.append((label, rect))
    anim = c["anim"] if c["anim"] != "auto" else ("slide_right" if anchor.endswith("left") else "slide_left")
    persistent = win[1] >= float(duration) - 0.01                       # lasts to the end of the video -> no fade-out at the very end
    return Item(spr, win[0], win[1], cx, cy, anim, c["anim_dur"], out=not persistent, rtl=bool(spr.info.get("rtl")))


def _tag_items(cfg, c, duration, W, H, s, fm, placed):
    label = "name tag '%s'" % (c["name"] or c["role"])[:30]
    win = _window(cfg, c, label, duration, 5.0)
    if win is None:
        return []
    icon = c["icon"]
    if icon and icon not in ICON_NAMES:
        t4_warn("%s: icon '%s' unknown (use one of: %s) - no icon." % (label, icon, ", ".join(ICON_NAMES)))
        icon = ""
    # widest panel: canvas - safe margin - glow tails (~30 px) on both sides; frame on: +36 px each side so the tag can still clear the rails
    fp, max_w = _font(c.get("font")), W - 2 * (T4_SAFE + 30 + (36 if fm is not None else 0)) * s
    spr = _cached(_SPR, ("tag", c["style"], c["name"], c["role"], icon, c["color"], fp, W, H),
                  lambda: t4_tag_sprite(c["style"], c["name"], c["role"], icon, c["color"], fp, s, max_w))
    if spr is None:
        t4_warn("%s: name and role are both empty - skipped." % label)
        return []
    it = _item(spr, win, c["anchor"], c, W, H, s, fm, label, placed, duration)
    return [it] if it else []


def _list_items(cfg, c, duration, W, H, s, fm, placed):
    win = _window(cfg, c, "corner list", duration, None)
    if win is None:
        return []
    out, fp = [], _font(c.get("font"))
    if c["icon"]:
        lap = _cached(_SPR, ("lap", c["icon"], c["color"], W, H), lambda: t4_laptop_sprite(c["icon"], T4_LIST["laptop_w"], c["color"], s))
        out.append(_item(lap, win, c["icon_anchor"], c, W, H, s, fm, "corner list icon", placed, duration))
    key = ("list", repr(c["rows"]), c["size"], c["color"], fp, W, H)
    lst = _cached(_SPR, key, lambda: t4_list_sprite(c["rows"], c["size"], c["color"], fp, s, (W - 2 * T4_SAFE * s) * 0.6))
    out.append(_item(lst, win, c["anchor"], c, W, H, s, fm, "corner list", placed, duration))
    return [i for i in out if i]


def _warn_overlaps(placed):
    for i, (a, ra) in enumerate(placed):
        for b, rb in placed[i + 1:]:
            if ra[0] < rb[2] and rb[0] < ra[2] and ra[1] < rb[3] and rb[1] < ra[3]:
                t4_warn("%s and %s overlap - give one of them another corner (anchor) or an inset." % (a, b))


def t4_nameplate_items(cfg, duration):
    """Items of the name tag and the corner list (each one sprite, timed + animated); [] when both are off. Never raises."""
    t4 = cfg.get("t4") if isinstance(cfg, dict) and isinstance(cfg.get("t4"), dict) else {}
    tag, lst = t4_nameplate_cfg(t4.get("nameplate")), t4_corner_list_cfg(t4.get("corner_list"))
    if not (tag["on"] or lst["on"]):
        return []
    try:
        W, H = int(cfg["canvas"][0]), int(cfg["canvas"][1])
    except (KeyError, TypeError, ValueError, IndexError):
        t4_warn("t4 nameplate: the job has no canvas - skipped.")
        return []
    s = min(W, H) / 1080.0
    placed, items = [], []
    try:
        fm = _frame_mask(W, H, t4_frame_cfg(t4.get("frame")), s)
        if tag["on"]:
            items += _tag_items(cfg, tag, duration, W, H, s, fm, placed)
        if lst["on"]:
            items += _list_items(cfg, lst, duration, W, H, s, fm, placed)
        _warn_overlaps(placed)
    except Exception as e:
        t4_warn("t4 nameplate: could not be drawn (%s: %s) - skipped." % (type(e).__name__, e))
    return items


# ---- questions --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
def _ask_corner(prompt, default, key, allow_none=False):
    """Corner question -> 'top-left' | ... (or '' for 'none' when allowed)."""
    labels = (["None (hide it)"] if allow_none else []) + ["Top-left", "Top-right", "Bottom-left", "Bottom-right"]
    n = ask_choice(prompt, labels, default=CORNERS.index(default) + 1 + (1 if allow_none else 0), key=key) - (1 if allow_none else 0)
    return CORNERS[n - 1] if n >= 1 else ""


def _ask_motion(prompt, corner, key, whole_video):
    """Timing + animation questions -> dict(start|trigger, hold?, anim, anim_dur). whole_video: offer 'keep it for the whole video' first."""
    if whole_video and yn("Keep the %s on screen for the whole video?" % prompt, default=True):
        out = {"start": 0.0}
    else:
        out = t4_ask_when(prompt.capitalize(), 5.0, key=key + "_when")
    out["anim"] = ask_anim("%s animation" % prompt.capitalize(), default="slide_right" if (corner or "left").endswith("left") else "slide_left",
                           key=key + "_anim")
    out["anim_dur"] = ask("Animation duration (seconds)", default=0.45, key=key + "_adur", cast=c_float)
    return out


def t4_ask_nameplate():
    """Question group 'nameplate' (called by t4_ask_config). -> the dict for cfg['t4']['nameplate']."""
    if not yn("Add a name tag (name + role - references R2 / R10)?", default=False):
        return {"on": False}
    st = ask_choice("Tag style:", ["tag_a - hexagon-cut glass panel, code icon | name + role, stripes in the corner (R2)",
                                   "tag_b - chamfered panel, capitals, bright bar + stripes on the bottom edge (R10)"],
                    default=1, key="t4_np_style")
    style = TAG_STYLES[st - 1]
    d = T4_NAMEPLATE
    clean = lambda s: "" if s.strip() in ("-", "none") else s.strip()
    name = ask("Name (Arabic works too)", default=d["name"], key="t4_np_name", cast=clean)
    role = ask("Role / subtitle ('-' = none)", default=d["role"], key="t4_np_role", cast=clean)
    ic = ask_choice("Icon in the tag:", ["None"] + list(ICON_NAMES), default=2, key="t4_np_icon")
    color = ask("Tag colour (hex or name)", default=d["color"], key="t4_np_color", cast=c_color)
    anchor = _ask_corner("Corner:", STYLE_ANCHOR[style], "t4_np_corner_" + style)
    out = {"on": True, "style": style, "name": name, "role": role, "icon": "" if ic == 1 else ICON_NAMES[ic - 2], "color": color, "anchor": anchor}
    out.update(_ask_motion("name tag", anchor, "t4_np", whole_video=False))
    return out


def t4_ask_corner_list():
    """Question group 'corner_list' (called by t4_ask_config). -> the dict for cfg['t4']['corner_list']."""
    if not yn("Add the corner list (laptop icon + service rows - reference R10)?", default=False):
        return {"on": False}
    d = T4_CORNER_LIST
    rows = []
    for i, r in enumerate(DEFAULT_ROWS, 1):
        v = ask("Row %d: text, or icon|text (icons: %s; '-' = no row)" % (i, ", ".join(ICON_NAMES)), default="%s|%s" % (r["icon"], r["text"]),
                key="t4_cl_row%d" % i, cast=lambda s: s.strip())
        if v and v != "-":
            rows.append(v)
    laptop = _ask_corner("Laptop icon corner:", d["icon_anchor"], "t4_cl_icon_corner", allow_none=True)
    anchor = _ask_corner("List corner:", d["anchor"], "t4_cl_corner")
    color = ask("Colour (hex or name)", default=d["color"], key="t4_cl_color", cast=c_color)
    out = {"on": True, "rows": rows, "icon": "laptop" if laptop else "", "icon_anchor": laptop or d["icon_anchor"], "anchor": anchor, "color": color}
    out.update(_ask_motion("corner list", anchor, "t4_cl", whole_video=True))
    return out


====================================================================================================
FILE: mt\templates\t4\nameplate_list.py
====================================================================================================
"""mt.templates.t4.nameplate_list - Template 4: the two sprites of the 'corner_list' module (ref R10, bottom corners).
  laptop  a thin neon laptop (screen outline + keyboard deck) with a glowing '</>' on the screen. Any other kit icon name gives
          that icon as a plain neon icon instead.
  list    up to 5 rows: neon icon + small white text (soft dark shadow so it reads on a bright video). R10 has 3 rows.
Public: LIST_ICONS, T4_LIST, t4_laptop_sprite(icon, width, color, scale), t4_list_sprite(rows, size, color, font, scale, max_w).
Authored in px at short side 1080 (scale = short side / 1080); each sprite has a ~14-26 px glow margin that the placement code
ignores (it measures the visible ink). Arabic / RTL rows are mirrored: icon on the right, text right-aligned."""
import os

from PIL import Image, ImageDraw, ImageFilter

from mt.draw import t2_paint, t2_poly_mask, t2_rgb
from mt.fonts import default_font, is_rtl_text, load_font, shape
from mt.templates.t4.kit import connector, kit_paste, neon_icon

LIST_ICONS = ("globe", "phone", "gear", "database", "cloud")           # row icon by position when a row names none
T4_LIST = {"size": 22, "laptop_w": 172, "pitch": 1.75, "icon": 1.38, "gap": 0.72, "pad": 14}   # pitch / icon / gap in font sizes


def _closed(pts):
    return pts + [pts[0]]


def t4_laptop_sprite(icon="laptop", width=172, color="#19D3FF", scale=1.0):
    """Neon laptop of the given width (px at short side 1080) with '</>' on its screen. icon != 'laptop' -> that kit icon, neon, as is."""
    s = max(0.2, float(scale))
    W, col = max(24, int(round(float(width) * s))), t2_rgb(color)
    if icon != "laptop":
        return neon_icon(icon or "code", max(16, int(W * 0.7)), col)
    g, Hh = int(round(26 * s)), int(round(W * 0.68))
    spr = Image.new("RGBA", (W + 2 * g, Hh + 2 * g), (0, 0, 0, 0))

    def P(pts):
        return [(g + x * W, g + y * Hh) for x, y in pts]
    c = 0.035
    screen = [(0.10 + c, 0.0), (0.90 - c, 0.0), (0.90, 0.045), (0.90, 0.66), (0.10, 0.66), (0.10, 0.045)]
    t2_paint(spr, t2_poly_mask(P(screen)), (4, 12, 30), 0.60)                                   # dark glass behind the glyph
    deck = [(0.13, 0.72), (0.87, 0.72), (1.0, 1.0), (0.0, 1.0)]
    for pts, w in ((_closed(screen), 3.4), (_closed(deck), 3.0), ([(0.22, 0.80), (0.78, 0.80)], 1.8), ([(0.17, 0.88), (0.83, 0.88)], 1.8),
                   ([(0.42, 0.945), (0.58, 0.945)], 2.4)):
        kit_paste(spr, connector(P(pts), col, False, w * s, 9 * s), 0, 0)
    isz = int(round(W * 0.34))
    kit_paste(spr, neon_icon("code", isz, col, 6 * s), g + W * 0.5 - isz / 2.0, g + Hh * 0.33 - isz / 2.0)
    return spr


def _fit_rows(rows, fp, size, avail):
    """Shrink the text size (down to 60 %) then cut the longest row with '...' until icon + gap + text fit `avail`. -> (font, shaped, widths, size)."""
    texts, k = [r["text"] for r in rows], 1.0
    for _ in range(120):
        f = load_font(fp, max(8, int(round(size * k))))
        shaped = [shape(t) for t in texts]
        ws = [f.getlength(t) for t in shaped]
        room = avail - (size * k * (T4_LIST["icon"] + T4_LIST["gap"]))
        if max(ws) <= room or max(len(t) for t in texts) <= 2:
            break
        if k > 0.6:
            k -= 0.05
        else:
            i = ws.index(max(ws))
            texts[i] = texts[i][:-2].rstrip() + "â€¦"
    return f, shaped, ws, size * k


def t4_list_sprite(rows, size=22, color="#19D3FF", font=None, scale=1.0, max_w=None):
    """rows = [{'icon': kit icon name | '', 'text': str}] -> RGBA sprite, or None for no rows. max_w = widest allowed block (px).
    sprite.info: rows (int), rtl (any row is RTL). Never raises on odd values."""
    rows = [r for r in (rows or []) if isinstance(r, dict) and str(r.get("text", "")).strip()]
    if not rows:
        return None
    s = max(0.2, float(scale))
    fp = font if font and os.path.isfile(str(font)) else default_font()
    col = t2_rgb(color)
    f, shaped, ws, sz = _fit_rows(rows, fp, float(size) * s, float(max_w) if max_w else 10.0 ** 6)
    isz, gap, pitch, pad = int(round(sz * T4_LIST["icon"])), sz * T4_LIST["gap"], sz * T4_LIST["pitch"], int(round(T4_LIST["pad"] * s))
    bw = int(max(w + isz + gap for w in ws))
    spr = Image.new("RGBA", (bw + 2 * pad, int(pitch * (len(rows) - 1) + isz) + 2 * pad), (0, 0, 0, 0))
    ink = Image.new("L", spr.size, 0)
    d = ImageDraw.Draw(ink)
    asc, desc = f.getmetrics()
    anyrtl = False
    for i, (r, t, w) in enumerate(zip(rows, shaped, ws)):
        cy = pad + isz / 2.0 + i * pitch
        rtl = is_rtl_text(str(r["text"]))
        anyrtl = anyrtl or rtl
        if r.get("icon"):
            ic = neon_icon(r["icon"], isz, col, max(3.0, isz * 0.16))
            kit_paste(spr, ic, pad + (bw - isz if rtl else 0), cy - isz / 2.0)
        x = pad + (bw - isz - gap if rtl else isz + gap)
        d.text((x, cy + (asc - desc) / 2.0), t, font=f, fill=255, anchor="rs" if rtl else "ls")
    t2_paint(spr, (ink.filter(ImageFilter.GaussianBlur(max(1.5, sz * 0.11))).point(lambda v: min(255, int(v * 1.8))), (0, 0)), (0, 0, 0), 0.7)
    t2_paint(spr, (ink, (0, 0)), (255, 255, 255))
    spr.info["rows"], spr.info["rtl"] = len(rows), anyrtl
    return spr


====================================================================================================
FILE: mt\templates\t4\nameplate_tags.py
====================================================================================================
"""mt.templates.t4.nameplate_tags - Template 4: the two name-tag sprites of the 'nameplate' module.
  tag_a  ref R2:  dark glass panel with hexagon-cut ends (big cuts on the left), neon border, '</>' icon | divider | name + role,
                  three slanted stripes inside the bottom-right corner.
  tag_b  ref R10: chamfered panel with a faint second outline, icon | divider | NAME (white) + ROLE (neon colour) in capitals, a bright
                  bar on the bottom edge and five slanted stripes hanging below it.
Public: TAG_STYLES, T4_TAG, t4_tag_sprite(style, name, role, icon, color, font, scale, max_w) -> RGBA sprite | None.
Authored in px at short side 1080 (scale = short side / 1080). The sprite carries its own glow margin (about 30 px); the placement
code (nameplate.py) measures the visible ink, so the margin never counts against the 48 px safe area. Arabic / RTL text is shaped with
mt.fonts.shape and right-aligned in the text area; Latin text is left-aligned."""
import os
from pathlib import Path

from PIL import Image, ImageDraw

from mt.draw import t2_lighten, t2_rgb
from mt.fonts import default_font, is_rtl_text, load_font, shape
from mt.templates.t4.kit import chamfer_rect, connector, glass_panel, hazard_stripes, kit_paste, neon_icon, neon_stroke

TAG_STYLES = ("tag_a", "tag_b")
T4_TAG = {                                    # px at short side 1080; cut = chamfers (top-left, top-right, bottom-right, bottom-left)
    "tag_a": {"h": 124, "min_w": 440, "icon_w": 112, "icon": 58, "name": 38, "role": 26, "right": 104, "cut": (26, 16, 12, 26), "caps": False},
    "tag_b": {"h": 112, "min_w": 520, "icon_w": 108, "icon": 54, "name": 34, "role": 24, "right": 44, "cut": (24, 24, 24, 16), "caps": True},
}
GLOW_PAD = 30                                 # transparent margin around the panel (glow room), px at short side 1080
TINT = (5, 12, 30, 214)                       # panel fill (rgba): darker than the kit's default glass, as R2 / R10


def _is_bold(path):
    n = Path(str(path)).stem.lower().replace("-", "").replace(" ", "")
    return any(k in n for k in ("bold", "black", "heavy", "extrabold", "semibold"))


def _fade(sp, a):
    sp.putalpha(sp.getchannel("A").point(lambda v: int(v * a)))
    return sp


def _fit(lines, fp, sizes, avail):
    """Shrink both font sizes together (down to 55 %), then cut the widest line with '...' until it fits `avail` px.
    -> (fonts, shaped lines, widest width). An empty line is allowed (width 0)."""
    texts, k = [str(t) for t in lines], 1.0
    for _ in range(120):
        fonts = [load_font(fp, max(8, int(round(sz * k)))) for sz in sizes]
        shaped = [shape(t) for t in texts]
        ws = [f.getlength(t) if t else 0.0 for f, t in zip(fonts, shaped)]
        if max(ws) <= avail or max(len(t) for t in texts) <= 2:
            break
        if k > 0.55:
            k -= 0.05
        else:
            i = ws.index(max(ws))
            texts[i] = texts[i][:-2].rstrip() + "â€¦"
    return fonts, shaped, max(ws)


# ---- parts ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
def _panel(spr, style, w, H, g, col, s):
    """Glass panel + the style's extras (tag_a: stripes inside the corner; tag_b: outer outline, bottom bar, hanging stripes)."""
    sp = T4_TAG[style]
    cut = tuple(c * s for c in sp["cut"])
    if style == "tag_b":                                                # faint second outline, 8 px outside (R10's double border)
        d = 8 * s
        outer = neon_stroke(chamfer_rect((w + 2 * d, H + 2 * d), tuple(c + d for c in cut)), col, 1.4 * s, 5 * s)
        kit_paste(spr, _fade(outer, 0.45), g - d, g - d)
        ln = connector([(g + 44 * s, g + H + 9 * s), (g + w * 0.50, g + H + 9 * s)], col, False, 1.5 * s, 5 * s)
        kit_paste(spr, _fade(ln, 0.55), 0, 0)
    kit_paste(spr, glass_panel((w, H), 0, TINT, col, cut, 2.6 * s, 11 * s), g, g)
    if style == "tag_a":
        sw, sh = int(round(62 * s)), int(round(24 * s))
        kit_paste(spr, hazard_stripes((sw, sh), 35.0, col, 5, 10 * s, 6 * s), g + w - 24 * s - sw, g + H - 15 * s - sh)
    else:
        bar = connector([(g + w * 0.24, g + H), (g + w * 0.36, g + H)], t2_lighten(col, 0.30), False, 5 * s, 10 * s)
        kit_paste(spr, bar, 0, 0)
        sw, sh = int(round(116 * s)), int(round(18 * s))
        kit_paste(spr, hazard_stripes((sw, sh), 35.0, col, 5, 12 * s, 7 * s), g + w * 0.53, g + H - 4 * s)


def _emblem(spr, icon, left, H, g, col, s):
    """Neon icon centred in the left block + the thin vertical divider on its right edge. No icon -> nothing."""
    if not icon:
        return
    isz = int(round(T4_TAG["tag_a"]["icon"] * s))
    ic = neon_icon(icon, isz, col, 8 * s)
    kit_paste(spr, ic, g + left / 2.0 - isz / 2.0, g + H / 2.0 - isz / 2.0)
    x = g + left
    kit_paste(spr, connector([(x, g + H * 0.24), (x, g + H * 0.76)], t2_lighten(col, 0.15), False, 2.0 * s, 5 * s), 0, 0)


def _texts(spr, fonts, shaped, x0, x1, ymid, rtl, bold, fills, s):
    """Lines stacked and centred on ymid. LTR: left edge x0; RTL: right edge x1. Non-bold fonts get a thin same-colour stroke."""
    d = ImageDraw.Draw(spr)
    gap = 7 * s
    lines = [(f, t, c) for f, t, c in zip(fonts, shaped, fills) if t]
    total = sum(f.getmetrics()[0] + f.getmetrics()[1] for f, _, _ in lines) + gap * (len(lines) - 1)
    y = ymid - total / 2.0
    for i, (f, t, c) in enumerate(lines):
        asc, desc = f.getmetrics()
        sw = 1 if (i == 0 and not bold and f.size >= 24) else 0
        d.text((x1 if rtl else x0, y + asc), t, font=f, fill=c + (255,), anchor="rs" if rtl else "ls", stroke_width=sw, stroke_fill=c + (255,))
        y += asc + desc + gap


# ---- public -----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
def t4_tag_sprite(style, name, role="", icon="code", color="#19D3FF", font=None, scale=1.0, max_w=None):
    """RGBA sprite of a name tag, or None when name and role are both empty. Unknown style -> 'tag_a'. max_w = widest allowed PANEL (px):
    text shrinks, then is cut with '...'. sprite.info: panel (w, h in px), rtl. Never raises on odd text / colour / font values."""
    style = style if style in T4_TAG else "tag_a"
    sp = T4_TAG[style]
    name, role = str(name or "").strip(), str(role or "").strip()
    if not name and not role:
        return None
    if sp["caps"]:
        name, role = name.upper(), role.upper()
    s = max(0.2, float(scale))
    fp = font if font and os.path.isfile(str(font)) else default_font()
    col = t2_rgb(color)
    H, g = int(round(sp["h"] * s)), int(round(GLOW_PAD * s))
    left, right = int(round((sp["icon_w"] if icon else 34) * s)), int(round(sp["right"] * s))
    gapx = int(round(26 * s)) if icon else 0                              # divider -> text
    avail = (float(max_w) - left - gapx - right) if max_w else 10.0 ** 6
    fonts, shaped, tw = _fit([name, role], fp, (sp["name"] * s, sp["role"] * s), max(40.0, avail))
    w = int(max(sp["min_w"] * s, left + gapx + tw + right))
    if max_w:
        w = min(w, int(max_w))
    ext = int(round(14 * s)) if style == "tag_b" else 0
    spr = Image.new("RGBA", (w + 2 * g, H + ext + 2 * g), (0, 0, 0, 0))
    _panel(spr, style, w, H, g, col, s)
    _emblem(spr, icon, left, H, g, col, s)
    rtl = is_rtl_text(name + " " + role)
    role_col = t2_lighten(col, 0.15) if style == "tag_b" else (226, 234, 246)
    area0, area1 = g + left + gapx, g + w - right
    _texts(spr, fonts, shaped, area0, area1, g + H / 2.0, rtl, _is_bold(fp), [(255, 255, 255), role_col], s)
    spr.info["panel"], spr.info["rtl"] = (w, H), rtl
    return spr

"""mt.ask - moved from montage.py by tools/mt_move.py"""
from mt.fonts import default_font, find_font, font_supports_arabic, list_local_fonts
from mt.common import AR_DIGITS, D, ask
from mt.anim import ANIMS
import os
from pathlib import Path
from mt.anim import PRESETS
from mt.common import AR_DIGITS, D, IMAGE_EXTS, TEMPLATES_DIR, clean_path, probe, VIDEO_EXTS, ask, ask_choice, ask_time, c_color, c_existing_file, c_float, c_int, c_percent, c_text, yn
from mt.common import TEMPLATE_REGISTRY
import re
import json


def ask_font(prompt, key):
    local = list_local_fonts()
    if local:
        print("   Fonts in the 'fonts' folder:")
        for i, f in enumerate(local, 1):
            print(f"     {i}) {Path(f).name}")
    print("   (type a number, a font name like Arial / Cairo, or a full path to a .ttf/.otf file)")
    default = D.get(key) if D.get(key) and os.path.isfile(D.get(key)) else default_font()

    def _c(s):
        if s.isdigit() and local and 1 <= int(s) <= len(local):
            return local[int(s) - 1]
        p = find_font(s)
        if not p:
            raise ValueError("font not found. Put the .ttf in the 'fonts' folder next to the script or give the full path")
        return p

    path = ask(prompt, default=default, cast=_c)
    D[key] = path
    if not font_supports_arabic(path):
        print("   [!] This font does not seem to support Arabic (fine for English-only text).")
    return path


ANIM_LINE = ("0=none  1=fade  2=slide_up  3=slide_down  4=slide_left  5=slide_right  "
             "6=zoom_in  7=pop  8=spin_in  9=reveal(typewriter)  10=drop")


def c_anim(s):
    s = s.strip().lower().translate(AR_DIGITS)
    if s.isdigit():
        v = int(s)
        if v == 0:
            return "none"
        if 1 <= v <= len(ANIMS):
            return ANIMS[v - 1]
        raise ValueError("choose 0-10")
    if s in ("none", "reveal", "typewriter") or s in ANIMS:
        return "reveal" if s == "typewriter" else s
    raise ValueError("unknown animation")


def ask_anim(prompt, default="fade", key=None):
    print("   " + ANIM_LINE)
    return ask(prompt, default=default, key=key, cast=c_anim)


POS_LINE = ("1=top-left 2=top 3=top-right 4=left 5=center 6=right 7=bottom-left 8=bottom 9=bottom-right"
            "  or  x,y  (center point, px or %, e.g. 540,300 or 50%,15%)")


def c_pos(s):
    s = s.strip().lower().translate(AR_DIGITS).replace("_", "-").replace(" ", "")
    if s in PRESETS:
        return PRESETS[s]
    if s in PRESETS.values() or s in ("middle", "centre"):
        return "center" if s in ("middle", "centre") else s
    m = re.fullmatch(r"(-?[\d.]+%?)[,;x](-?[\d.]+%?)", s)
    if m:
        return [m.group(1), m.group(2)]
    raise ValueError("use 1-9, a preset name, or x,y")


def ask_pos(prompt, default="center", key=None):
    print("   " + POS_LINE)
    return ask(prompt, default=default if not isinstance(default, list) else ",".join(default),
               key=key, cast=c_pos)


def list_templates():
    if not TEMPLATES_DIR.is_dir():
        return []
    return sorted(TEMPLATES_DIR.glob("*.json"))


def ask_layers(W, H):
    layers = []
    print("\nTemplate layers = PNG/ICO decorations that stay on screen for the whole video")
    print("(frames, borders, logo, side decorations...).")
    while yn("Add a template layer?", default=False):
        L = {}
        L["path"] = ask("  Image path (PNG/ICO)", cast=c_existing_file(IMAGE_EXTS))
        L["pos"] = ask_pos("  Position", default="center")
        L["width"] = ask("  Width: px or % of canvas (Enter = original size)", allow_empty=True)
        L["opacity"] = ask("  Opacity %", default=100, cast=c_percent)
        L["anim"] = ask_anim("  Entrance animation", default="none")
        L["anim_dur"] = 0.6
        layers.append(L)
    return layers


def maybe_save_template(tpl):
    name = ask("Save this as a template? Type a name (Enter = don't save)", allow_empty=True)
    if name:
        TEMPLATES_DIR.mkdir(exist_ok=True)
        safe = re.sub(r"[^\w\- ]+", "", name, flags=re.UNICODE).strip() or "template"
        (TEMPLATES_DIR / f"{safe}.json").write_text(json.dumps(tpl, ensure_ascii=False, indent=1),
                                                    encoding="utf-8")
        print(f"   Saved templates/{safe}.json")
    return tpl


_PRESET = {}     # {"path": saved template chosen in the selector, "sel": True while Template 2's own flow runs}


def _saved_templates():
    """[(path, registry_id)] for every templates/*.json: Template 2 / 3 designs -> 2 / 3, everything else -> 4 (Custom)."""
    out = []
    for p in list_templates():
        out.append((p, _tpl_template_id(p) if _tpl_template_id(p) in (2, 3) else 4))
    return out


def ask_texts(duration):
    texts = []
    if not yn("\nDo you want text on the video?", default=False):
        return texts
    while True:
        print(f"\n--- Text #{len(texts) + 1} ---")
        t = {}
        t["text"] = ask("Text  (use \\n for a new line, or @file.txt to read it from a file)", cast=c_text)
        t["start"] = ask_time("Appears at", default=0)
        t["end"] = ask_time("Disappears at", default=round(min(duration, t["start"] + 3), 2))
        t["pos"] = ask_pos("Position", default="top", key="txt_pos")
        t["font"] = ask_font("Font", "txt_font")
        t["size"] = ask("Font size (px)", default=90, key="txt_size", cast=c_int)
        t["color"] = ask("Text color", default="white", key="txt_color", cast=c_color)
        t["stroke_color"] = ask("Outline color", default="black", key="txt_stroke", cast=c_color)
        t["stroke_w"] = ask("Outline width (px, 0 = none)", default=0, key="txt_strokew", cast=c_int)
        t["anim"] = ask_anim("Animation", default="fade", key="txt_anim")
        t["anim_dur"] = ask("Animation duration (seconds)", default=0.5, key="txt_adur", cast=c_float)
        texts.append(t)
        if not yn("Add another text?", default=False):
            break
    return texts


def ask_elements(duration):
    els = []
    if not yn("\nDo you want elements (PNG / ICO images) on the video?", default=False):
        return els
    while True:
        print(f"\n--- Element #{len(els) + 1} ---")
        e = {}
        e["path"] = ask("Image path (PNG/ICO/...)", cast=c_existing_file(IMAGE_EXTS))
        e["start"] = ask_time("Appears at", default=0)
        e["end"] = ask_time("Disappears at", default=round(min(duration, e["start"] + 3), 2))
        e["pos"] = ask_pos("Position", default="center")
        e["width"] = ask("Width: px or % of canvas (Enter = original size)", allow_empty=True)
        e["opacity"] = ask("Opacity %", default=100, cast=c_percent)
        e["anim"] = ask_anim("Animation", default="pop", key="el_anim")
        e["anim_dur"] = ask("Animation duration (seconds)", default=0.5, key="el_adur", cast=c_float)
        els.append(e)
        if not yn("Add another element?", default=False):
            break
    return els


def ask_video_layers(duration):
    vls = []
    if not yn("\nDo you want a video layer (screen recording / b-roll) shown at a certain time?", default=False):
        return vls
    while True:
        print(f"\n--- Video layer #{len(vls) + 1} ---")
        v = {}
        v["path"] = ask("Video path", cast=c_existing_file(VIDEO_EXTS))
        v["start"] = ask_time("Appears at", default=0)
        v["end"] = ask_time("Disappears at", default=round(min(duration, v["start"] + 5), 2))
        a = ask_choice("Where?", ["Over the video box", "Over the whole canvas"], default=1)
        v["area"] = "box" if a == 1 else "canvas"
        m = ask_choice("Fit:", ["fill (crop to cover)", "fit (whole video visible)"], default=1)
        v["mode"] = "fill" if m == 1 else "fit"
        v["fade"] = 0.3
        vls.append(v)
        if not yn("Add another video layer?", default=False):
            break
    return vls


def _tpl_template_id(path):
    try:
        return int(json.loads(Path(path).read_text(encoding="utf-8")).get("template_id") or 0)
    except Exception:
        return 0


# ---- generic question steps of the main flow (extracted verbatim from montage._interactive_core) ----
def ask_video_path():
    """Video path + probe summary line. Returns (path, info)."""
    video = ask("Video path", cast=c_existing_file(VIDEO_EXTS))
    info = probe(video)
    print(f"   -> {info['w']}x{info['h']}, {float(info['fps']):.2f} fps, {info['duration']:.1f} s"
          f"{'' if info['audio'] else ', no audio'}")
    return video, info


def ask_srt_paths(parse_srt):
    """One or more SRT paths (parse_srt is passed in: mt.captions imports mt.ask, so ask cannot import it)."""
    srt_paths = []
    nxt = ask("SRT captions file path (Enter = no captions)", cast=c_existing_file((".srt",)),
              allow_empty=True)
    while nxt:
        cues = parse_srt(nxt)
        if not cues:
            print("   [!] No captions could be read from that SRT file - skipped.")
        else:
            print(f"   -> {len(cues)} caption blocks read.")
            srt_paths.append(nxt)
        nxt = ask("Another SRT (for example the English translation)? Path, or Enter = no",
                  cast=c_existing_file((".srt",)), allow_empty=True)
    return srt_paths


def ask_output_options(cfg):
    """Output file + quick-preview seconds; fills cfg['output'] and cfg['preview']."""
    print("\n--- Output ---")
    stem = Path(cfg["video"]).with_suffix("")
    cfg["output"] = ask("Output file", default=str(stem) + "_edited.mp4",
                        cast=lambda s: clean_path(s if s.lower().endswith(".mp4") else s + ".mp4"))
    cfg["preview"] = ask("Quick test: render only the first N seconds (Enter = full video)",
                         allow_empty=True, cast=c_float)
    return cfg

"""mt.render - moved from montage.py by tools/mt_move.py"""
from mt.common import get_template
from mt.draw import Item, _img_ok, draw_item, load_image_sprite, make_text_sprite, resolve_pos
from mt.fonts import _font_ok, _font_or_fallback, default_font, is_rtl_text
from mt.common import FFMPEG, _WARNED, _hex0x, _require_ffmpeg, _vid_ok, _warn, probe
from mt.captions import caption_streams, make_rich_sprite, parse_srt, stream_groups, t2_caption_items
from mt.split import SPLIT_T, compute_splits, split_at, split_boxes
from mt.anim import anim_state
import os
from pathlib import Path
import json
from PIL import Image
import subprocess
import tempfile
import shutil
import math
import time


def preflight_cfg(cfg, need_video=True):
    """Deep copy of cfg with every missing / unreadable asset dropped or replaced (one-line [!] message each)."""
    import copy
    _WARNED.clear()                                   # each render reports its own problems once
    cfg = copy.deepcopy(cfg)
    v = cfg.get("video")
    if need_video and (not v or not os.path.isfile(str(v))):
        raise ValueError(f"video file not found: {v}")
    cfg["layers"] = [L for L in cfg.get("layers", []) if _img_ok(L.get("path"), "template layer")]
    cfg["elements"] = [e for e in cfg.get("elements", []) if _img_ok(e.get("path"), "element")]
    cfg["video_layers"] = [x for x in cfg.get("video_layers", []) if _vid_ok(x.get("path"), "video layer")]
    for t in cfg.get("texts", []):
        t["font"] = _font_or_fallback(t.get("font"), "text")
    caps = []
    for c in cfg.get("captions", []):
        if not (c.get("srt") and os.path.isfile(str(c["srt"]))):
            _warn(f"SRT file '{Path(str(c.get('srt'))).name}' not found - those captions are skipped")
            continue
        c["font"] = _font_or_fallback(c.get("font"), "captions") if c.get("font") else default_font()
        caps.append(c)
    cfg["captions"] = caps
    bg = cfg.get("background") or {}
    if bg.get("type") in ("image", "video") and not os.path.isfile(str(bg.get("path"))):
        _warn(f"background {bg.get('type')} '{bg.get('path')}' not found - using a solid dark background")
        cfg["background"] = {"type": "color", "value": "#101820"}
    for c in cfg.get("cards", []) or []:
        if c.get("icon_path") and not _img_ok(c["icon_path"], "card icon"):
            c["icon"], c["icon_path"] = "none", ""
        if c.get("font") and not _font_ok(c["font"]):
            c["font"] = _font_or_fallback(c["font"], "card")
    if get_template(cfg)["id"] == 2:
        t2 = cfg.setdefault("t2", {})
        try:
            import numpy  # noqa: F401
        except ImportError:
            _warn("numpy is not installed - Template 2 uses a slower pure-Python audio waveform (optional: pip install numpy)")
        fonts = t2.get("fonts") or {}
        for role in list(fonts):
            if not _font_ok(fonts[role]):
                _warn(f"Template 2 font override '{Path(str(fonts[role])).name}' not found - put it in the fonts/ folder "
                      f"(using the automatic choice)")
                fonts.pop(role)
        cards = []
        for c in t2.get("cards", []) or []:
            if not _vid_ok(c.get("media"), "floating card media"):
                continue
            if c.get("icon_path") and not _img_ok(c["icon_path"], "card icon"):
                c["icon"], c["icon_path"] = "none", ""
            cards.append(c)
        t2["cards"] = cards
        for st in t2.get("about", []) or []:
            for r in st.get("rows", []) or []:
                if r.get("icon_path") and not _img_ok(r["icon_path"], "about icon"):
                    r["icon"], r["icon_path"] = "none", ""
    if get_template(cfg)["id"] == 3:                  # Template 3: font overrides that are not files -> one message, automatic choice
        fonts = (cfg.get("t3") or {}).get("fonts") or {}
        for role in list(fonts):
            if not _font_ok(fonts[role]):
                _warn(f"Template 3 font override '{Path(str(fonts[role])).name}' not found - put it in the fonts/ folder "
                      f"(using the automatic choice)")
                fonts.pop(role)
    return cfg


def _load_job(path):
    try:
        job = json.loads(Path(path).read_text(encoding="utf-8-sig"))
        if (isinstance(job, dict) and job.get("template_id") == 3 and "t3" not in job
                and (job.get("background") or {}).get("type") != "t3"):
            job["template_id"] = 4      # jobs saved before S4 used id 3 for Custom; real Template 3 jobs carry cfg["t3"]
        return job
    except FileNotFoundError:
        raise ValueError(f"job file not found: {path}")
    except ValueError as e:
        raise ValueError(f"job file '{Path(path).name}' is not valid JSON ({e})")


def build_items(cfg, duration):
    W, H = cfg["canvas"]
    box = cfg["video_box"]
    items = []
    T = SPLIT_T
    splits = compute_splits(cfg, duration)
    lbox, rbox = split_boxes(cfg) if splits else (None, None)
    
    for L in cfg.get("layers", []):
        spr = load_image_sprite(L["path"], L.get("width"), W)
        cx, cy = resolve_pos(L["pos"], spr.width, spr.height, W, H)
        items.append(Item(spr, 0.0, duration + 1, cx, cy, L.get("anim", "none"), L.get("anim_dur", 0.6),
                          out=False, opacity=L.get("opacity", 100) / 100.0))

    for e in cfg.get("elements", []):
        if get_template(cfg)["id"] == 3:                  # T3: style "card" (default) sits inside the real frame
            from mt.templates.t3.layout import t3_element_items      # (lazy: render.py never imports templates at top level)
            t3_items = t3_element_items(cfg, e, duration)            # None for style "plain" -> old path below
            if t3_items is not None:
                items += t3_items
                continue
        spr = load_image_sprite(e["path"], None if rbox else e.get("width"), W)
        if rbox:
            r = min(rbox["w"] / spr.width, rbox["h"] / spr.height)
            spr = spr.resize((max(2, int(spr.width * r)), max(2, int(spr.height * r))), Image.LANCZOS)
            cx, cy = rbox["x"] + rbox["w"] / 2.0, rbox["y"] + rbox["h"] / 2.0
        else:
            cx, cy = resolve_pos(e["pos"], spr.width, spr.height, W, H)
        items.append(Item(spr, e["start"], min(e["end"], duration + 1), cx, cy,
                          e.get("anim", "fade"), e.get("anim_dur", 0.5), True,
                          e.get("opacity", 100) / 100.0))

    if get_template(cfg)["id"] == 3 and cfg.get("_t2_still") and cfg.get("video_layers"):      # T3 still: side video layers as first-frame cards
        from mt.templates.t3.layout import t3_side_video_items
        items += t3_side_video_items(cfg, duration)

    for t in cfg.get("texts", []):
        spr = make_text_sprite(t["text"], t["font"], t["size"], t["color"],
                               t.get("stroke_color"), t.get("stroke_w", 0), max_w=int(W * 0.92))
        cx, cy = resolve_pos(t["pos"], spr.width, spr.height, W, H)
        items.append(Item(spr, t["start"], min(t["end"], duration + 1), cx, cy,
                          t.get("anim", "fade"), t.get("anim_dur", 0.5), True, 1.0,
                          rtl=is_rtl_text(t["text"])))

    # template-specific items (HUD cards for Template 1 / Custom; Template 2 plugs in here)
    items += get_template(cfg)["build_items"](cfg, duration, splits, lbox, rbox)

    for c in caption_streams(cfg):
        cues = parse_srt(c["srt"])
        groups = stream_groups(cues, c)
        if c.get("t2_style") in ("box", "sub"):          # Template 2 caption style (Part 3A)
            items += t2_caption_items(cfg, c, groups, duration)
            continue
        if c.get("t3_style") in ("box", "sub") or c.get("t4_style") in ("pill", "glow", "twoline"):      # T3 / T4 captions come from their build_items hook
            continue
        if c.get("ref", "box") == "box":
            cx = box["x"] + box["w"] / 2.0
            cy = box["y"] + box["h"] * c.get("y_pct", 72) / 100.0
            max_w = int(box["w"] * 0.94)
        else:
            cx = W / 2.0
            cy = H * c.get("y_pct", 73) / 100.0
            max_w = int(W * 0.84)
        for s, e, toks in groups:
            if s >= duration:
                continue
            ccx, ccy, cmw = cx, cy, max_w
            if lbox and c.get("ref", "box") == "box" and split_at(splits, (s + e) / 2.0):
                ccx = lbox["x"] + lbox["w"] / 2.0
                ccy = lbox["y"] + lbox["h"] * c.get("y_pct", 72) / 100.0
                cmw = int(lbox["w"] * 0.94)
            spr = make_rich_sprite([list(t) for t in toks], c["font"], c["size"], c["color"],
                                   c.get("hl_color"), c.get("stroke_color"), c.get("stroke_w", 0),
                                   max_w=cmw, shadow=c.get("shadow", False))
            items.append(Item(spr, s, e, ccx, ccy, c.get("anim", "fade"), c.get("anim_dur", 0.2),
                              out=False, rtl=is_rtl_text(" ".join(t[0] for t in toks))))
    return items


def build_ffmpeg_cmd(cfg, info, duration, out_path, preview, overlay_path):
    W, H = cfg["canvas"]
    box = cfg["video_box"]
    fps = info["fps"]
    fps_s = f"{fps.numerator}/{fps.denominator}" if fps.denominator != 1 else str(fps.numerator)
    bg = cfg["background"]
    T = SPLIT_T
    splits = compute_splits(cfg, duration)
    lbox, rbox = split_boxes(cfg) if splits else (None, None)

    lay3 = cfg.get("_t3_lay")            # Template 3 adaptive layout: animated frame (see mt/templates/t3/layout.py)
    lay3 = lay3 if lay3 and not lay3.get("still") else None
    cmd = [FFMPEG, "-y", "-hide_banner", "-loglevel", "error"]
    # input 0: background
    if bg["type"] == "color":
        cmd += ["-f", "lavfi", "-t", f"{duration:.3f}", "-i", f"color=c={_hex0x(bg['value'])}:s={W}x{H}:r={fps_s}"]
        bg_f = "[0:v]setsar=1,format=yuv420p[bg]"
    elif bg["type"] == "image":
        cmd += ["-loop", "1", "-framerate", fps_s, "-t", f"{duration:.3f}", "-i", bg["path"]]
        bg_f = (f"[0:v]scale={W}:{H}:force_original_aspect_ratio=increase,crop={W}:{H},"
                f"setsar=1,fps={fps_s},format=yuv420p[bg]")
    else:
        cmd += ["-stream_loop", "-1", "-t", f"{duration:.3f}", "-i", bg["path"]]
        bg_f = (f"[0:v]fps={fps_s},scale={W}:{H}:force_original_aspect_ratio=increase,crop={W}:{H},"
                f"setsar=1,format=yuv420p[bg]")
    blur4 = cfg.get("_t4_blur") if cfg.get("template_id") == 5 else None      # Template 4 blur backdrop (see mt/templates/t4/base.py)
    if blur4:
        from mt.templates.t4.base import t4_blur_filter
        bg_f = f"[rb]{t4_blur_filter(blur4)}[bg]"                              # input 0 stays an unused solid colour (keeps input numbering)
    # input 1: source video
    cmd += ["-i", cfg["video"]]
    # input 2: overlay frames (texts/captions/cards) pre-rendered by Python into a lossless alpha file
    cmd += ["-i", overlay_path]
    # inputs 3..: timed video layers (screen recordings, b-roll...)
    vl = [L for L in (cfg.get("video_layers") or [])
          if float(L["end"]) > float(L["start"]) and float(L["start"]) < duration]
    for L in vl:
        # -itsoffset delivers the frames at their real time (no early buffering);
        # L["path"] is already a small pre-scaled proxy (see prepare_video_layers)
        cmd += ["-thread_queue_size", "32", "-itsoffset", f"{float(L['start']):.3f}",
                "-t", f"{float(L['end']) - float(L['start']):.3f}", "-i", L["path"]]
    
    sb_idx = 3 + len(vl)
    if splits:
        cmd += ["-loop", "1", "-framerate", fps_s, "-t", f"{duration:.3f}", "-i", cfg["_split_bg"]]
    rg_idx = None   # Template 2 split: lime rounded borders + corner masks (fade/enable like the split background)
    if splits and cfg.get("_t2_split_ring"):
        rg_idx = sb_idx + 1
        cmd += ["-loop", "1", "-framerate", fps_s, "-t", f"{duration:.3f}", "-i", cfg["_t2_split_ring"]]
    fg_idx = None   # Template 2: foreground plate (design + rounded transparent video window)
    if cfg.get("_t2_fg"):
        fg_idx = sb_idx + (1 if splits else 0) + (1 if rg_idx is not None else 0)
        cmd += ["-loop", "1", "-framerate", fps_s, "-t", f"{duration:.3f}", "-i", cfg["_t2_fg"]]
    ph_idx, ph = None, cfg.get("_t2_playhead")     # Template 2 panel playhead: one tiny looped png moved by an x-expression
    if fg_idx is not None and ph:
        ph_idx = fg_idx + 1
        cmd += ["-loop", "1", "-framerate", fps_s, "-t", f"{duration:.3f}", "-i", ph["path"]]

    lay_idx = 3 + len(vl) + (1 if splits else 0) + (1 if rg_idx is not None else 0) + (1 if fg_idx is not None else 0) \
        + (1 if ph_idx is not None else 0)
    if lay3:
        from mt.templates.t3.layout import t3_layout_inputs
        cmd += t3_layout_inputs(cfg, fps_s, duration)
    bw, bh = box["w"], box["h"]
    vw, vh = info["w"], info["h"]
    ns = 1 + len(splits)
    if ns > 1 or blur4:
        src = (f"[1:v]fps={fps_s},setpts=PTS-STARTPTS,split={ns + (1 if blur4 else 0)}"
               + "".join(f"[r{i}]" for i in range(ns)) + ("[rb]" if blur4 else ""))
    else:
        src = f"[1:v]fps={fps_s},setpts=PTS-STARTPTS[r0]"
    if box.get("mode", "fit") == "fill":
        v_f = (f"[r0]scale={bw}:{bh}:force_original_aspect_ratio=increase,crop={bw}:{bh},"
               f"setsar=1[v]")
        ox, oy = box["x"], box["y"]
    else:
        sc = min(bw / vw, bh / vh)
        sw, sh = max(2, int(vw * sc) // 2 * 2), max(2, int(vh * sc) // 2 * 2)
        v_f = f"[r0]scale={sw}:{sh}:flags=lanczos,setsar=1[v]"
        ox, oy = box["x"] + (bw - sw) // 2, box["y"] + (bh - sh) // 2

    if lay3:
        from mt.templates.t3.layout import t3_layout_video
        _vf, ox, oy = t3_layout_video(cfg)
        v_f = f"[r0]{_vf}[v]"
    fc = f"{bg_f};{src};{v_f};[bg][v]overlay={ox}:{oy}[base0]"
    cur = "base0"
    if splits:
        n = len(splits)
        if n > 1:
            fc += f";[{sb_idx}:v]split={n}" + "".join(f"[sbr{i}]" for i in range(n))
            srcs = [f"sbr{i}" for i in range(n)]
        else:
            srcs = [f"{sb_idx}:v"]
        for i, (a, b) in enumerate(splits):
            s0, e1 = max(0.0, a - T), b + T
            fd = (f"fade=t=in:st={s0:.3f}:d={max(0.05, a - s0):.3f}:alpha=1,"
                  f"fade=t=out:st={b:.3f}:d={T}:alpha=1")
            en = f"enable='between(t,{s0:.3f},{e1:.3f})'"
            fc += (f";[{srcs[i]}]format=yuva420p,{fd}[sbf{i}]"
                   f";[{cur}][sbf{i}]overlay=0:0:eof_action=pass:{en}[sbo{i}]"
                   f";[r{i + 1}]scale={lbox['w']}:{lbox['h']}:force_original_aspect_ratio=increase,"
                   f"crop={lbox['w']}:{lbox['h']},setsar=1,format=yuva420p,{fd}[lv{i}]"
                   f";[sbo{i}][lv{i}]overlay={lbox['x']}:{lbox['y']}:eof_action=pass:{en}[sp{i}]")
            cur = f"sp{i}"    
    seq = list(enumerate(vl))
    if fg_idx is not None or lay3:
        # layers over the video box go UNDER the rounded frame (clipped by it), full-canvas layers above it
        seq = ([x for x in seq if x[1].get("area", "box") != "canvas"] + [(None, None)]
               + [x for x in seq if x[1].get("area", "box") == "canvas"])
    for i, L in seq:
        if i is None and lay3:
            from mt.templates.t3.layout import t3_layout_graph
            _frag, cur = t3_layout_graph(cfg, cur, lay_idx)
            fc += _frag
            continue
        if i is None:
            if rg_idx is not None:
                n = len(splits)
                if n > 1:
                    fc += f";[{rg_idx}:v]split={n}" + "".join(f"[rgs{j}]" for j in range(n))
                for j, (a, b) in enumerate(splits):
                    s0, e1 = max(0.0, a - T), b + T
                    fd = (f"fade=t=in:st={s0:.3f}:d={max(0.05, a - s0):.3f}:alpha=1,"
                          f"fade=t=out:st={b:.3f}:d={T}:alpha=1")
                    rsrc = f"rgs{j}" if n > 1 else f"{rg_idx}:v"
                    fc += (f";[{rsrc}]format=yuva420p,{fd}[rgf{j}]"
                           f";[{cur}][rgf{j}]overlay=0:0:eof_action=pass:enable='between(t,{s0:.3f},{e1:.3f})'[rgo{j}]")
                    cur = f"rgo{j}"
            fc += f";[{cur}][{fg_idx}:v]overlay=0:0:eof_action=repeat:format=auto[tfg]"
            cur = "tfg"
            continue
        s, e = float(L["start"]), float(L["end"])
        if L.get("area", "box") == "canvas":
            ax, ay, aw, ah = 0, 0, W, H
        elif rbox:
            ax, ay, aw, ah = rbox["x"], rbox["y"], rbox["w"], rbox["h"]
        else:
            ax, ay, aw, ah = box["x"], box["y"], bw, bh
        pos = f"{ax}:{ay}"
        if L.get("mode", "fill") != "fill":
            pos = f"{ax}+({aw}-overlay_w)/2:{ay}+({ah}-overlay_h)/2"
        if L.get("_xy"):                     # Template 2 floating card: fixed canvas position
            pos = f"{int(L['_xy'][0])}:{int(L['_xy'][1])}"
        fd = float(L.get("fade", 0.3))
        fades = ""
        if fd > 0:
            fades = (f",fade=t=in:st={s:.3f}:d={fd}:alpha=1"
                     f",fade=t=out:st={max(s, e - fd):.3f}:d={fd}:alpha=1")
        fc += (f";[{3 + i}:v]format=yuva420p{fades}[vl{i}]"
               f";[{cur}][vl{i}]overlay={pos}:eof_action=pass:"
               f"enable='between(t,{s:.3f},{e:.3f})'[base{i + 1}]")
        cur = f"base{i + 1}"
    if ph_idx is not None:
        fc += (f";[{cur}][{ph_idx}:v]overlay=x='{ph['cx0']:.3f}+({ph['cx1']:.3f}-{ph['cx0']:.3f})"
               f"*min(max(t/{float(info['duration']):.4f},0),1)-{ph['w'] / 2.0:.2f}':y={ph['y']}:"
               f"eof_action=repeat:format=auto[tph]")
        cur = "tph"
    fc += f";[{cur}][2:v]overlay=0:0:format=auto,format=yuv420p[out]"
    cmd += ["-filter_complex", fc, "-map", "[out]"]
    if info["audio"]:
        cmd += ["-map", "1:a:0", "-c:a", "aac", "-b:a", "192k"]
    cmd += ["-t", f"{duration:.3f}", "-c:v", "libx264",
            "-preset", "ultrafast" if preview else "veryfast",
            "-crf", "23" if preview else "18", "-pix_fmt", "yuv420p", "-movflags", "+faststart", out_path]
    return cmd


def video_layer_area(cfg, L):
    W, H = cfg["canvas"]
    if L.get("area", "box") == "canvas":
        return W, H
    if cfg.get("split") and cfg.get("_split_bg"):
        r = split_boxes(cfg)[1]
        return r["w"], r["h"]
    box = cfg["video_box"]
    return box["w"], box["h"]


def prepare_video_layers(cfg, info, tmpdir):
    """Pre-render every video layer (screen recording / b-roll) into a small, already
    scaled/cropped video with the right fps. This keeps FFmpeg's memory low even when
    the recording is huge (4K / 60fps / high bitrate)."""
    fps = info["fps"]
    fps_s = f"{fps.numerator}/{fps.denominator}" if fps.denominator != 1 else str(fps.numerator)
    out = []
    for i, L in enumerate(cfg.get("video_layers") or []):
        L = dict(L)
        dur = float(L["end"]) - float(L["start"])
        aw, ah = video_layer_area(cfg, L)
        if L.get("mode", "fill") == "fill":
            vf = (f"fps={fps_s},scale={aw}:{ah}:force_original_aspect_ratio=increase,"
                  f"crop={aw}:{ah},setsar=1,format=yuv420p")
        else:
            vf = (f"fps={fps_s},scale={aw}:{ah}:force_original_aspect_ratio=decrease:"
                  f"force_divisible_by=2,setsar=1,format=yuv420p")
        proxy = os.path.join(tmpdir, f"vlayer{i}.mp4")
        print(f"Preparing video layer {i + 1} ({Path(L['path']).name})...")
        cmd = [FFMPEG, "-y", "-hide_banner", "-loglevel", "error", "-t", f"{dur:.3f}", "-i", L["path"],
               "-an", "-vf", vf, "-threads", "2", "-c:v", "libx264", "-preset", "veryfast", "-crf", "16",
               "-pix_fmt", "yuv420p", proxy]
        r = subprocess.run(cmd, capture_output=True, text=True, encoding="utf-8", errors="replace")
        if r.returncode != 0 or not os.path.isfile(proxy):
            raise SystemExit("[!] Could not prepare the video layer:\n" + r.stderr[-800:])
        L["path"] = proxy
        out.append(L)
    return out


def render(cfg):
    cfg = preflight_cfg(cfg)
    cfg = dict(cfg)
    # old job files have no template_id -> Template 1
    cfg["template_id"] = get_template(cfg)["id"]
    # temp folders live next to the output file (not on the system drive)
    Path(cfg["output"]).parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    tmp = tempfile.mkdtemp(prefix="montage_", dir=str(Path(cfg["output"]).parent))
    # the template builds its static background (and split background) into cfg
    get_template(cfg)["build_background"](cfg, tmp)
    try:
        _require_ffmpeg()
        if cfg["template_id"] == 3 and cfg.get("video_layers"):       # T3: video layers with place "side" become baked cards beside the frame
            from mt.templates.t3.layout import t3_split_side_video_layers
            cfg["video_layers"], cfg["_t3_side_vl"] = t3_split_side_video_layers(cfg)
        pre = prepare_video_layers(cfg, probe(cfg["video"]), tmp) if cfg.get("video_layers") else None
        _hook = get_template(cfg).get("prepare_layers")      # only Template 2 registers one (t2_prepare_card_layers); render.py must not import template code
        cards = _hook(cfg, probe(cfg["video"]), tmp) if _hook and cfg["template_id"] in (2, 3, 5) else []
        if pre is not None or cards:
            cfg["video_layers"] = (pre or []) + cards      # floating video cards last = on top
        _render(cfg)
    finally:
        shutil.rmtree(tmp, ignore_errors=True)


def _render(cfg):
    _require_ffmpeg()
    info = probe(cfg["video"])
    W, H = cfg["canvas"]
    duration = info["duration"]
    preview = cfg.get("preview")
    if preview:
        duration = min(duration, float(preview))
    fps = info["fps"]
    fps_f = float(fps)
    n_frames = int(math.ceil(duration * fps_f))

    print("\nPreparing texts, captions and elements...")
    items = build_items(cfg, info["duration"])
    print(f"   {len(items)} timeline items ready.")

    out_path = cfg["output"]
    Path(out_path).parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    log_path = Path(out_path).with_suffix(".ffmpeg.log")
    fps_s = f"{fps.numerator}/{fps.denominator}" if fps.denominator != 1 else str(fps.numerator)
    work = tempfile.mkdtemp(prefix="montage_ov_", dir=str(Path(out_path).parent))
    overlay_path = os.path.join(work, "overlay.mov")

    # ---- Pass 1: draw the overlay frames in Python and encode them to a small lossless alpha file.
    # ffmpeg has NO other input in this pass, so it never buffers decoded video while waiting
    # for Python (that buffering is what filled the RAM before).
    cmd1 = [FFMPEG, "-y", "-hide_banner", "-loglevel", "error",
            "-f", "rawvideo", "-pix_fmt", "rgba", "-video_size", f"{W}x{H}", "-framerate", fps_s, "-i", "pipe:0",
            "-c:v", "qtrle", "-pix_fmt", "argb", overlay_path]
    logf = open(log_path, "w", encoding="utf-8", errors="replace")
    proc = subprocess.Popen(cmd1, stdin=subprocess.PIPE, stderr=logf, stdout=subprocess.DEVNULL)

    empty_bytes = bytes(W * H * 4)
    last_sig, last_bytes = (), empty_bytes
    t0 = time.time()
    print(f"Pass 1/2: drawing {n_frames} overlay frames ({W}x{H} @ {fps_f:.2f} fps)...")
    try:
        for i in range(n_frames):
            t = i / fps_f
            active = []
            sig = []
            for it in items:
                if it.start <= t < it.end:
                    st = anim_state(it.anim, it.progress(t), W, H)
                    active.append((it, st))
                    sig.append((id(it), round(st[0], 3), round(st[1]), round(st[2]), round(st[3], 3),
                                round(st[4], 1), None if st[5] is None else round(st[5], 3)))
            sig = tuple(sig)
            if sig != last_sig:
                if not active:
                    last_bytes = empty_bytes
                else:
                    canvas = Image.new("RGBA", (W, H), (0, 0, 0, 0))
                    for it, st in active:
                        draw_item(canvas, it, st)
                    last_bytes = canvas.tobytes()
                    del canvas
                last_sig = sig
            proc.stdin.write(last_bytes)
            if i % 20 == 0 or i == n_frames - 1:
                el = time.time() - t0
                eta = el / (i + 1) * (n_frames - i - 1)
                print(f"\r   {i + 1}/{n_frames}  ({(i + 1) * 100 // n_frames}%)  ETA {eta:4.0f}s   ",
                      end="", flush=True)
        proc.stdin.close()
    except (BrokenPipeError, OSError):
        pass
    except KeyboardInterrupt:
        proc.kill()
        logf.close()
        shutil.rmtree(work, ignore_errors=True)
        raise SystemExit("\nStopped.")
    rc = proc.wait()
    logf.close()
    print()
    if rc != 0 or not os.path.isfile(overlay_path):
        tail = Path(log_path).read_text(encoding="utf-8", errors="replace")[-1500:]
        shutil.rmtree(work, ignore_errors=True)
        print("[!] FFmpeg failed while saving the overlay:\n" + tail)
        raise SystemExit(1)

    # ---- Pass 2: combine background + video + video layers + overlay file (all plain files).
    print("Pass 2/2: combining everything and encoding the final video...")
    cmd2 = build_ffmpeg_cmd(cfg, info, duration, out_path, bool(preview), overlay_path)
    cmd2[cmd2.index("-loglevel") + 1] = "error"
    with open(log_path, "w", encoding="utf-8", errors="replace") as logf2:
        rc = subprocess.run(cmd2, stdin=subprocess.DEVNULL, stderr=logf2, stdout=subprocess.DEVNULL).returncode
    shutil.rmtree(work, ignore_errors=True)
    if rc != 0:
        tail = Path(log_path).read_text(encoding="utf-8", errors="replace")[-1500:]
        print("[!] FFmpeg failed:\n" + tail)
        raise SystemExit(1)
    try:
        log_path.unlink()
    except Exception:
        pass
    size = os.path.getsize(out_path) / (1024 * 1024)
    print(f"Done in {time.time() - t0:.0f}s  ->  {out_path}  ({size:.1f} MB)")


# ---- Template 2: still preview (--still) ------------------------------------------------
def _first_frame(video, size_hint=None):
    """First frame of the video as an RGB PIL image (None if it cannot be read)."""
    if not video or not os.path.isfile(str(video)) or not FFMPEG:
        return None
    fd, png = tempfile.mkstemp(suffix=".png")
    os.close(fd)
    try:
        r = subprocess.run([FFMPEG, "-y", "-hide_banner", "-loglevel", "error", "-i", str(video),
                            "-frames:v", "1", png], capture_output=True)
        return Image.open(png).convert("RGB").copy() if r.returncode == 0 and os.path.getsize(png) > 0 else None
    except Exception:
        return None
    finally:
        try:
            os.remove(png)
        except OSError:
            pass


def render_still(cfg, out_png, t=0.5):
    """ONE png of the final composite at time t (default 0.5 s), in about a second - no video encoding.
    Video = the first frame of cfg['video'] (mid-grey placeholder when there is no readable video)."""
    cfg = preflight_cfg(cfg, need_video=False)
    cfg["template_id"] = get_template(cfg)["id"]
    cfg["_t3_still_t"] = t               # Template 3 layout: which layout state to preview
    cfg["_t2_still"] = True              # video cards are drawn as Items (first frame) in a still
    W, H = cfg["canvas"]
    tmp = tempfile.mkdtemp(prefix="montage_still_")
    try:
        get_template(cfg)["build_background"](cfg, tmp)
        bg = cfg["background"]
        if bg["type"] == "color":
            img = Image.new("RGBA", (W, H), bg["value"])
        elif bg["type"] == "image":
            img = Image.open(bg["path"]).convert("RGB")
            if img.size != (W, H):
                s = max(W / img.width, H / img.height)
                img = img.resize((max(W, int(img.width * s)), max(H, int(img.height * s))), Image.LANCZOS)
                img = img.crop((0, 0, W, H))
            img = img.convert("RGBA")
        else:
            img = Image.new("RGBA", (W, H), (0, 0, 0, 255))
        box = cfg["video_box"]
        bw, bh = box["w"], box["h"]
        frame = _first_frame(cfg.get("video")) or Image.new("RGB", (max(2, bw), max(2, bh)), (128, 128, 128))
        if cfg.get("template_id") == 5 and cfg.get("_t4_blur"):                # Template 4 blur backdrop: blurred cover copy of the frame
            from mt.templates.t4.base import t4_blur_still
            img = t4_blur_still(frame, W, H, cfg["_t4_blur"])
        if box.get("mode", "fit") == "fill":
            s = max(bw / frame.width, bh / frame.height)
            fr = frame.resize((max(bw, int(round(frame.width * s))), max(bh, int(round(frame.height * s)))),
                              Image.LANCZOS)
            l, tp = (fr.width - bw) // 2, (fr.height - bh) // 2
            img.paste(fr.crop((l, tp, l + bw, tp + bh)), (box["x"], box["y"]))
        else:
            s = min(bw / frame.width, bh / frame.height)
            fr = frame.resize((max(2, int(frame.width * s)), max(2, int(frame.height * s))), Image.LANCZOS)
            img.paste(fr, (box["x"] + (bw - fr.width) // 2, box["y"] + (bh - fr.height) // 2))
        if cfg.get("_t2_fg"):
            img.alpha_composite(Image.open(cfg["_t2_fg"]).convert("RGBA"))
        info = probe(cfg["video"]) if cfg.get("video") and os.path.isfile(str(cfg["video"])) else None
        ph = cfg.get("_t2_playhead")
        if ph:
            frac = max(0.0, min(1.0, t / float(info["duration"] if info else 5.0)))
            sp = Image.open(ph["path"]).convert("RGBA")
            img.alpha_composite(sp, (int(ph["cx0"] + (ph["cx1"] - ph["cx0"]) * frac - sp.width / 2.0), ph["y"]))
        items = build_items(cfg, info["duration"] if info else 5.0)
        ov = Image.new("RGBA", (W, H), (0, 0, 0, 0))
        for it in items:
            if it.start <= t < it.end:
                draw_item(ov, it, anim_state(it.anim, it.progress(t), W, H))
        img.alpha_composite(ov)
        Path(out_png).parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
        img.convert("RGB").save(out_png)
        return out_png
    finally:
        shutil.rmtree(tmp, ignore_errors=True)

from pathlib import Path

def _ensure_python_packages():
    missing = []
    try:
        import PIL  # noqa: F401
    except ImportError:
        missing.append("Pillow")
    try:
        import arabic_reshaper  # noqa: F401
    except ImportError:
        missing.append("arabic-reshaper")
    try:
        try:
            from bidi import get_display  # noqa: F401
        except ImportError:
            from bidi.algorithm import get_display  # noqa: F401
    except ImportError:
        missing.append("python-bidi")
    try:
        import fontTools  # noqa: F401
    except ImportError:
        missing.append("fonttools")
    if not missing:
        return
    print("Missing Python packages: " + ", ".join(missing))
    try:
        ans = input("Install them now with pip? [Y/n]: ").strip().lower()
    except EOFError:
        sys.exit("Please run:  pip install " + " ".join(missing))
    if ans in ("", "y", "yes"):
        subprocess.check_call([sys.executable, "-m", "pip", "install", *missing])
        print("Installed. Continuing...\n")
    else:
        sys.exit("Please run:  pip install " + " ".join(missing))


# pip check runs on import, BEFORE the first PIL import (same order as the original montage.py: check, then Pillow)
_ensure_python_packages()
from PIL import ImageColor  # noqa: E402  (used by c_color)


def _find_tool(name):
    p = shutil.which(name)
    if p:
        return p
    exe = name + (".exe" if os.name == "nt" else "")
    for cand in (BASE / exe, BASE / "ffmpeg" / "bin" / exe, BASE / "bin" / exe):
        if cand.is_file():
            return str(cand)
    return None


FFMPEG = _find_tool("ffmpeg")


FFPROBE = _find_tool("ffprobe")


def _require_ffmpeg():
    if FFMPEG and FFPROBE:
        return
    print("\n[!] FFmpeg was not found on this computer.")
    print("    Windows : winget install Gyan.FFmpeg      (then close and reopen the terminal)")
    print("    macOS   : brew install ffmpeg")
    print("    Linux   : sudo apt install ffmpeg")
    sys.exit(1)

def _load_defaults():
    try:
        return json.loads(DEFAULTS_FILE.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
    except Exception:
        return {}


D = _load_defaults()


def _save_defaults():
    try:
        DEFAULTS_FILE.write_text(json.dumps(D, ensure_ascii=False, indent=1), encoding="utf-8")
    except Exception:
        pass
