r, w = quiet(lambda: (t4_time({"trigger": "open the project", "hold": 2}, CUES, 10, 4), t4_time({"trigger": "open the project"}, [CUES, []], 10, 4)))
rec("T4-3 trigger found (one cue list, or a list of streams); hold / default_hold", r == ((3.0, 5.0), (3.0, 7.0)) and not w, f"{r} {w}")
t4_warn_reset()
r, w = quiet(lambda: [t4_time({"trigger": "never said"}, CUES, 10, 4) for _ in range(3)])
rec("T4-3 trigger missing -> None, warned once", r == [None] * 3 and len(w) == 1, f"{r} {w}")
r, w = quiet(lambda: t4_time({"trigger": "never said", "start": 2, "hold": 1}, CUES, 10, 4))
rec("T4-3 trigger missing but start given -> start is used", r == (2.0, 3.0), f"{r} {w}")
r, w = quiet(lambda: (t4_time({"start": 1, "hold": 2.5}, None, 10, 4), t4_time({"start": 1}, None, 10, 4), t4_time({"start": 1, "hold": 0}, None, 10, 3), t4_time({"start": 1, "end": 9, "hold": 2}, None, 10, 4)))

r, w = quiet(lambda: (t4_time({"start": 8, "end": 20}, None, 10, 4), t4_time({"start": 9, "hold": 5}, None, 10, 4), t4_time({"start": 10}, None, 10, 4), t4_time({"start": 12}, None, 10, 4), t4_time({"start": 2}, None, 10, None)))

            ys, xs = np.where(al[H - 90:H - 30, int(W * 0.30):int(W * 0.70)] > 200)
            bar_w = (xs.max() - xs.min()) if xs.size else 0
            if not (pl.size == (W, H) and pl.mode == "RGBA" and cen.max() == 0 and al[:60].max() > 200 and al[-60:].max() > 200
                    and al[:, :60].max() > 200 and al[:, -60:].max() > 200 and bar_w > 120):
                bad_f.append(f"{cv}/{nm}: size={pl.size} centre_alpha={cen.max()} bar_w={bar_w}")
    rec("T4-7 frame plate (both canvases x EN / AR / long text): full-canvas RGBA, centre fully transparent, rails + brackets on all 4 sides, bar drawn", not bad_f, "; ".join(bad_f[:3]))
    W, H = CV["reels"]
    base = t4_frame_cfg({"on": True})
    notxt, noic, notk = (np.asarray(t4_frame_plate(W, H, dict(base, **kw)).getchannel("A"), int) for kw in ({"text": ""}, {"icon": ""}, {"show_ticks": False}))
    full = np.asarray(t4_frame_plate(W, H, base).getchannel("A"), int)
    rec("T4-7 frame options: text '' drops the bar + stripes, icon '' drops the icon, show_ticks False drops the ticks (nothing else changes)",
        (full - notxt)[H - 120:].sum() > 0 and (full - notxt)[:H - 120].sum() == 0 and (full - noic)[:260, W - 260:].sum() > 0 and (full - noic).clip(0).sum() == (full - noic)[:260, W - 260:].clip(0).sum()
        and (full - notk)[:, 40:70].sum() > 0 and (full - notk)[:, :20].sum() == 0 and (full - notk)[:, 100:W - 100].sum() == 0)
    # compose: one plate from several parts
    got = {}
    for cv, (W, H) in CV.items():
        cfg = {"canvas": [W, H], "t4": t4_cfg({"canvas_mode": cv, "frame": {"on": True}}), "video": str(ftmp / "none.mp4")}
        (ftmp / cv).mkdir()
        quiet(t4mod.t4_build_static_layers, cfg, str(ftmp / cv))
        got[cv] = (cfg.get("_t2_fg"), cfg["t4"]["frame"], cfg["canvas"])
    ok = all(v[0] and os.path.isfile(v[0]) and Image.open(v[0]).size == tuple(CV[k]) and v[1] == dict(T4_FRAME, on=True) and v[2] == CV[k] for k, v in got.items())
    rec("T4-7 t4_build_static_layers -> cfg['_t2_fg'] = one canvas-size PNG (both canvases), cfg['t4']['frame'] completed", ok, str(got))
    cfg = {"canvas": [1080, 1920], "t4": t4_cfg({"frame": {"on": False}}), "_t2_fg": "/stale.png"}
    quiet(t4mod.t4_build_static_layers, cfg, str(ftmp))
    cfg2 = {"canvas": [1080, 1920], "t4": t4_cfg({})}
    quiet(t4mod.t4_build_static_layers, cfg2, None)
    rec("T4-7 frame off (or no frame key) -> no plate, a stale cfg['_t2_fg'] is removed", "_t2_fg" not in cfg and "_t2_fg" not in cfg2, str(cfg.get("_t2_fg")))
    # a later module adds its part to the SAME plate; a failing part is skipped with one warning
    def dummy(W, H, t4):
        im = Image.new("RGBA", (W, H), (0, 0, 0, 0)); im.paste((255, 0, 0, 255), (W // 2 - 20, H // 2 - 20, W // 2 + 20, H // 2 + 20)); return im
    def boom(W, H, t4):
        raise ValueError("boom")
    def wrong(W, H, t4):
        return Image.new("RGBA", (10, 10))
    saved = list(t4mod.T4_FG_PARTS)
    try:
        t4mod.T4_FG_PARTS[:] = saved + [("dummy", dummy), ("boom", boom), ("wrong", wrong)]
        cfg = {"canvas": [1080, 1920], "t4": t4_cfg({"frame": {"on": True}})}
        t4_warn_reset(); r, w = quiet(t4mod.t4_compose_fg, cfg, str(ftmp))
        pm = np.asarray(Image.open(cfg["_t2_fg"]).convert("RGBA"))
        t4mod.T4_FG_PARTS[:] = [("dummy", dummy)]
        cfg3 = {"canvas": [1080, 1920], "t4": t4_cfg({})}
        quiet(t4mod.t4_compose_fg, cfg3, str(ftmp))
    finally:
        t4mod.T4_FG_PARTS[:] = saved
    rec("T4-7 t4_compose_fg: frame + a later part merged into ONE plate; a raising / wrong-size part -> one warning each, others kept; parts without the frame still work",
        pm[960, 540].tolist() == [255, 0, 0, 255] and pm[:60, :, 3].max() > 200 and len(w) == 2 and "boom" in w[0] and "1080x1920" in w[1] and "_t2_fg" in cfg3, f"{w}")
    t4_warn_reset(); r, w = quiet(t4_frame_part, 1080, 1920, {"frame": {"on": True, "icon": "banana"}})
    rec("T4-7 unknown icon -> one warning, plate still built without an icon; part = None when off", r is not None and len(w) == 1 and "banana" in w[0]
        and t4_frame_part(1080, 1920, {"frame": {"on": False}}) is None and t4_frame_part(1080, 1920, None) is None, f"{w}")
    def ask_with(ans):
        it = iter(ans); old = builtins.input; builtins.input = lambda *_: next(it)
        try: return quiet(t4_ask_frame)[0]
        finally: builtins.input = old
    from mt.common import D                                                 # ask() remembers answers as the next defaults
    D.clear(); qn, qy = ask_with(["n"]), ask_with(["y", "KEEP IT", "red", "3", "n"])
    D.clear(); qd = ask_with(["y", "", "", "", ""]); D.clear()
    rec("T4-7 question group: n -> {'on': False}; y + answers -> {on, text, color, icon, show_ticks}; Enter = defaults",
        qn == {"on": False} and qy == {"on": True, "text": "KEEP IT", "color": "#ff0000", "icon": "laptop", "show_ticks": False}
        and qd == {"on": True, "text": T4_FRAME["text"], "color": T4_FRAME["color"].lower(), "icon": "code", "show_ticks": True}, f"{qn} {qy} {qd}")
    # real --still on both canvases: the video pixels are untouched wherever the plate is transparent
    bad_s = []
    for cv, (W, H) in CV.items():
        mp4 = ftmp / f"v_{cv}.mp4"
        subprocess.run([FFMPEG, "-y", "-hide_banner", "-loglevel", "error", "-f", "lavfi", "-i", f"testsrc2=s={W}x{H}:r=25:d=2", "-pix_fmt", "yuv420p", str(mp4)], check=True)
        res = {}
        for nm, fr in (("off", {"on": False}), ("on", {"on": True})):
            fr = dict(fr, font=default_font())                              # same font in this process and in montage.py
            job = dict(job_for(mp4, cv, "fill"), t4=t4_cfg({"canvas_mode": cv, "fit": "fill", "frame": fr}), output=str(ftmp / "o.mp4"))
            jp, out = ftmp / f"{cv}_{nm}.json", ftmp / f"{cv}_{nm}.png"
            jp.write_text(json.dumps(job), encoding="utf-8")
            r = subprocess.run([sys.executable, str(P / "montage.py"), "--still", "--job", str(jp), "--still-out", str(out), "--arabic-mode", "reshape"], cwd=str(ftmp), capture_output=True, text=True, encoding="utf-8", errors="replace")
            res[nm] = np.asarray(Image.open(out).convert("RGB"), int) if r.returncode == 0 and out.is_file() else None
        if res["off"] is None or res["on"] is None:
            bad_s.append(f"{cv}: still failed"); continue
        al = np.asarray(t4_frame_plate(W, H, t4_frame_cfg({"on": True, "font": default_font()})).getchannel("A"))
        same = (np.abs(res["on"] - res["off"]).max(axis=2) <= 2)
        if not (same[al == 0].all() and (~same).sum() > 3000 and (~same)[:, :].any(axis=1)[:60].any()):
            bad_s.append(f"{cv}: untouched={same[al == 0].mean():.4f} changed_px={(~same).sum()}")
    rec("T4-7 --still on both canvases: frame drawn over the video, every pixel the plate leaves transparent is byte-identical to the frame-less render", not bad_s, "; ".join(bad_s))
finally:
    shutil.rmtree(ftmp, ignore_errors=True)

def _np_job(cv, frame_on, tag=None, lst=None, **extra):
    t4 = t4_cfg({"canvas_mode": cv, "frame": {"on": frame_on}, "nameplate": tag or {"on": False}, "corner_list": lst or {"on": False}})
    return dict({"canvas": list(CV[cv]), "t4": t4}, **extra)
def _ink(it, W, H):
    full = Image.new("L", (W, H), 0)
    full.paste(it.sprite.getchannel("A").point(lambda v: 255 if v > INK_THR else 0), (int(it.cx - it.sprite.width / 2), int(it.cy - it.sprite.height / 2)))
    return full

        and el == [(100, 200), (90.0, 200), (30.0, 260), (20, 260)] and abs((el[2][0] - el[1][0])) == abs(el[2][1] - el[1][1])
        and _elbow((100, 200), (20, 260), "left", "line") == [(100, 200), (20, 260)] and len(_elbow((100, 100), (160, 40), "top", "elbow")) == 2, f"{el}")
    kc = t4_cards_cfg([{"media": SQ, "connector": {"points": ["card-left", [5, 5000], "999%,50%"]}}])[0]["connector"]
    pts = _conn_points(kc, (500, 100, 980, 580), 1080, 1920, 1.0)
    m_ = 48 + 9 * 1.6 + 2
    rec("T4-11 connector points: 'card-left' = the card's left edge centre; points far outside the canvas are clamped so glow + dot stay in the 48 px safe area",
        pts[0] == (500.0, 340.0) and all(m_ - 1e-6 <= x <= 1080 - m_ + 1e-6 and m_ - 1e-6 <= y <= 1920 - m_ + 1e-6 for x, y in pts), f"{pts}")
    W, H = CV["youtube"]
    ic = quiet(t4_cards_items, cj("youtube", False, [{"media": SQ, "connector": {"target": [576, 648], "end_dot": True}}]), 10.0)[0][0]
    px = np.asarray(ic.sprite.getchannel("A"))[int(648 - (ic.cy - ic.sprite.height / 2)) - 2:int(648 - (ic.cy - ic.sprite.height / 2)) + 3, int(576 - (ic.cx - ic.sprite.width / 2)) - 2:int(576 - (ic.cx - ic.sprite.width / 2)) + 3]
    rec("T4-11 connector end dot sits exactly on the target point (opaque there)", px.max() > 230, f"max alpha at the target = {px.max()}")
    for cv, (W, H) in CV.items():
        ix = quiet(t4_cards_items, cj(cv, False, [{"media": SQ, "xy": ["50%", "50%"], "label": "CODE"}, {"media": TL, "xy": [-500, 99999], "width": 300}]), 10.0)[0]
        b1, b2 = card_ink(ix[0], W, H).getbbox(), card_ink(ix[1], W, H).getbbox()
        rec(f"T4-11 xy on {cv}: the card block is centred on 50%,50%; an xy far outside is clamped inside the safe area",
            abs((b1[0] + b1[2]) / 2 - W / 2) < 2 and abs((b1[1] + b1[3]) / 2 - H / 2) < 2 and b2[0] >= 47 and b2[1] >= 47 and W - b2[2] >= 47 and H - b2[3] >= 47, f"{b1} {b2}")
    t4_warn_reset(); r1, w1 = quiet(t4_cards_items, cj("reels", False, [{"media": "/no/such/a.png"}]), 10.0)
    t4_warn_reset(); r1b, w1b = quiet(lambda: (t4_cards_items(cj("reels", False, [{"media": str(ktmp / "gone.mp4")}]), 10.0), t4_cards_items(cj("reels", False, [{"media": str(ktmp / "gone.mp4")}]), 10.0)))
    t4_warn_reset(); r2, w2 = quiet(t4_cards_items, dict(cj("reels", False, [{"media": SQ, "trigger": "never said"}]), captions=[]), 10.0)
    t4_warn_reset(); r3, w3 = quiet(t4_cards_items, cj("reels", False, [{"media": SQ, "start": 50}]), 10.0)
    r4 = quiet(t4_cards_items, {"t4": t4_cfg({"cards": [{"media": SQ}]})}, 10.0)[0]
    r5 = quiet(t4_cards_items, cj("reels", False, {"on": False}), 10.0)[0]
    rec("T4-11 never raises: missing picture -> still 1 card + ONE warning; missing video -> placeholder + warning naming the file (printed once per render); trigger not found / start after the end -> skipped + 1 warning; no canvas / module off -> []",
        len(r1) == 1 and len(w1) == 1 and len(r1b[0]) == 1 and len(w1b) == 1 and "gone.mp4" in w1b[0] and r2 == [] and len(w2) == 1 and r3 == [] and len(w3) == 1 and r4 == [] and r5 == [], f"{w1} {w1b} {w2} {w3}")
    t4_warn_reset(); r, w = quiet(t4_cards_items, cj("reels", False, [{"media": SQ, "anchor": "top-right"}, {"media": TL, "anchor": "top-right"}]), 10.0)
    rec("T4-11 two cards on the same anchor -> an overlap warning (never silent)", len(r) == 2 and any("overlap" in x for x in w), f"{w}")
    jb = dict(job_for(ctmp / "none.mp4", "youtube", "fill"), t4=cj("youtube", True, [{"media": SQ, "connector": [900, 500]}])["t4"])
    rec("T4-11 render.build_items: the T4 hook draws the card items next to captions / nameplate / tiles", len([i for i in quiet(build_items, jb, 10.0)[0] if i.anim in ("t4_pop", "fade")]) == 2)
    def ask_k(ans):
        it_ = itertools.chain(ans, itertools.repeat("")); old = builtins.input; builtins.input = lambda *_: next(it_)
        try: return quiet(t4_ask_image_cards)[0]
        finally: builtins.input = old
    D.clear(); q0 = ask_k(["n"])
    D.clear(); q1 = ask_k(["y", SQ, "LIVE CODING", "400", "1", "y", "30%,40%", "1", "0:02", "4", "fade"])
    D.clear(); q2 = ask_k(["y", "/nope.png", SQ, "", "", "", "n", "2", "open the project", "", ""])
    D.clear()
    rec("T4-11 question group: n -> {'on': False}; answers -> one card (media, label, width, anchor, connector target, start + hold, anim); a missing path is re-asked; Enter = defaults",
        q0 == {"on": False} and len(q1) == 1 and q1[0]["media"] == SQ and q1[0]["label"] == "LIVE CODING" and q1[0]["width"] == 400.0 and q1[0]["anchor"] == "top-left"
        and q1[0]["connector"]["target"] == ["30%", "40%"] and q1[0]["start"] == 2.0 and q1[0]["hold"] == 4.0 and q1[0]["anim"] == "fade"
        and q2[0]["media"] == SQ and q2[0]["label"] == "" and q2[0]["width"] == 480.0 and q2[0]["anchor"] == "top-right" and "connector" not in q2[0] and q2[0]["trigger"] == "open the project" and q2[0]["anim"] == "pop", f"{q1} {q2}")
    bad_s = []
    for cv, (W, H) in CV.items():
        mp4 = ktmp / f"v_{cv}.mp4"
        subprocess.run([FFMPEG, "-y", "-hide_banner", "-loglevel", "error", "-f", "lavfi", "-i", f"testsrc2=s={W}x{H}:r=25:d=2", "-pix_fmt", "yuv420p", str(mp4)], check=True)
        res = {}
        for nm, cd in (("off", []), ("plain", [{"media": SQ}]), ("label", [{"media": SQ, "label": "LIVE CODING DEVELOPMENT"}]), ("conn", [{"media": SQ, "connector": {"target": "30%,60%"}}])):
            job = dict(job_for(mp4, cv, "fill"), t4=t4_cfg({"canvas_mode": cv, "fit": "fill", "cards": cd}), output=str(ktmp / "o.mp4"))
            out = ktmp / f"s_{cv}_{nm}.png"; quiet(render_still, job, str(out), 1.5)
            res[nm] = np.asarray(Image.open(out).convert("RGB"), int) if out.is_file() else None
        if any(v is None for v in res.values()):
            bad_s.append(f"{cv}: still failed"); continue
        df = {nm: np.abs(res[nm] - res["off"]).max(axis=2) > 2 for nm in ("plain", "label", "conn")}
        bb = {nm: (np.where(m.any(axis=0))[0], np.where(m.any(axis=1))[0]) for nm, m in df.items()}
        tx, ty = int(W * 0.3), int(H * 0.6)
        ok = (df["plain"].sum() > 20000 and bb["plain"][0].min() >= 24 and bb["plain"][1].min() >= 24 and bb["plain"][0].max() <= W - 24 and not df["plain"][:, :int(W * 0.45)].any()
              and not df["label"][:, :int(W * 0.45)].any() and bb["label"][1].max() > bb["plain"][1].max() + 20 and df["conn"][ty - 3:ty + 4, tx - 3:tx + 4].any() and df["conn"].sum() > df["plain"].sum() + 150)
        if not ok:
            bad_s.append(f"{cv}: px plain/label/conn={df['plain'].sum()}/{df['label'].sum()}/{df['conn'].sum()} plain x={bb['plain'][0].min()}-{bb['plain'][0].max()} y={bb['plain'][1].min()}-{bb['plain'][1].max()} label ymax={bb['label'][1].max()}")
    rec("T4-11 --still on both canvases (plain / label / connector): the card is drawn over the video in the top-right, the label sticks out below it, the connector reaches its target, the left of the canvas stays byte-identical", not bad_s, "; ".join(bad_s))
finally:
    shutil.rmtree(ktmp, ignore_errors=True)
print(sum(rows), "pass,", len(rows) - sum(rows), "fail"); sys.exit(0 if all(rows) else 1)

import os, sys
sys.path.insert(0, os.getcwd())
from PIL import Image
from mt.templates.t4.scenes import SLOTS, _put
from mt.templates.t4.scenes_reg import get_widget
CASES = {
  "en": {"icon": "globe", "title": "Web Technology", "subtitle": "Build \u2022 Develop \u2022 Deploy"},
  "ar": {"icon": "globe", "title": "تقنيات الويب", "subtitle": "تصميم • تطوير • نشر"},
  "long": {"icon": "globe", "title": "Full Stack Web Development And Cloud Deployment Services For Growing Businesses", "subtitle": "Build \u2022 Develop \u2022 Deploy"},
  "nosub": {"icon": "globe", "title": "Web Technology", "subtitle": ""},
}
os.makedirs("out/t4", exist_ok=True)
for tag, spec in CASES.items():
    for name, (W, H) in {"reels": (1080, 1920), "youtube": (1920, 1080)}.items():
        s, asp = min(W, H) / 1080.0, ("portrait" if H > W else "landscape")
        ctx = {"W": W, "H": H, "s": s, "aspect": asp, "preset": "web_dev", "cfg": {}}
        bg = Image.new("RGBA", (W, H), (12, 22, 48, 255))
        for t, slot, ws in (("title_block", "title", {}), ("code_window", "code", {}), ("ring", "ring", {"icon": "globe"}), ("bullet_list", "bullets", {}), ("info_card", "card", spec)):
            box = tuple(v * s for v in SLOTS["web_dev"][asp][slot])
            sp = get_widget(t)(dict(ws, type=t), box, ctx)
            pl = _put(sp, box, W, H, s)
            x, y = int(round(pl[0] - sp.width / 2.0)), int(round(pl[1] - sp.height / 2.0))
            bg.alpha_composite(sp, (x, y))
            for ls, lx, ly in sp.info.get("lines") or []:
                bg.alpha_composite(ls, (x + lx, y + ly))
            print(tag, name, t, "ink", [round(v) for v in pl[2]], "moved", pl[3])
        bg.convert("RGB").save("out/t4/p18_card_%s_%s.png" % (tag, name))

"""P16 check (put in tools/): python tools/mt_t4_p16_check.py
Joint check of the web_dev title scene (title_block + code_window + ring + bullet_list + info_card) on R5 (1080x1920) and R6 (1920x1080):
ink-rect overlaps, 48 px safe margin, connector-vs-widget crossings, stagger, widget failure isolation, stills + 3 s renders -> out/t4/p16_*.
EDIT AR_SPEC / EN_SPEC below with the real cfg keys of ring / bullet_list / info_card (empty dict = widget defaults)."""
import json, os, re, subprocess, sys, tempfile, shutil
from pathlib import Path
P = Path(os.environ.get("MT_PROJECT", Path(__file__).resolve().parent.parent))
sys.path.insert(0, str(P)); sys.argv = ["montage.py", "--help"]
import mt.templates  # noqa: E402
from mt.common import FFMPEG  # noqa: E402
from mt.templates.t4 import scenes as S  # noqa: E402
from mt.templates.t4.cfg import t4_cfg  # noqa: E402
from mt.templates.t4.scenes_reg import register_widget  # noqa: E402
from mt.templates.t4.nameplate import T4_SAFE  # noqa: E402

EN_SPEC = {"title_block": {"title": "Web Development", "subtitle": "BUILD. SHIP. SCALE."}, "code_window": {}, "ring": {}, "bullet_list": {}, "info_card": {}}
AR_SPEC = {"title_block": {"title": "ØªØ·ÙˆÙŠØ± Ø§Ù„ÙˆÙŠØ¨", "subtitle": "Ø§Ø¨Ù†Ù ÙˆØ§Ù†Ø´Ø±"}, "code_window": {}, "ring": {}, "bullet_list": {}, "info_card": {}}   # <- add Arabic text keys
CANV = {"reels": (1080, 1920), "youtube": (1920, 1080)}
rows, warns = [], []
def rec(name, ok, detail=""):
    rows.append(bool(ok)); print(f"{'PASS' if ok else 'FAIL':5} {name}  {detail}")
def hook_warn():                                   # collect every t4_warn, whichever module imported it
    def w(msg, *a, **k): warns.append(str(msg))
    for m in [m for n, m in list(sys.modules.items()) if n.startswith("mt.templates.t4") and hasattr(m, "t4_warn")]: m.t4_warn = w
def scene(spec, **kw):
    return dict({"preset": "web_dev", "widgets": [dict(type=t, **v) for t, v in spec.items()], "start": 0, "hold": 3, "stagger": 0.2}, **kw)
def cfg_for(cv, sc): 
    W, H = CANV[cv]; return {"canvas": [W, H], "t4": t4_cfg({"canvas_mode": cv, "scenes": [sc]})}
def seg_hits_rect(a, b, r, pad=1):                 # sampled segment vs rect (connectors are straight / elbow polylines)
    n = max(2, int(max(abs(b[0]-a[0]), abs(b[1]-a[1])))); 
    return any(r[0]+pad < a[0]+(b[0]-a[0])*i/n < r[2]-pad and r[1]+pad < a[1]+(b[1]-a[1])*i/n < r[3]-pad for i in range(n+1))
hook_warn()
for lang, spec in (("en", EN_SPEC), ("ar", AR_SPEC)):
    for cv, (W, H) in CANV.items():
        tag = f"{cv}/{lang}"; warns.clear(); sc = S.t4_scenes_cfg([scene(spec)])[0]; s = min(W, H) / 1080
        ctx = {"W": W, "H": H, "s": s, "aspect": S._aspect(W, H), "preset": "web_dev", "cfg": cfg_for(cv, scene(spec))}
        rects, placed = {}, []
        S._widget_items(ctx["cfg"], sc, "p16", (0.0, 3.0), ctx, S.SLOTS["web_dev"][ctx["aspect"]], rects, placed)
        rec(f"{tag}: all widgets placed", len(rects) == len(spec), f"got {sorted(rects)}")
        m, bad = T4_SAFE * s - 1, [k for k, r in rects.items() if r[0] < T4_SAFE*s-1 or r[1] < T4_SAFE*s-1 or r[2] > W-T4_SAFE*s+1 or r[3] > H-T4_SAFE*s+1]
        rec(f"{tag}: ink inside 48 px safe margin", not bad, ",".join(bad))
        ov = [(a, b) for i, (a, ra) in enumerate(placed) for b, rb in placed[i+1:] if ra[0] < rb[2] and rb[0] < ra[2] and ra[1] < rb[3] and rb[1] < ra[3]]
        rec(f"{tag}: no sprite ink overlap", not ov, str(ov))
        lines = S._lines(S.CONNECTORS["web_dev"][ctx["aspect"]]) or []; hit = []
        for ln in lines:
            pts = [S._resolve(p, rects, s) for p in ln["points"]]
            if any(p is None for p in pts): continue
            named = {re.sub(r"-(top|bottom|left|right|center)$", "", p) for p in ln["points"] if isinstance(p, str)}
            for k, r in rects.items():
                if k not in named and any(seg_hits_rect(pts[i], pts[i+1], r) for i in range(len(pts)-1)): hit.append((ln["points"], k))
        rec(f"{tag}: connectors ({len(lines)}) cross no other widget", not hit, str(hit))
        its = S.t4_scenes_items(ctx["cfg"], 6.0); st = sorted({round(i.start, 2) for i in its})
        rec(f"{tag}: stagger (distinct start times, ~0.2 s apart)", len(st) >= len(spec) and all(0.1 <= b-a <= 0.35 for a, b in zip(st[:len(spec)], st[1:len(spec)])), str(st))
        rec(f"{tag}: no unexpected warnings", not [w for w in warns if "overlap" in w or "could not" in w], "; ".join(warns[:3]))
# failure isolation: one widget raises, one is unknown, one is off -> others still drawn, one warning each
register_widget("t4_boom", lambda spec, box, ctx: 1 / 0)
for cv in CANV:
    warns.clear(); sp = scene(EN_SPEC); sp["widgets"] += [{"type": "t4_boom", "slot": "ring"}, {"type": "nope", "slot": "card"}, {"type": "ring", "slot": "zzz"}, {"type": "info_card", "on": False}]
    base = len(S.t4_scenes_items(cfg_for(cv, scene(EN_SPEC)), 6.0)); n = len(S.t4_scenes_items(cfg_for(cv, sp), 6.0))
    rec(f"{cv}: failing / unknown / bad-slot widgets are skipped, rest unchanged", n <= base and sum("boom" in w or "nope" in w or "zzz" in w for w in warns) >= 3, f"items {n} vs {base}; warns {len(warns)}")
# stills + 3 s renders
tmp, out = Path(tempfile.mkdtemp(prefix="t4p16_")), P / "out" / "t4"; out.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
try:
    vid = tmp / "v.mp4"
    subprocess.run([FFMPEG, "-y", "-hide_banner", "-loglevel", "error", "-f", "lavfi", "-i", "testsrc2=s=640x360:r=25:d=4", "-f", "lavfi", "-i", "sine=f=440:d=4",
                    "-c:v", "libx264", "-pix_fmt", "yuv420p", "-c:a", "aac", "-shortest", str(vid)], check=True)
    for cv, (W, H) in CANV.items():
        for lang, spec in (("en", EN_SPEC), ("ar", AR_SPEC)):
            job = {"template_id": 5, "video": str(vid), "canvas": [W, H], "video_box": {"x": 0, "y": 0, "w": W, "h": H, "mode": "fill"}, "background": {"type": "color", "value": "#000000"},
                   "t4": t4_cfg({"canvas_mode": cv, "scenes": [scene(spec, hold=3, anim="none")]}), "layers": [], "captions": [], "cards": [], "texts": [], "elements": [], "video_layers": [],
                   "output": str(out / f"p16_{cv}_{lang}.mp4"), "preview": 3}
            jf = tmp / f"{cv}_{lang}.json"; jf.write_text(json.dumps(job), encoding="utf-8")
            for kind, extra in (("still", ["--still", "--still-out", str(out / f"p16_{cv}_{lang}.png")]), ("render", [])):
                r = subprocess.run([sys.executable, str(P / "montage.py"), "--job", str(jf), "--arabic-mode", "raqm"] + extra, cwd=str(tmp), capture_output=True, text=True, encoding="utf-8", errors="replace")
                w = [l for l in (r.stdout + r.stderr).splitlines() if re.search(r"warn", l, re.I)]
                ok = r.returncode == 0 and (out / (f"p16_{cv}_{lang}.png" if kind == "still" else f"p16_{cv}_{lang}.mp4")).is_file()
                rec(f"{cv}/{lang} {kind}: exit 0 + file + no warnings", ok and not w, "; ".join(w[:3]) or (r.stderr[-200:] if not ok else ""))
finally:
    shutil.rmtree(tmp, ignore_errors=True)
print(sum(rows), "pass,", len(rows) - sum(rows), "fail"); sys.exit(0 if all(rows) else 1)


====================================================================================================
FILE: tools\mt_t4_charts_check.py

"""Template 4 check of 'bar_chart' / 'progress_list' / 'badge' (P19): python tools/mt_t4_charts_check.py   (run from the project root)
Both canvases x EN / AR: items, 48 px safe margin, growth bands <= 9, junk specs, growth over time, ms / frame of a 5 s scene,
stills -> out/t4/charts_<canvas>_<lang>.png"""
import contextlib, io, os, shutil, subprocess, sys, tempfile, time
from pathlib import Path
sys.path.insert(0, os.getcwd())
import numpy as np
from PIL import Image
import mt.templates                                            # noqa: F401  (registers the templates)
from mt.anim import anim_state
from mt.common import FFMPEG
from mt.draw import draw_item
from mt.render import render_still
from mt.templates.t4.cfg import t4_cfg
from mt.templates.t4.scenes import SLOTS, t4_scenes_items
from mt.templates.t4.scenes_reg import get_widget
from mt.templates.t4.warn import t4_warn_reset

CV = {"reels": (1080, 1920), "youtube": (1920, 1080)}
LANG = {"en": {"bar_chart": {"values": [35, 55, 42, 78, 95], "labels": ["Jan", "Feb", "Mar", "Apr", "May"]},
               "progress_list": {"rows": ["code|Python|90", "database|SQL|75", "globe|Web design|60"]},
               "badge": {"text": "Python", "icon": "code"}},
        "ar": {"bar_chart": {"values": [35, 55, 42, 78, 95], "labels": ["ÙŠÙ†Ø§ÙŠØ±", "ÙØ¨Ø±Ø§ÙŠØ±", "Ù…Ø§Ø±Ø³", "Ø£Ø¨Ø±ÙŠÙ„", "Ù…Ø§ÙŠÙˆ"]},
               "progress_list": {"rows": ["code|Ø¨Ø§ÙŠØ«ÙˆÙ†|90", "database|Ù‚ÙˆØ§Ø¹Ø¯ Ø§Ù„Ø¨ÙŠØ§Ù†Ø§Øª|75", "globe|ØªØµÙ…ÙŠÙ… Ø§Ù„Ù…ÙˆØ§Ù‚Ø¹|60"]},
               "badge": {"text": "Ø¨Ø§ÙŠØ«ÙˆÙ†", "icon": "code"}}}
rows = []
def rec(name, ok, detail=""):
    rows.append(bool(ok)); print(f"{'PASS' if ok else 'FAIL':5} {name}  {detail}")
def quiet(fn, *a):
    buf = io.StringIO()
    with contextlib.redirect_stdout(buf):
        r = fn(*a)
    return r, [l for l in buf.getvalue().splitlines() if "[!]" in l]
def ink(it):
    ys, xs = np.where(np.asarray(it.sprite.getchannel("A")) > 8)
    if not len(xs): return None
    x0, y0 = it.cx - it.sprite.width / 2.0, it.cy - it.sprite.height / 2.0
    return x0 + xs.min(), y0 + ys.min(), x0 + xs.max() + 1, y0 + ys.max() + 1
def scene(lang):
    return [{"preset": "web_dev", "start": 0, "hold": 5, "stagger": 0.3, "widgets": [dict(type=t, **v) for t, v in LANG[lang].items()]}]
def job(video, cv, sc, out):
    W, H = CV[cv]
    return {"template_id": 5, "video": str(video), "canvas": [W, H], "video_box": {"x": 0, "y": 0, "w": W, "h": H, "mode": "fill"},
            "background": {"type": "color", "value": "#000000"}, "t4": t4_cfg({"canvas_mode": cv, "fit": "fill", "scenes": sc}), "layers": [],
            "captions": [], "cards": [], "texts": [], "elements": [], "video_layers": [], "output": str(out)}
def draw_at(items, W, H, t):
    cv = Image.new("RGBA", (W, H), (0, 0, 0, 0))
    for it in items:
        if it.start <= t < it.end:
            draw_item(cv, it, anim_state(it.anim, it.progress(t), W, H))
    return cv
def cost(items, W, H, fps=25, dur=5.0):
    """Same loop as render._render pass 1 (signature cache + tobytes): -> (avg ms / frame, worst ms, frames really drawn, ms per drawn frame)."""
    last, per, drawn = None, [], 0
    for i in range(int(dur * fps)):
        t, t0, act, sig = i / fps, time.perf_counter(), [], []
        for it in items:
            if it.start <= t < it.end:
                st = anim_state(it.anim, it.progress(t), W, H); act.append((it, st))
                sig.append((id(it), round(st[0], 3), round(st[1]), round(st[2]), round(st[3], 3), round(st[4], 1), None if st[5] is None else round(st[5], 3)))
        sig = tuple(sig)
        if sig != last:
            if act:
                c = Image.new("RGBA", (W, H), (0, 0, 0, 0))
                for it, st in act: draw_item(c, it, st)
                c.tobytes(); drawn += 1
            last = sig
        per.append((time.perf_counter() - t0) * 1000)
    return sum(per) / len(per), max(per), drawn, sum(per) / max(1, drawn)

tmp = Path(tempfile.mkdtemp(prefix="t4ch_"))
os.makedirs("out/t4", exist_ok=True)
try:
    for cv, (W, H) in CV.items():
        s, asp = min(W, H) / 1080.0, ("portrait" if H > W else "landscape")
        mp4 = tmp / f"v_{cv}.mp4"
        subprocess.run([FFMPEG, "-y", "-hide_banner", "-loglevel", "error", "-f", "lavfi", "-i", f"testsrc2=s={W}x{H}:r=25:d=2", "-pix_fmt", "yuv420p", str(mp4)], check=True)
        for lang in LANG:
            tag = f"{cv}/{lang}"
            j = job(mp4, cv, scene(lang), tmp / "o.mp4")
            t4_warn_reset(); items, w = quiet(t4_scenes_items, j, 5.0)
            m = 48 * s - 1
            bad = [i for i in (ink(it) for it in items) if i is None or i[0] < m or i[1] < m or i[2] > W - m or i[3] > H - m]
            rec(f"{tag}: items built, no warning, every item >= 48 px from the edges", len(items) >= 12 and not w and not bad, f"{len(items)} items warn={w} bad={bad[:2]}")
            ctx = {"W": W, "H": H, "s": s, "aspect": asp, "preset": "web_dev", "cfg": {}}
            sp = get_widget("bar_chart")(dict(LANG[lang]["bar_chart"]), tuple(v * s for v in SLOTS["web_dev"][asp]["chart"]), ctx)
            rec(f"{tag}: bar_chart growth = {len(sp.info['lines'])} pre-built items (<= 9 = 8 steps + value labels), static sprite has no bars",
                0 < len(sp.info["lines"]) <= 9, "")
            box = [int(v * s) for v in SLOTS["web_dev"][asp]["chart"]]
            a1 = draw_at(items, W, H, 0.6).getchannel("A").crop((box[0], box[1], box[0] + box[2], box[1] + box[3]))
            a2 = draw_at(items, W, H, 3.0).getchannel("A").crop((box[0], box[1], box[0] + box[2], box[1] + box[3]))
            rec(f"{tag}: bars grow over time (chart alpha t=0.6 s < t=3.0 s)", np.asarray(a1, int).sum() < np.asarray(a2, int).sum(), f"{np.asarray(a1, int).sum()} < {np.asarray(a2, int).sum()}")
            avg, worst, drawn, per = cost(items, W, H)
            rec(f"{tag}: 5 s scene render cost", avg < 250, f"avg {avg:.1f} ms/frame, worst {worst:.0f} ms, {drawn} of 125 frames really drawn ({per:.0f} ms each)")
            out = Path("out/t4") / f"charts_{cv}_{lang}.png"
            quiet(render_still, j, str(out), 3.0)
            rec(f"{tag}: --still written -> {out}", out.is_file() and Image.open(out).size == (W, H), "")
    junk = [None, 5, "x", [], {"values": "abc"}, {"values": [None, "q", -3, float("nan")]}, {"rows": 5}, {"rows": ["|", {}, 7]}, {"text": None, "icon": None},
            {"text": "x" * 200, "icon": "banana"}, {"values": [1] * 40, "steps": "z", "glow": "q"}]
    errs = []
    for name in ("bar_chart", "progress_list", "badge"):
        for jk in junk:
            try:
                quiet(get_widget(name), jk, (50, 50, 600, 300), {"W": 1080, "H": 1920, "s": 1.0})
            except Exception as e:
                errs.append(f"{name}/{str(jk)[:20]}: {type(e).__name__}: {e}")
    rec("junk specs never raise (3 widgets x 11 junk values)", not errs, "; ".join(errs[:3]))
finally:
    shutil.rmtree(tmp, ignore_errors=True)
print(sum(rows), "pass,", len(rows) - sum(rows), "fail"); sys.exit(0 if all(rows) else 1)
